Actions `hrs_import`, `hrs_people`, `hrs_start`, `hrs_back`, `hrs_end`, `hrs_rater`, `hrs_remind`, `hrs_close`, `hrs_delete` (HR / CEO) and `hrs_answer` (the person or the rater). Pages and actions added to the HR, employee and assistant scopes.

**Tests (sandbox)**: import of a text file renamed .xlsx, of a workbook with five planted errors (all reported with sheet and row), by a user without HR rights (refused) and of the template; start without people refused; notifications to people and raters, also to the person who starts the campaign; partial save, submit with missing answers (marked), submit; another person's form and a rating by a non-rater refused; rating oneself refused; result closed to the person while the campaign runs and to other employees always; closing writes `hrs_scores` and the evaluation; people added while running; scheduled start and automatic closing by the sweep; delete of a closed campaign refused; browser run on desktop and phone width without script errors; page sweep and API test unchanged.

**Not tested**: real notifications by e-mail; a campaign with many people (group averages need five); the Greek / Italian screens were not read on screen; the HR mobile app does not show assessments.

**Security review**: every action checks the role on the server and carries the CSRF token; a form can be saved only by its owner (`user_id`) or its rater (`rater_user_id`); results only for HR / CEO and the person after closing (`hrs_may_see()`), each view by HR written to the person's change log; the upload is limited to .xlsx of 3 MB, refused above 300 parts or 40 MB unpacked, read and discarded; no export; no inline script without nonce. Assistant: nothing in `hrs.php` calls it, and `hr_eval_ai_context()` now removes the `as_` values of last year's evaluation before sending. Proposals (no effect on speed): legal review of the profiling aspect (GDPR art. 22 / 35, Italian contracts) before the first real campaign; a written retention decision for "no expiry"; two-step sign-in obligatory for HR and CEO accounts.

## §348 v1.155.1 (R97-064) — BOM template offered where a BOM is uploaded

**Request**: "give me the template of the BOM and also added to System Budgets and procurement in projects so they know the template of the BOM to upload".

**Before**: the template (`?p=bom_template`, `bom_import_template()`, R93-006) was offered only on a draft BOM revision (`views/bom.php`). The two other places where a BOM file is chosen had no link.

**Change**:

- `bom_template_sheets()` (new, `app/bom.php`) holds the sheets; `bom_import_template()` downloads them. Sheet BOM gains the column **Unit price** — the header `sb_bom_stage()` already reads as the cost; `bom_import_stage()` has no such column and ignores it. Sheet "How to" now states where to upload, what each column does, and the limit of 400 lines per file in a System Budget.
- Download buttons: `views/system_budgets.php` (page head, when the BOM import of System Budgets is active), `views/system_budget.php` (next to Parts › Import BOM parts), `views/_procurement_tab.php` (head of the BOM card for everybody who sees it, and inside New BOM).
- Language keys `bom.tpl_btn`, `bom.tpl_hint` (10 546 keys per file).

**Tests (sandbox)**: download as CEO and as project manager; without sign-in the login page is returned, not the file; the downloaded file uploaded unchanged into a System Budget (2 lines staged, the price 412.50 read as cost on line 1, empty on line 2) and into a draft BOM revision (2 lines staged); buttons present on the three pages.

**Security review**: no new entry point — the template page already required a signed-in staff account; the file is generated on request and contains only example lines, no data of the portal. No proposals from this change; the open ones of §347 stand.

## §349 v1.155.2 (R97-065) — a sub-project with a doubled code could not be saved

**Report** (screenshot, PRJ.000550 › Y.006, Edit sub-project): "he is trying to set the subproject complete but cannot" — the save answered "That sub-project code already exists in this project."

**Cause**: `subproject_update` (`app/pm_actions.php`) refused every save when another sub-project of the project had the same code, also when the code was not being changed. Two sub-projects that already share a code (ERP import, older entry — `subproject_save` had no such check) could therefore never be edited: no status, no order, no invoicing.

**Change**:

- `subproject_update`: the check runs only when the posted code differs from the stored one. The message names the sub-project that has the code.
- `subproject_save`: a new sub-project with a code already used in the project is refused (no new doubles through the form).
- `views/project_new.php` (Edit sub-project): a red note under Code when another sub-project of the project has the same code (`sp.dup_warn`, 10 547 keys per file).

**Tests (sandbox)**: a double made in the database; the sub-project saved as Completed with the code unchanged (refused before); the note shown; change to a used code refused with the name; new sub-project with a used code refused; double removed, note gone.

**Not done**: the live data was not looked at — which other sub-project of PRJ.000550 carries Y.006 is shown by the red note on the form.

**Security review**: no new entry point; the two actions keep their staff check and CSRF token; the name in the message is of a sub-project of the same project, which the user already sees. No new proposals.

## §350 v1.155.3 (R97-066) — Payables: why an invoice has no tick box

**Report** (screenshot, Payables › Supplier invoices, GAS-SI-26-0001, DEIF A/S, Draft, gross 0,00 EUR): "i cannot select the payable".

**Cause**: by design — `views/payables.php` prints the tick box only for status `checked` (`si_request` refuses anything else, `si.need_checked`). The invoice was a Draft without lines (gross 0,00); `si_check()` needs at least one line. The list gave no reason for the empty cell.

**Change** (display only): Draft and Disputed rows show the button "Check first" (`si.tick_check`) linking to the invoice; the hint under the list adds `si.tick_why`. 10 549 keys per file. No change to the rules.

**Tests (sandbox)**: a Draft and a Checked test invoice — button on the Draft, tick box on the Checked, hint shown; test rows removed.

**Security review**: display only, no new entry point, no new proposals.

## §351 v1.155.4 (R97-067) — Payables: the reason behind "Quantity mismatch"

**Report** (screenshot, supplier invoice GAS-SI-26-0001, two lines equal to the purchase order in quantity and price, PO open qty 0, both "Quantity mismatch"): "it gives qty mismatch which is not true".

**Finding**: the rule works as written — `si_match()` compares the invoiced quantity with `po_lines.qty_accepted − qty_invoiced` (received and not yet invoiced), not with the ordered quantity. PO open qty 0 means no goods receipt is recorded for the line, or the quantity was invoiced before. The label did not say which, so the result looked wrong.

**Change** (display only, `views/payable.php`): on an editable invoice, under the label of a line with a quantity mismatch: `si.why_nums` (ordered · received · invoiced before) and the reason `si.why_recv` (goods receipt missing, with a link to the purchase order), `si.why_inv` (already invoiced) or `si.why_over`. 10 554 keys per file. The rule is unchanged.

**Tests (sandbox)**: a test order with one line not received, one already invoiced and one received; the two explanations shown, none on the received line; test data removed.

**Not done**: the live order GAS-PO-26-0003 was not looked at — the new text on the invoice shows its received and invoiced quantities.

**Security review**: display only, no new entry point, no new proposals.

## §352 v1.155.5 (R97-068) — PO / requisition: Category searchable by typing

**Report**: "in the PO in the categories he cannot search by name as typing".

**Cause**: the Category drop-down of a new inventory item in the line form (`views/purchase_order.php`, `views/requisition.php`) carried `data-nosearch`, which switches off the type-to-filter list of `assets/portal.js` (R30-007). It was set with the item picker of R86-002; no script depends on it.

**Change**: the mark is removed in both views. No other change.

**Tests (sandbox)**: a test order with twelve test categories, in a browser: the list opens with the search box, typing "rel" leaves "Relays", Enter selects it, no script errors; test data removed.

**Security review**: display only; no new proposals.

## §353 v1.155.6 (R97-069) — purchase order: total with logistics

**Request**: "In POs the logistic costs should be added in the total so parts+logistics to be shown so we can pay all".

**Before**: the order showed Net and Gross of the parts; the logistic costs (`po_costs`, `po_orders.logistics_total`, R80-004) appeared only in their own table with "Landed cost (net + logistics)" and as small text in the delivery tile.

**Change** (display only):

- `views/purchase_order.php`: a fifth tile "Total with logistics" = `gross_total + logistics_total` with the two parts under it (orders only, not requests for quotation); a row "Total to pay (gross + logistics)" at the end of the Logistic costs table.
- `views/purchase_orders.php`: under the net of an order with logistic costs, "+ logistics · total".
- Keys `po.pay_total`, `po.pay_total_s`, `po.pay_total_none`, `po.pay_total_long` (10 558 per file).
- Not changed: the amounts stored, the PDF of the order (logistic costs are internal and often of another provider), Payables.

**Known limit**: `logistics_total` adds the amounts as typed; a cost entered in another currency than the order is added without conversion (as before, also in the landed cost).

**Tests (sandbox)**: a test order 16.950,00 net / 21.018,00 gross with freight 385,50: tile 21.403,50 with "gross 21.018,00 + logistics 385,50", landed cost 17.335,50, total to pay 21.403,50, list line shown; without costs the tile equals the gross and links to the costs table; test data removed.

**Security review**: display only; no new proposals.

## §354 v1.155.7 (R97-070) — Payables: why "Pull received PO lines" brings 0 lines

**Report**: "Pull PO lines is not working properly it brings 0 lines why".

**Finding**: by design — `si_lines_from_po()` pulls, per order line, `qty_accepted − qty_invoiced` and skips a line when that is 0 (nothing received, or everything invoiced) or when the line is already on the invoice. The answer was "0 lines pulled" in green, without a reason. Same root as §351: the order has no goods receipt.

**Change**: `si_pull_why()` (`app/si.php`) counts the order lines by reason; the action `si_pull` answers with a warning `si.pull_zero` (+ `si.pull_recv` when a receipt is missing) instead of the green message when nothing came. 10 560 keys per file. The rule is unchanged.

**Tests (sandbox)**: test order with two lines — nothing received: warning "2 without goods receipt"; after receipt: "2 lines pulled"; pulled again: warning "2 already on this invoice"; test data removed.

**Security review**: no new entry point (same action, same checks); no new proposals.

## §355 v1.156.0 (R97-071) — Payables: logistic costs of the order on the supplier invoice

**Request**: "also in the Supplier invoice it does not bring the logisitc costs" (after "In POs the logistic costs should be added in the total … so we can pay all").

**Before**: a supplier invoice was built from the order lines only; freight charged by the supplier had to be typed as a free line.

**Change**:

- Migration `r97y`: `si_lines.po_cost_id` (the logistic cost of the order the line carries).
- `app/si.php`: `si_lc_has()`, `si_lc_open($oid)` (costs not on a non-cancelled invoice), `si_lc_same()` (provider empty, or the supplier of the invoice — compared without spaces and punctuation), `si_costs_from_po()` (one line per cost: kind · PO no · ref · note, quantity 1, the amount), `si_lc_net()`, `si_lc_invoice()`.
- `si_pull` (and the tick at registration) pulls lines and costs; the message reports costs of another provider and costs in another currency, which are left out.
- `si_sp_cost_sync()`: the cost of the invoice on the sub-project is its net **without** the logistic lines (they are already there as kind Logistics from the order), and the reduction of the order commitment uses the same figure — no double counting.
- `po_cost_delete()` refuses while the cost is on an invoice. `views/purchase_order.php` shows the invoice next to the cost; `views/payable.php` marks the line "logistics".
- Keys `si.lc_*`, labels `si.pull_lines`, `si.pull_lines_btn` reworded (10 565 per file).

**Tests (sandbox)**: order with two received lines and four costs (no provider, provider DSV, provider = supplier, one in USD): pulled 2 lines + 2 costs, DSV named, USD reported; net 17.385,50; check → Matched; sub-project cost of the invoice 16.950,00 with the logistics rows unchanged; invoice shown on the two costs of the order; delete of a cost on the invoice refused; test data removed.

**Not tested**: an invoice with VAT (the VAT rate of the invoice applies to the logistic lines as to every line — import VAT typed as a logistic cost would get VAT on top; such a cost should be left for the broker's invoice or its line removed); the live data.

**Security review**: same action and checks as before (`require_si`, CSRF, the invoice must be editable and belong to the order); the new column is filled only from costs of the invoice's own order. No new proposals.

## §356 v1.156.1 (R97-072) — Payables: a logistic cost of another provider added by choice

**Report** (screenshot, GAS-SI-26-0002, DEIF A/S, PO GAS-PO-26-0004): "i have 2 logisitc costs it brought only 1 of them" — the second had Provider DHL and was left out by the rule of §355.

**Finding**: the Provider field of a logistic cost is used for the carrier also when the supplier re-invoices the freight. The automatic rule (provider empty or = supplier) is right as a default — it keeps a carrier's own invoice from being paid to the supplier — but the user must be able to overrule it.

**Change**: `views/payable.php` lists, on an editable invoice of an order, the logistic costs of the order that are on no invoice (`si_lc_open()`), with "Add to this invoice"; action `si_lc_add` → `si_costs_from_po($iid, $oid, $costId)` adds that one cost whatever its provider (the provider is written in the line text). The message of the pull points to the list. Keys `si.lc_open_title`, `si.lc_open_hint`, `si.lc_add`, `si.lc_other_cur`; `si.lc_other` reworded (10 569 per file).

**Tests (sandbox)**: order with a cost without provider and a cost of DHL: pull adds the first and lists the second; "Add to this invoice" adds it; adding it twice or a cost of another order is refused; test data removed.

**Security review**: the new action has the same checks as the pull (`require_si`, CSRF, editable invoice) and takes only a cost of the invoice's own order that is on no other invoice. No new proposals.

## §357 v1.156.2 (R97-073) — Projects › Doubled codes

**Report** (two screenshots, PRJ.000418 › Y.028 Navigation Lights Upgrade, M/Y AMBROSIA): opened from Projects the sub-project shows offer SQ-E000006, system "NAVIGATION LIGHTING SYSTEM", 0 % progress, fully invoiced; opened from Vessels it shows no offer, 100 % progress, not invoiced and the description "SoftOne status: to be invoiced · Created 25.02.2026".

**Reading**: the two screens are two different records with the same visible code — the second carries the description written by the SoftOne projects import, the first was worked on in the portal. Same family as §349 (Y.006 in PRJ.000550). Whether the double is at sub-project level (two Y.028 in one project) or at project level (two projects PRJ.000418) cannot be read from the screenshots; the live data was not inspected.

**Change** (read only): `views/data_doubles.php` (`?p=data_doubles`, management and administrators): sub-projects sharing a code in one project and projects sharing a project code, each record with id, name, system, description start, status, and "Linked records" — a count per table of every row that points to it (tables found through `information_schema` by the column `subproject_id` / `project_id`). Button on Projects when doubles exist (count kept 10 minutes); link from the red note of Edit sub-project. Keys `dd.*` (10 582 per file).

**Not done**: no merge. Merging moves the rows of about forty tables from one record to the other and cannot be undone by a button; it needs a decision per pair which record is kept.

**Tests (sandbox)**: a doubled sub-project and a doubled project made in the database: both listed with their linked records; the button on Projects; project manager refused (403); doubles removed, lists empty.

**Security review**: read only, management / administrators; table names for the counts come from `information_schema` of the portal's own database and are checked against `[a-z0-9_]` before use. No new proposals.

## §358 v1.156.3 (R97-074) — Service reports: filter by vessel, order; header sort in grouped tables

**Report** (screenshot, Service reports grouped by vessel): "shorting is not working in service reports i need to filter per vessel or ascending descending of the vessels".

**Cause**: `sortTable()` in `assets/portal.js` (R29) gives up when any row of the table has a cell with `colspan` — the group header rows of "Grouped by vessel" (`tr.vg-h`, R-vg) have one. So in every grouped table a click on a header did nothing. The page had no filter.

**Change**:

- `assets/portal.js`: in a table with group rows, the data rows are sorted inside each group and the groups are ordered by their first row; headers move with their rows. Tables without groups behave as before.
- `views/reports.php`: filter bar — Vessel (`fv`, a name from the lists) and Order (`fo`: newest / `old` / `vaz` / `vza`), applied to the three lists and kept in their pagers.
- `vg_order()` (`app/helpers.php`), used by `_fsr_list.php`, `_isr_list.php`, `_fc_list.php` instead of `vg_sort()`.
- Keys `r.order`, `r.o_*` (10 587 per file).

**Tests (sandbox, browser)**: reports on three vessels; header clicks Vessel ▲▼, Date ▲▼, Report no — groups reordered, each header followed by its rows, no script errors; Order Z → A; filter by one vessel; a vessel name that is not in the list is ignored.

**Security review**: `fv` is accepted only when it equals a vessel name already present in the user's own lists and is printed escaped; `fo` is one of three fixed values. No new proposals.

## §359 v1.156.4 (R97-075) — sub-project codes compared loosely

**Request**: "Step 1 do it" — after the question "is it possible to pass the same sub project in the system?" (answer: through the Excel import when the code is typed another way; no lock in the database).

**Change** (`app/pm.php`): `sp_code_norm()` — capitals, spaces removed, the Greek capitals Α Β Ε Ζ Η Ι Κ Μ Ν Ο Ρ Τ Υ Χ written as their Latin look-alikes; `sp_find_by_code($pid, $code, $exceptId)`; `sp_doubles()`.

- `subproject_save`: refused when another sub-project of the project has the code (loosely).
- `subproject_update`: checked when the code changes in the loose sense; correcting only the spelling of the own code (Υ.01 → Y.01) is allowed.
- Excel import (`proj_import`): exact match first, then the loose one — the existing sub-project is updated, no second record.
- `views/project_new.php` (red note), `views/projects.php` (button count), `views/data_doubles.php` (list, and a mark on a code with a non-ASCII character; `dd.greek`) use the loose comparison. 10 588 keys per file.
- Not included: leading zeros (Y.28 ≠ Y.028), and the lock in the database (step 2, after the existing doubles are merged or renamed).

**Tests (sandbox)**: `sp_code_norm` on seven spellings; new sub-project with Greek Υ and with small letter and spaces refused; edit to a used code in Greek refused; spelling change of the own code allowed; import of a file with `y.01` and `Υ.010` updated the two existing sub-projects and created none; a Greek-letter double listed and marked on the Doubled codes page and named in the red note; test data removed.

**Security review**: no new entry point; comparison in PHP on the sub-projects of one project. No new proposals.

## §360 v1.157.0 (R97-076) — merge of doubled sub-projects

**Request**: "Mergo tool yes do it" (after §357: Y.028 in PRJ.000418 exists twice; §349: Y.006 in PRJ.000550).

**Code**: `customer/app/spm.php`, preview and buttons in `views/data_doubles.php`, action `spm_merge`.

`spm_run($keep, $drop, $opt, $dry)`:

1. Allowed for management / administrators (`spm_can()`); the two records must be of the same project and have the same code in the loose sense (`sp_code_norm`).
2. Tables = every base table with a column `subproject_id` (`spm_tables()`, from `information_schema`); refused when one of them is not InnoDB (no all-or-nothing).
3. One transaction. Per table: `UPDATE IGNORE … SET subproject_id = keep WHERE subproject_id = drop`. Rows left behind collide with a unique key: in the link tables `SPM_DROP_DUP` (sp_team, sp_contacts, wf_item_subs, kpi_snapshots, sp_progress_snapshots, pm_milestones) they are deleted and kept in the restore file; in any other table the merge is **blocked**. `SPM_SINGLE` (sp_budgets): blocked when both records have a row.
4. Kept record: each `pct_*` = the higher of the two; the fields of `SPM_FILL` filled when empty; status of the removed record when chosen. Then the removed record is deleted.
5. Blocked or preview → rollback. Otherwise the restore file `storage/merge/sp_<drop>_into_<keep>_<time>.json` (both records, ids of every moved row per table, full rows of dropped duplicates) is written **before** the commit; when it cannot be written nothing is merged. `storage/merge/merge.log` gets one line. After the commit: `sp_reorder()`, caches reset.

The preview (`?p=data_doubles&keep=&drop=`) runs the same function with `$dry = true`, so its figures are those of the real merge.

**Not covered**: two *projects* with the same project code; references to a sub-project inside free text or stored settings; files stay in their folder on disk (they are found by their stored name, so they open normally).

**Tests (sandbox, database backed up and restored)**: a Greek-letter double of a sub-project with 16 files, proformas, service reports: preview leaves the database unchanged; merge moves the rows, deletes the double, renumbers; with a team member and two progress snapshots on both → dropped and listed in the file; with a BOM revision of the same number and a budget on both → blocked, nothing changed; after clearing → merged, progress 10 → 80, system filled, status of the removed record taken when chosen; without the tick refused; two sub-projects with different codes refused; project manager refused (action and preview).

**Not tested**: the live data; a table of the live database that the sandbox does not have (it is handled by the same generic rule: moved, or blocked when it collides).

**Security review**: action and preview only for management / administrators, CSRF token, confirmation tick checked on the server; table names from `information_schema` checked against `[a-z0-9_]`; column names of the kept record come from its own row and are checked the same way; the restore file is under `storage/`, closed to the web by its `.htaccess` (R97-005) and not in the repository. Proposal: include `storage/merge` in the server backup you keep outside the hosting.

## §361 v1.157.1 (R97-077) — Service reports: three tabs, filters, needs action

**Request**: "In Service Reports page please separate the page in 3 tabs Field Service Reports, Inspection reports and Function Check / Commissioning as the 3 blocks on the page with filters also for vessel, project, word, Status so they can find easier" and "They need to be able to see what needs processing also as action".

**Change** (`views/reports.php`; no change to the data):

- Tabs `?p=reports[&tab=isr|fc]` with the number of reports and a flag with the number that need action. Only the list of the open tab is drawn.
- Filter bar on the open tab: `q` (report no, project code / name, sub-project, vessel, subject, engineer, form), `fv` vessel, `fp` project, `fs` status, `fa` needs action, `fo` order. Options come from the rows of the tab; a value that is not among them is ignored. Filters are kept in the pager (`$keep`).
- Needs action (closure `$act`): staff — field service report / function check: draft → finish and submit, submitted → customer acceptance, disputed → correct and resubmit, external report not processed → process; inspection report: draft → finish, checking → check and issue. Customer — submitted → own acceptance. The text is put on the row as `_action` and printed under the status by `_fsr_list.php`, `_isr_list.php`, `_fc_list.php` (other pages that use these tables pass no `_action` and are unchanged).
- Older documents stay at the bottom of the first tab. Keys `r.word`, `r.show*`, `r.act_*`, `r.a_*` (10 630 per file).

**Tests (sandbox)**: the three tabs, an unknown tab (falls back to the first), search with and without hits, status, needs action alone and combined; counts on the tabs; screenshot read; no script errors. The sandbox has no inspection or commissioning reports, so those two lists were tested empty.

**Security review**: filters are compared in PHP against the user's own rows (the lists are already limited by the scope of the account); every value is printed escaped; no new query from user input. No new proposals.

## §362 v1.157.2 (R97-078) — Service reports: the dropped query of "Older documents"

**Report** (screenshot, System health › PHP errors): `PHP Warning: Undefined variable $docs in /views/reports.php on line 84`, new since 1.157.1, 28 times in a quarter of an hour.

**Cause**: in §361 the block of `views/reports.php` that prepares the lists was replaced as a whole and the query of the older documents (`$docs`), which stood inside that block, went with it. Effect: a warning on every opening of the page and the list "Older documents" not shown. No data affected.

**Change**: the query is back.

**Why the tests of §361 missed it**: the sandbox does not print warnings on the page, it writes them to `storage/php-errors.log` — and the log was not read. It had the warning eighteen times. **Rule from now on**: after the page tests of a release the lines added to `storage/php-errors.log` are read; a release goes out only when there are none.

**Tests (sandbox)**: the pages changed today opened as CEO, project manager and HR; no line added to the log.

**Security review**: none of it touches access or data; no new proposals.

## §363 v1.158.0 (R97-079) — speed: the schema kept between requests

**Request** (screenshot, System health › Slow statements, last 7 days, 15 groups): "can you optimize the slowest statements".

**Reading of the list**: the totals are small (the top line 8,2 s in a week). Most lines are simple statements by primary key that were slow once or twice (`SELECT * FROM subprojects WHERE id = ?` 190 ms, `SELECT k, v FROM cp_settings` 142 ms, the update of an offer by id) — that is waiting time of the shared database, not the statement. What can be improved is how many statements a page sends:

- `SHOW TABLES LIKE '?'` (63 slow in a week) and `SHOW COLUMNS … LIKE '?'`: the `*_has()` checks of every module — about forty per page, each one prepare + execute.
- the totals of leave requests with `YEAR(date_from) = ?` (718 ms): the function on the column keeps the index from being used.
- writes on every request: `customer_users.last_seen_at`, `mob_devices.last_seen_at`.

**Change**:

- `app/db.php`: `db_schema()` — the list of tables (`SHOW TABLES`) and, per table on first use, of columns (`SHOW COLUMNS FROM t`), kept five minutes (`cache_set('db_schema')`, per release). `scalar()` and `one()` answer `SHOW TABLES LIKE 'x'` / `SHOW COLUMNS FROM t LIKE 'c'` from it through `db_schema_answer()` — **only a yes**; on a no the real statement runs, so a new table or column is seen at once. 52 checks written as `db()->query("SHOW …")->fetch()` were changed to `scalar("SHOW …")` so they pass through it. `install/update.php` drops the kept schema after the migrations (`db_schema_forget()`).
- `db()`: the setting `tz_db` read at every connection is kept one minute (`cache 'tz_db'`, dropped when the setting is saved).
- `app/hr2.php` `hr_req_sum()`: `date_from >= 'Y-01-01' AND date_from < 'Y+1-01-01'`.
- `presence_touch()`: at most every 120 s (was 60; online = seen in the last five minutes). `api/v1/lib/jwt.php`: the device row is written at most every five minutes or when the app version changes.
- `views/data_doubles.php`: the table list from `information_schema` kept ten minutes.

**Measured (sandbox, statements per page, before → after)**: dashboard 107 → 69, projects 263 → 218, sub-project 122 → 73, service reports 55 → 17, tickets 50 → 14, payables 54 → 17, purchase orders 54 → 17, system budgets 56 → 17, collections 77 → 37, proformas 54 → 15, HR dashboard 79 → 33, person 72 → 26, timesheets 53 → 17, offers 56 → 18, vessels 50 → 13, my leave 82 → 37.

**Tests (sandbox)**: yes / no answers for tables and columns; a column and a table added after the schema was kept are seen in the same request; page sweeps as CEO, project manager and HR; API test; a browser run; no line added to `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Not done**: the Projects page still sends about 200 statements — eight budget-and-actual statements per sub-project of the page; a candidate for the next round. The slow lines that are single statements by key cannot be improved in the portal.

**Security review**: the kept schema holds table and column names only, in the portal's cache (APCu or `storage/cache`, closed to the web); the statements answered from it are matched by a strict pattern with literal names; nothing from user input reaches it. No new proposals.

## §364 v1.159.0 (R97-080) — IT & Cybersecurity menu group and the ISO 27001 module

**Request**: "IT and Cybersecurity should be disengaged from the Library menu and create a new module containing the Remote Support Contracts as one of the submenu and then all the reporting and filing required for ISO 27001".

**Menu**: new group **IT & Cybersecurity** (before Library): Remote support contracts (page `cyber`, unchanged, also for customers), ISO 27001 — ISMS (`isms`, for those with access), Report a security event (`isms_report`, all staff). The entry is removed from Library.

**Access**: `ism_can()` = administrators, management (`mgmt_can()`) and the ISMS team (setting `ism_team`, chosen on the overview by management / administrators). Deleting a record and choosing the team: management / administrators only (`ism_manage()`). Reporting an event: every staff account, including the restricted HR / planning / employee accounts (page and action added to their lists). Customers: none of it.

**Registers** (`ism_regs()` in `app/ism.php` — one definition per register: clause, code prefix, statuses, which of them are closed, fields):

| Register | Clause | Code | What it files |
|---|---|---|---|
| Documents | 7.5 | ISMS- | policies, procedures, records: version, approval, next review |
| Risks | 6.1.2 · 8.2 · 8.3 | RSK- | asset, threat, vulnerability, likelihood × impact, treatment, plan, residual score |
| Statement of Applicability | 6.1.3 d | A x.y | the 93 Annex A controls: applicable or not, justification, state, how / evidence |
| Assets | A 5.9 | AST- | type, classification, location, criticality |
| Incidents | A 5.24 – 5.28 | INC- | what happened, severity, containment, cause, lessons, who was notified |
| Audits & reviews | 9.2 · 9.3 | AUD- | internal audit, management review, access review, restore test, continuity exercise |
| Nonconformities | 10.2 | NC- | source, cause, correction, corrective action, check of effectiveness |
| Objectives | 6.2 | OBJ- | measure, target, result |
| Suppliers | A 5.19 – 5.22 | SUP- | service, access to our information, agreement, last review |
| Training & awareness | 7.2 · 7.3 | TRN- | audience, attendees, trainer |
| Legal requirements | A 5.31 | LEG- | obligation and how it is met |

Every record: code (proposed, unique per register), title, status, owner, due date, next review, reference, the fields of its register, files, history (`ism_events`: created, changed fields, status from → to, file attached / removed, with name and time).

**Pre-filled once** (`ism_seed()`, setting `ism_seeded`): 32 items of documented information of ISO/IEC 27001:2022 as drafts and the 93 controls of Annex A (5.1–5.37, 6.1–6.8, 7.1–7.14, 8.1–8.34) as "Planned". Titles only — the content of the policies is written by the company and attached.

**Files**: `storage/isms`, random stored name, original name kept in the database, SHA-256 and size recorded, 25 MB, extensions from a fixed list (pdf, Office, images, txt, csv, eml, msg, zip); served only through `?p=isms_rec&id=…&file=…` to those with access, as attachment with `nosniff`.

**Overview**: documents approved, controls implemented (of the applicable), open risks by level, open incidents, open nonconformities; table of registers (records, open, overdue); Needs attention (due date passed or review date reached); planned audits and reviews; the ISMS team.

**Report a security event**: title, when, what is affected, what happened → an incident with status Reported; the team and the administrators are notified (`ism:incident`).

**Reminders**: `ism_sweep()` hourly in the background sweep — due date or review date within 14 days: one notification per record and date to the owner, or to the team when there is no owner (`ism:due`).

**Export**: All registers (Excel) — a Summary sheet and one sheet per register with every field and the number of files.

**Database**: `install/migrate_cp_r97_080_isms.sql` — `ism_records`, `ism_files`, `ism_events` (step `r97z`).

**Tests (sandbox)**: menu for CEO / PM / restricted account; 32 + 93 records seeded; create, edit (history with changed fields and status), doubled code refused; PM outside the team refused on pages (403), on save and on file download; PM in the team sees and edits, cannot delete, does not see the team form; upload of pdf accepted, `.php` and `.pdf.php` refused; download headers; file and record delete remove the stored files; event report by a PM filed as INC-001 and notified; empty report refused; reminder sent by the sweep; workbook opened (12 sheets); page sweeps for three roles, API test, browser pages without console errors; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Not tested / not done**: e-mail of notifications (the sandbox sends no mail); no PDF report — the workbook is the report; no link yet between a risk and the controls that treat it other than the reference text; the contents of the policies are not written by the portal.

**Security review**: the module holds the company's most sensitive security information — pages, actions and downloads all check `ism_can()` on the server, not only the menu; nothing of it is in the assistant's context; all posts carry the CSRF token, no inline scripts; uploads are never executed (random name, fixed extension list, folder closed to the web by `storage/.htaccess`). Proposals, no cost in speed: (1) include `storage/isms` in the off-hosting backup; (2) keep the ISMS team small and review it at every access review (register Audits & reviews); (3) the four secrets shown in the earlier screenshot of `config.php` should be rotated and the rotation filed as an incident record with its closing date.

## §365 v1.160.0 (R97-081) — Marine cyber safety (IACS UR E26 / E27): products, documentation, software life cycle

**Request**: "We need to implement also in the same module a menu for Marine Cyber safety UR E26/27 products that we produce as part of our projects and their corresponding documentation and monitoring process of the approval and lifecycle management of the software from our Programming team. All of those should be also linked to the corresponding projects and vessels accordingly."

**Menu**: IT & Cybersecurity › **Marine cyber safety (E26 / E27)** (page `mcs`; `views/mcs.php` sets the area and uses `views/isms.php`; overview in `views/_mcs_overview.php`; a record is the same page `isms_rec`).

**Access**: `ism_can_marine()` = those of the ISMS (administrators, management, ISMS team) **plus the programming team** (setting `ism_mteam`, chosen on the overview by management / administrators). The programming team has this area only: pages, saves, files and exports of the ISO 27001 registers answer 403 / no access (`ism_can_reg()` decides per register). Delete: management / administrators.

**Three registers** (`'grp' => 'marine'` in `ism_regs()`):

| Register | Code | Statuses | Fields |
|---|---|---|---|
| Products (`mprod`) | CBS-001 | in preparation → submitted to class → class comments → approved → in service → retired | system / function, hardware, software platform, system category (UR E22 I / II / III), security zone, interface to an untrusted network, classification society, approval route (plan approval per vessel / type approval), class reference, certificate no., certificate valid until, description, notes · **vessel, project, sub-project** |
| UR E27 documentation (`mdoc`) | CBS-001-D01 … D10 | missing → draft → internal review → submitted to class → class comments → approved · not applicable | document no., revision, submitted on, approved on, comments of class, notes · belongs to a product |
| Software releases (`msw`) | SW-001 | development → test → review → approved → released → installed on board → superseded · withdrawn | version (required), component, kind of change, what changed and why, change request, repository tag / archive, SHA-256 of the package, tested by, test report, notification of class, installed on / by, rollback plan, notes · approved by / on (automatic) · belongs to a product |

**The ten documents** created with every new product (UR E27 Rev.1, 3.1.1 – 3.1.10): CBS asset inventory · topology diagrams · description of security capabilities · test procedure of security capabilities · security configuration guidelines · secure development lifecycle documents · plans for maintenance and verification of the CBS · information supporting the owner's incident response and recovery plan · management of change plan · test reports. Further documents (E26 inputs to the yard, type approval certificate …) are added with + on the product.

**Links** (migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_081_marine_cyber.sql`, step `r98a`: `ism_records.parent_id`, `project_id`, `subproject_id`, `vessel_id`): a product carries the three links (a sub-project fills its project, a project its vessel; a sub-project of another project is refused); documents and releases take them from their product (`ism_link_sql()`). Lists filter by product, vessel and project. `ism_marine_card()` shows the products on the **vessel page** and on the **first tab of the sub-project page** (sub-project's own products and those of the project without a sub-project), with + New product already linked — only for those with access. The merge of doubled sub-projects (§360) moves these links too (column `subproject_id`).

**Software approval rules** (in `ism_save_from_post()`): status Approved sets approved by = the signed-in user and the date; the developer (owner of the record) cannot approve his own release unless management / administrator; Released and Installed are refused without an approval; a step back before Approved removes the approval; Installed sets the installation date when empty. Notifications (`ism:release`): Review → programming team, ISMS team, administrators; Approved / Released / Installed / Withdrawn → the developer. History keeps every status step with the name.

**Overview**: products approved, UR E27 documents approved, releases to approve, in work, to install on board; table per product (vessel, project, class, status, documents n / 10, software version on board, releases in work); releases waiting for approval; approved and not installed; certificates expiring within 90 days; programming team; how it works. **Excel**: Products · UR E27 documentation · Software releases.

**Tests (sandbox)**: programming-team member: marine pages 200, ISO pages / save / export 403; product from a sub-project only → project and vessel filled; ten documents created; wrong project / sub-project pair dropped; document without product refused; release without version refused; development → released refused; developer approving his own refused; CEO approves (name and date stored); installed (date stored); back to test (approval removed); review notification to administrators; cards on vessel and sub-project pages (not for the restricted account); filters; workbook (3 sheets); product delete by a programmer refused, by the CEO removes its documents, releases and history; page sweeps for three roles, API test, browser pages without console errors; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Not done**: the 41 security capabilities of UR E27 section 4 as a per-product checklist (today they are the content of document 3.1.3); UR E26 ship-level deliverables of the systems integrator (zones and conduits diagram, vessel asset inventory, cyber resilience test procedure) as their own list — they can be added as extra documents of a product; no connection to a code repository — the tag and the checksum are typed.

**Security review**: source code and packages are not stored in the portal — only their reference and checksum, so a copy can be verified before installation on board; the four-eyes rule on releases is enforced on the server; the programming team cannot reach the ISO 27001 registers (checked per register on pages, actions, downloads and exports); links are validated against the database. Proposals, no cost in speed: (1) record the SHA-256 of every released package and check it on board before installation; (2) name at least two people who may approve releases, so the rule never blocks a delivery; (3) keep the release packages in one access-controlled archive and write its location in the release.

## §366 v1.160.1 (R97-082) — IT & Cybersecurity: access by position

**Request**: "Access to IT and Cybersecurity should have Automation Engineers, ITs, Electrical Engineers, PMs and top management including QHSE".

**Change**: `ism_can()` is also true when the staff role or the discipline of the account is in the setting `ism_access` (`{roles: [ids], disc: [ids]}`) — `ism_access()`, `ism_by_position()` in `app/ism.php`. Card **Access by position** on ISO 27001 — ISMS › Overview (management / administrators; action `ism_access_save`): every active staff role and discipline with the number of active accounts.

**Before the first save** the names decide (`ISM_ROLE_RX`, `ISM_DISC_RX`): roles — CEO, Owner, General Manager, Managing Director, Director, Technical Manager, QHSE / HSE / Quality, Project Manager, IT / ICT, Automation, Electrical, Programmer / Software (and the Greek stems); disciplines — Automation, Electrical, IT / Software / Programming, Project Management, QHSE / Quality, Management. The card says that the ticks come from the names. **Role and discipline names on the live server are not visible from here — the ticks must be checked once and saved.**

**Scope**: the whole group — ISO 27001 registers (risks, incidents, audits …) and Marine cyber safety. The ISMS team (everything) and the programming team (marine only) stay for single people. Delete, teams and this card: management / administrators. Notifications of reported events still go to the ISMS team and the administrators, not to everybody with access. The restricted HR / planning / employee accounts keep only Report a security event.

**Tests (sandbox)**: engineer without discipline and outside the teams — 403 on pages and export; with discipline Automation — access by default; a non-manager cannot save the card; saved choice replaces the defaults (unknown ids dropped); defaults ticked for CEO, Owner, Technical Manager, Project Manager and the disciplines Electrical, Automation, Project Management; page sweeps for three roles; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Security review**: this widens who can read the risk register, the incidents and the audit findings from a named team to whole positions — it is the access the request asks for; the choice is made by management only, checked on the server at every page, action, download and export. Proposals, no cost in speed: (1) after saving, look at the numbers next to each role and discipline — they are the people who now read the registers; (2) file an "access review" in Audits & reviews every six months for this card; (3) if incidents should be narrower than the rest, say so and the Incidents register can be kept to management, QHSE and the ISMS team.

## §367 v1.161.0 (R97-083) — ISO 27001: suppliers from the supplier list and their evaluation; training with HR; who sets access by position

**Requests**: "Suppliers have to be integrated with our supplier list and an evaluation process to be implemented in 27001. Also Training of the 27001 should be integrated with the HR training process to be kept also in the personnel records." · "Access by position should be only available to CEO and general manager for the ISO 27001".

**Database**: `install/migrate_cp_r97_083_isms_suppliers_training.sql` (step `r98b`) — `ism_records.supplier_id` (→ `companies.id`, the supplier list), `ism_records.hr_training_id` (→ `hr_trainings.id`).

**Suppliers** (register `supplier`): field *Supplier of the supplier list* (`ism_supplier_options()` — companies that are suppliers); one record per supplier (`ism.e_sup_twice`); the name is the title when none is typed. Statuses: To evaluate → Active · Approved with conditions · Not approved · Ended.

**Evaluation** (new register `supeval`, code SEV-, child of a supplier record): kind (initial / periodic / after an incident), planned date, evaluated on, evaluator, six criteria 1–5 (`ISM_EVAL_CRIT`: agreement, certification and evidence, access control, incidents and reporting, continuity, quality and response), result, conditions, notes, files. Rating = sum / 30 in percent. Done needs all six scores; date and evaluator are filled when empty; the result is proposed from the rating (≥ 70 approved, 50–69 with conditions, < 50 not approved) when none is chosen. `ism_eval_apply()`: the latest finished evaluation writes rating, result and last review to the supplier, sets its status, and dates the next evaluation (`review_date`) 12 months later when criticality ≥ 4 or access to confidential / personal information, otherwise 24. The reminder of the register (14 days before the review date) is the reminder of the evaluation. The supplier record lists its evaluations; `ism_supplier_card()` shows state, rating and next evaluation on Procurement › Suppliers (people of the ISMS only), or a button that opens a new register record with the supplier chosen.

**Training** (register `training`): card *HR › Training and personnel records* on the record — `ism_tr_create()` creates the training in `hr_trainings` (internal, provider "Golden A/S — ISO 27001", date = planned date, offered to all, closed for applications) or `ism_tr_link()` links one HR already published; `ism_tr_attend()`: the ticked people become *attended* in `hr_training_members` (those un-ticked who were attended become absent), the number of attendees of the ISO record follows, and *Set to Done* closes the ISO record and the HR training. The attendance therefore shows on the Training tab of each personnel record and in the HR evaluation that counts trainings; certificates and scores stay with HR. The HR training page shows the ISO record it belongs to (`ism_tr_badge()`).

**Access by position**: the card and its action `ism_access_save` — `ism_top()`: staff role CEO or General Manager only (administrators and the Owner scope no longer).

**Tests (sandbox)**: supplier chosen from the list without a title; the same supplier twice refused; evaluation Done with five scores refused; with six: rating 60 %, result proposed, supplier → Approved with conditions, next evaluation in 12 months (criticality 4), history line; card on the supplier page; training created in HR, two people ticked, then one: attended / absent in HR, attendees 1, both Done; visible on the HR training page and on the person's Training tab; unknown person id ignored; access card hidden and save refused for an administrator whose role is not CEO / General Manager; page sweeps for three roles, API test; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Not done**: no bulk adding of the whole supplier list — the register is for suppliers that handle our information or systems; the rating is not written to the procurement rating of the supplier; a supplier that is Not approved is not blocked in purchase orders (offered).

**Security review**: the names of employees shown for attendance are names only — no other personnel data leaves HR; attendance writes are limited to active people and to trainings linked to the record; supplier ids are checked against the supplier list. Proposals, no cost in speed: (1) warn on a purchase order when its supplier is Not approved or its evaluation is overdue; (2) ask critical suppliers for their ISO 27001 certificate yearly and attach it to the evaluation; (3) make the yearly awareness training a standing record so the reminder comes by itself.

## §368 v1.162.0 (R97-084) — purchases of the IT department

**Request**: "IT departments should be able to place a requisition and a PO to the system through the procurement process. Projects available to place also IT POs should be defined in the corresponding Sub project and only those will be listed to ITs for RFQ and PO".

**Database**: `install/migrate_cp_r97_084_it_purchases.sql` (step `r98c`) — `subprojects.it_purchase`.

**Where it is set**: Edit sub-project › *Open for IT purchases* (`subproject_update`). Settings › Procurement › *IT department purchases*: the staff roles and disciplines that are IT (setting `it_buy_pos`; until saved, the names IT / ICT / Information Technology decide — `IT_BUY_RX`), and the list of the opened sub-projects.

**Rules** (`app/prq.php`, `app/po.php`):

- `it_buyer()` — a staff account in an IT role or discipline **without** the procurement or warehouse right (those keep what they have).
- Requisition: `prq_can()` is true; `prq_save_from_post()` asks for a sub-project with `it_purchase = 1` (unless the person is the PM of the project). Approval is unchanged: procurement (`prq_decide`, not open to IT), over budget management.
- RFQ / PO: `po_can()` and `po_admin()` are true for IT, inside the scope only — `po_load()` returns nothing for an order whose sub-project is not opened (so every action on it answers "not found / no access"); `po_list()` and `po_to_buy()` list the opened sub-projects only; `po_save_from_post()` asks for an opened sub-project; `po_line_from_pr()` takes lines of requisitions of opened sub-projects only; a new free line is refused (`it.e_free_line`) — lines of an IT order come from approved requisitions; existing lines can be priced and edited.
- Forms: the project and sub-project lists of a new requisition, RFQ and PO show the opened sub-projects (and their projects) only. Menu: Procurement › Requisitions, Purchase orders.
- Prices are visible to IT on what they can open (`stk_price_visible()`).
- Not opened to IT: receiving goods (warehouse), suppliers, inventory, payables, procurement settings.

**Tests (sandbox)**: flag saved from the edit form; IT sees only the opened sub-project in the lists; requisition on another sub-project and without sub-project refused, on the opened one saved, line added, submitted; IT approving it refused (403); approved by procurement and management; line appears in To buy for IT; RFQ created by IT on the opened sub-project, line pulled, price typed; PO on another sub-project and without sub-project refused; free line refused; an order of another sub-project: not in the IT lists, opening it "Not found", cancelling it has no effect; a to-buy line of another sub-project is not listed and cannot be pulled; after the flag is removed the IT order is no longer reachable by IT; suppliers, inventory, payables and settings 403; page sweeps for three roles, API test; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Not done**: the numbers on the Purchase orders tabs count all orders, not only those of IT; no separate IT budget — the parts budget of the sub-project applies.

**Security review**: the scope is enforced where an order is loaded, so it holds for every present and future action on orders; IT cannot approve its own requisition and cannot order what was not approved; removing the flag closes the access at once. Proposals, no cost in speed: (1) keep IT purchases in a dedicated sub-project per year with its own parts budget, so approvals compare against a real figure; (2) leave receiving with the warehouse — the person who orders should not be the one who confirms receipt; (3) review the ticks of the IT card together with the access review of §366.

## §369 v1.162.1 (R97-085) — IT & Cybersecurity handbook of its own; automatic numbers filled in

**Requests**: "the help files for IT and Cybersecurity should be different in the support section" · "All records in the IT and Cybersecurity should also have automatic numbering by default and the user to change if wanted."

**Handbook**: new edition `customer/views/_manual_it.php` — *Customer Portal — IT & Cybersecurity Handbook* (`?p=manual&for=it`, all staff): where things are and who sees them · remote support contracts · reporting a security event · ISO 27001 overview and registers · suppliers and their evaluation · training kept in the personnel records · marine cyber safety (UR E26 / E27) · purchases of the IT department · the routine through the year. Card on Support, button on the manual page, included in the help search (`mn_editions()`). The five entries of v1.159.0 – v1.162.0 are taken out of the Staff and Project Manager handbooks and replaced by one line that points to the new handbook.

**Rule from now on**: a change in the menu group IT & Cybersecurity (and in the purchases of the IT department) is described in `_manual_it.php`, in the section it belongs to — not as a dated line in the Staff / PM handbooks. The other help files (this guide, CHANGELOG, What's new) stay as they are.

**Numbers**: a new record opens with its number in the Code field (`ism_next_code($reg, $parentId)`), with the hint "automatic — change it if you want". Register records: prefix-number (ISMS-033, RSK-001, INC-002, SUP-002, CBS-003 …). Records under a product or a supplier (`ISM_CHILD_SFX`): `<product>-Dnn` documents, `<product>-SWnn` software releases, `<supplier>-Enn` evaluations. When the number was not changed by hand it is taken again at save — so two people who open the form together do not collide, and a release moved to another product in the form gets that product's number; a typed number is kept and refused only when it is already used in the register. Remote support contracts already worked this way (GAS-RS-year-number). Existing records keep their numbers.

**Tests (sandbox)**: number shown on new forms of six registers; two products saved from forms opened with the same proposed number → CBS-003 and CBS-004; release opened under one product and saved under another → number of the second; typed number kept; the same typed number twice refused; evaluation SUP-001-E01; handbook page, Support card and search; Staff and PM handbooks without the old entries; page sweeps for three roles; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Security review**: the handbook describes procedures only and holds no record data; it is open to all staff because everybody must know how to report an event. Numbers are checked for uniqueness on the server. No new proposals in this revision.

## §370 v1.163.0 (R97-086) — QHSE: ISO 9001 · 14001 · 45001 · 22301, calibration, no financial data for the QHSE role

**Request**: "Also we need to integrate QHSE module for our quality department ISO 9001, ISO 14001, ISO 45001, ISO 22301 which our company is certified for. In the quality ISO9001 system we need also to add equipment calibrations and the process of calibrations and validity of the certificates. QHSE Manager should not have access to Financial Data of the company".

**Menu**: group **QHSE** › Management systems (ISO) — page `qhse` (`views/qhse.php` sets the area and uses `views/isms.php`; overview `views/_qhse_overview.php`; a record is the page `isms_rec`). Five tabs: Integrated system, ISO 9001, ISO 14001, ISO 45001, ISO 22301 (`QH_STD`). No database step — the registers use the tables of the ISMS module (`ism_records`, `ism_files`, `ism_events`).

**Registers** (`'grp' => 'qhse'`, `'std'` = the tab):

| Tab | Register | Clause | Code |
|---|---|---|---|
| Integrated system | Documents | 7.5 | QD- |
| | Risks & opportunities | 6.1 | QR- |
| | Objectives | 6.2 | QO- |
| | Audits & reviews | 9.2 · 9.3 | QA- |
| | Nonconformities | 10.2 | QN- |
| | Legal & other requirements | 6.1.3 · 9.1.2 | QL- |
| | Training & competence (HR link) | 7.2 · 7.3 | QT- |
| | Interested parties | 4.1 · 4.2 | QP- |
| ISO 9001 | Customer complaints & feedback | 9.1.2 · 8.2.1 | QC- |
| | Measuring equipment | 7.1.5 | EQ- |
| | Calibrations | 7.1.5.2 | EQ-nnn-Cnn |
| ISO 14001 | Environmental aspects | 6.1.2 | EA- |
| | Waste records | 8.1 · 9.1 | WR- |
| ISO 45001 | Hazards & risk assessment | 6.1.2 | HZ- |
| | Incidents & near misses | 10.2 | HS- |
| ISO 22301 | Business impact analysis | 8.2.2 | BI- |
| | Continuity plans | 8.3 · 8.4 | BC- |
| | Exercises & tests | 8.5 | BX- |

The common registers carry the field Standard (All four / one of them) and the list filters by it. Nonconformities, complaints, waste, hazards and incidents can be linked to vessel, project and sub-project. Registers with likelihood and impact show the score (flag `score`). Training uses the HR link of §367 (flag `hrtr`).

**Calibration**: an instrument (`qequip`) has kind, maker and model, serial number, range, location, holder, calibration required, interval in months. Its calibrations (`qcal`) go Planned → At the laboratory → Done / Failed. Done needs date, certificate number and result; the result Out of tolerance makes it Failed. `ism_cal_apply()`: the latest finished calibration writes last calibration, certificate, laboratory and result to the instrument and sets the date the certificate is valid until (`review_date`) — the date on the certificate, else calibration date + interval; Failed → instrument Out of service, the next good one → In use. `ism_cal_state()`: valid · due soon (30 days) · expired · never calibrated · out of service · not required — on the list, the record and the ISO 9001 overview (instruments calibrated, due in 30 days, Calibration to arrange). Reminder to the holder 30 days before expiry (`'remind' => 30`).

**Access**: `qh_can()` — management, administrators, the QHSE team (`qh_team`, set by management) and the staff roles / disciplines of `qh_access` (card Access by position on the first tab, CEO and General Manager only; by the names QHSE / HSE / Quality until saved). Delete: management / administrators. Export: per tab and All standards (`qh_export()`).

**No financial data for the QHSE role**: `fin_blocked()` (app/pm.php) is true for a staff role named QHSE, HSE, EHS or Quality. Then: `fin_visible()` is false (amounts masked everywhere it is used) — before the administrator shortcut; the rights `is_accounting`, `can_accounting`, `can_sales`, `can_cashflow` count as not ticked (`col_flag()`, `acc_user_flag()`, `pi_is_sales()`); `col_can()`, `sb_can()`, `acc_can_prepare()` and `stk_price_visible()` are false; and the router closes the pages of `FIN_PAGES` (pipeline, sales reports, offers, System Budgets, collections, statement, accounting, proforma invoices, direct invoice, payables, cash flow, cost control, financial report) with the message "Financial pages are not available for your role".

**Help**: new edition `views/_manual_qhse.php` — QHSE Handbook (card on Support, in the help search). From now on changes of the group QHSE are written there. One pointer line in the Staff and PM handbooks.

**Tests (sandbox)**: five tabs and eighteen registers open, new-record forms of all registers; an engineer and a restricted account refused; instrument and three calibrations — Done without certificate refused; first calibration → valid until date + 12 months; a newer one Out of tolerance → Failed and the instrument Out of service; a newer good one with a date on the certificate → In use and that date; history lines; numbers EQ-001-C01…; standard filter; hazard linked through a sub-project to project and vessel; training created in HR; workbook of all standards (19 sheets); a test account with every financial right ticked: before — collections, cash flow, payables, System Budgets, offers open; with the role QHSE Manager — all redirected, no amounts on dashboard, projects, sub-project; the CEO unchanged; page sweeps for three roles (one of them as QHSE Manager), API test, browser pages without console errors; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Not done / to check**: the registers start empty — existing documents, instruments and certificates are entered or imported by the quality department (an Excel import of the instrument list can be added); supplier evaluation for ISO 9001 (8.4) is not a separate register — the evaluation of §367 covers information security only; the certificates of the company itself (four certificates with their surveillance dates) are kept as documents; the phone apps were not checked for amounts shown to the QHSE role; staff outside QHSE cannot read the documents here (a read-only library of approved documents can be added).

**Security review**: the financial block is by role name and is enforced in the functions and in the router, so it does not depend on ticks; incident records can hold names of injured people — the handbook asks for the minimum and they are not sent to the assistant; access is checked on the server per register. Proposals, no cost in speed: (1) check on the live portal that the role of the QHSE Manager is named QHSE / HSE / Quality — otherwise the block and the default access do not apply (the role switch "Financial data visible" remains as a second lock); (2) if the QHSE Manager uses the phone apps, confirm that no amounts appear there; (3) file the six-monthly access review for this group together with those of §366 and §368.

## §371 v1.163.1 (R97-087) — QHSE team and Access by position could not be saved

**Report**: "I tried to save the QHSE team but it does nothing".

**Cause**: the two QHSE actions are named `qh_team_save` and `qh_access_save`; `ism_handle_action()` took up only actions that start with `ism_`, so the portal reloaded the page without saving. They were not posted in the sandbox tests of v1.163.0 — only the pages were opened.

**Fix**: `ism_handle_action()` also takes up actions that start with `qh_`.

**Tests (sandbox)**: team saved by the CEO and the ticked person gets the QHSE page; Access by position saved by the CEO; both refused for somebody who is not management / CEO; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Rule added to the release routine**: every form of a new page is posted in the sandbox before a release, not only opened.

## §372 v1.164.0 (R97-088) — QHSE: approved suppliers for procurement, Excel import, library for all staff; app check

**Requests**: "1. Check the phone apps for amounts shown 2. Excel import add 3. Ok Supplier evaluation should be done in integration with the existing supplier list and provide a list of approved suppliers to the procurement where he will select for the POs 4. add a read only library".

**1. Phone apps** (`api/v1`): read through for amounts. The staff apps already strip money (`Response::noMoney`, the banned field names of CS-12; the parts app follows `stk_price_visible()`, which the block of §370 now switches off). One exception: the PM app showed the net amount of offers (decision of 01/11/2026) to every PM — `portal_pm.php` now gives it only when `fin_visible()` is true, so not to the QHSE role nor to a role with "Financial data visible" off. The HR app never selects salary or payroll fields; the customer app is for customers. Checked in the code — not run on a device as a QHSE account.

**2. Excel import** (`ism_template()`, `ism_import()`, `ism_import_cols()`): on the list of a register — Import template (sheet Records with the columns, sheet Lists and rules with the allowed values) and Import Excel (.xlsx, 8 MB, 2000 rows). Columns are found by their names (or by position when the count is the same); status, lists and owner are matched by their words; dates 2026-11-17 or 17/11/2026; an empty code gets the automatic number; a code or a title already in the register is skipped; each row goes through the same save as the form, so every rule applies. Instruments: Last calibration + Certificate no. (+ laboratory, valid until) create a finished calibration under the instrument. Not for registers under another record, the Statement of Applicability and the supplier registers (`ism_importable()`). Works in the ISO 27001 and marine registers too.

**3. Approved suppliers** (ISO 9001 8.4): registers `qsup` (AS-001, a supplier of the supplier list, once) and `qsupeval` (AS-001-E01) on the ISO 9001 tab; the evaluation logic of §367 is shared (`ISM_SUP`, `ISM_EVAL`), with six quality criteria. `sup_approval($companyId)` gives approved / conditional / blocked / candidate / ended / none. Procurement: column Approval on Suppliers; card Supplier approval (ISO 9001) on the supplier (procurement reads, QHSE edits); the supplier select of a purchase order grouped Approved · Not evaluated yet · Not approved; `sup_po_check()` in `po_save_from_post()` (new PO, changed supplier of a draft) and in `po_mark_sent()` (a draft PO, also one made by awarding an RFQ): **Not approved → refused always**; setting `po_approved_only` (Settings › Procurement › Approved suppliers) → only Approved / Approved with conditions. RFQs are not limited. Approved supplier list (Excel): `qh_asl_export()`, for procurement and QHSE.

**4. Library** (`views/qhse_lib.php`, QHSE › Documents (library), all staff including the restricted HR / planning / employee accounts): the documents of `qdoc` with status Approved, by kind, with standard, version, approval and files; search and filters; `qh_library_send()` serves a file only when its document is approved.

**Decision to confirm**: the rule "purchase orders only to approved suppliers" is delivered switched **off** — with an empty approved list it would stop every order. Not approved suppliers are refused from the first day.

**Tests (sandbox) — every form posted**: supplier added from the list, twice refused; evaluations → Active 87 % with next evaluation in 12 months (critical) and Not approved 33 %; PO select groups; rule off: PO to Not approved refused, to not evaluated accepted, RFQ to Not approved accepted; rule switched on in the settings form: PO to not evaluated refused, RFQ accepted, approved accepted, the options disabled accordingly; a draft PO whose supplier became Not approved cannot be sent; cards and column on Suppliers; supplier list workbook, refused for an engineer; templates of two registers, import of five rows (three imported, one doubled code skipped, one empty ignored) with the instrument's calibration created and its validity set, the same file again → all skipped, a broken file refused, an engineer refused; library for the CEO, an engineer and a restricted account: approved document shown, draft not, approved file downloaded, draft file 403; page sweeps for three roles, API test; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Security review**: the library serves files only of approved documents and only to staff; an import runs every row through the access and validation of the form and never overwrites; the supplier rule is enforced on the server at creation and at sending, not only in the select. Proposals, no cost in speed: (1) switch the approved-only rule on once the suppliers in use are evaluated; (2) keep confidential documents (salaries, contracts, security procedures) out of the QHSE Documents register, since everything Approved there is readable by all staff; (3) add the calibration laboratories to the approved suppliers with the category Calibration laboratory and their accreditation certificate.

## §373 v1.165.0 (R97-089) — ISO 27001 filled from the company's ISMS manuals

**Request**: five Greek ISMS documents attached (ISMS-MAN-001, ISMS-TIER1-001 … 004, all version 1.0, in force 01/08/2026) — "use the attached manuals and fill up necessary data for ISO 27001, we need also in English, in the portal".

**Rule kept**: the documents are classified Confidential — internal use only. Their content (assets, risks, suppliers, names) is **not** in the repository. The code carries only document numbers, titles, version, dates and "CEO". The register data is delivered as Excel import files and the English translations as Word files, uploaded in the portal by the user.

**1. Documents** (`ism_docs_align()`, once, setting `ism_docs_gas1`): the four seeded documents that match (scope, policy, roles, risk methodology) get their real numbers ISMS-TIER1-002 / 001 / 003 / 004 and titles in English — Greek, version 1.0, approved by CEO on 2026-08-01, status Approved, next review 2027-08-01; ISMS-MAN-001 ISMS Manual is added. A seeded document that somebody already changed is left alone.

**2. Risk matrix of ISMS-TIER1-004** (`ISM_MATRIX`, `ism_matrix_level()`, `ism_level_pill()`): the ISO 27001 risk register uses the 5 × 5 matrix with four levels instead of likelihood × impact — impact 5 is at least High, Critical from likelihood 3. The selects show the names of the scales (1 — Rare … 5 — Almost certain; 1 — Negligible … 5 — Severe), the hint carries the acceptance criteria, the overview counts critical / high / medium / low / not scored. The residual level uses the same matrix. Marine and QHSE registers keep likelihood × impact.

**3. Interested parties** (register `party`, clause 4.2, prefix IP): kind of party, needs and expectations, how it is met.

**4. Import**: the ISO 27001 supplier register is importable (the supplier of the supplier list is chosen on the record afterwards); only parent-bound registers, the Statement of Applicability and the QHSE approved suppliers stay out.

**Delivered outside the repository**: six import workbooks (4 objectives, 19 assets, 8 risks — not scored, 6 interested parties, 2 suppliers, 4 legal requirements; select values by key so that they import in any language) and five English translations (.docx) of the manuals.

**Tests (sandbox) — every form posted**: alignment gives the five numbers (33 documents); matrix checked on eight combinations against the table of the methodology; risk form shows the named scales, the QHSE risk form the generic ones; the six workbooks imported with 0 skipped and the right fields; Interested parties record created through the form and deleted; sweeps admin 9 / hr 2 / pm 0 as before; API test unchanged; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`. 15 language keys added, 11 332 in each of the five files.

**Security review**: no confidential content of the manuals in code, commits or project documents; the import files and translations travel only through the portal's own upload (staff with access to ISO 27001, files outside the web root). Proposals without cost in speed: (1) when the documents are attached, limit the Documents register of ISO 27001 so that "Confidential" files are downloadable only by the ISMS team and management; (2) include `storage/isms` in the off-hosting backup (open since §364).

**Found in the manuals**: blanks and a few points where the documents differ from each other were reported to the CEO in the delivery note; they are not repeated here because the documents are confidential.

## §374 v1.166.0 (R97-090) — Statement of Applicability filled from Excel

**Request**: "based on what you know for my company and the processes and the It department lets fill the Statement of applicability in the system".

**Rule kept (§373)**: the content of the Statement of Applicability describes the security measures of the company and is confidential — it is not in the repository. The code only makes the register fillable from a file; the filled workbook (93 controls: status, justification, how it is met, next review) was handed to the CEO and is uploaded in the portal.

**Code** (`app/ism.php`): `ism_importable()` no longer excludes `soa`; `ism_template('soa')` writes the 93 controls as they stand (number, title, status, owner, dates, justification, how it is met); `ism_import()` for `soa` finds the control by its number (`ism_soa_code()`: "A.5.1", "a 5.1" and "5.1" are read as "A 5.1") and saves it through `ism_save_from_post()` — history entry, validation and access as in the form. A control with a status other than Planned or with a text already entered is skipped, as is a row that brings nothing; an unknown number is reported with its row. The theme (Organizational, People, Physical, Technological) is kept.

**Tests (sandbox) — form posted**: workbook of 93 rows → 92 filled and 1 skipped (a control changed by hand before the import kept its text); second upload → 0 filled, 93 skipped; unknown number reported; the template downloaded afterwards carries the filled values; restricted account gets no template; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`. 2 language keys, 11 334 per file.

**Security review**: no new page and no new permission — the import needs the same access as editing a control and cannot overwrite work already done. Proposal (no cost in speed): the Excel exports of ISO 27001 (All registers, templates with content) carry the security measures of the company — limit them to the ISMS team and management, as proposed for Confidential files in §373.

## §375 v1.167.0 (R97-091) — Customer subscriptions: expirations, import, renewal offers, pipeline; QHSE role in ISO 27001

**Requests**: the list of customer subscriptions kept by Sales (CSV) — "import as necessary and adapt the module of the subscriptions to show expirations, and also integrate with Sales for the offers, and to Sales pipeline accordingly"; and "the quality manager which is also QHSE manager should have access to 27001 and It modules also".

**Rule kept**: the list is customer data (vessels, serial numbers) — it is not in the repository. The portal got an import; the CEO uploads the file.

**Database** (`install/migrate_cp_r97_091_subscriptions.sql`, state `r98d`): `rs_contracts` gets `product`, `serial_no`, `pay_status`, `period_months`, `installed_on`, `license_expiry`, `sq_ref`, `offer_id`, `renewal_offer_id` (three keys). Everything new is behind `rs_sub_has()`, so the pages work before the step runs.

**Module** (`app/rs.php`, `views/cyber.php`, `rs_edit.php`, `rs_view.php`): the fields on the form and the page; KPIs `rs_kpis()` (active / pending payment, renewal within 30 and 90 days, licence within 90 days, expired); column Licence expiry with `rs_date_pill()`; filters `due90`, `lic`, `norenew`; search also in product, serial and SQ.

**Import** (`rs_import()`, action `rs_import`): .csv (comma or semicolon, UTF-8 or Windows-1253) or .xlsx, 4 MB; columns by name; vessel by `rs_vessel_key()` (prefix M/Y, S/Y, P/S … dropped, former names); optional creation of missing vessels; dates d/m/yyyy; period "1 year" / "3 years"; payment status → status (rejected → cancelled; expired or renewal date passed → expired; else active); start = date installed, else renewal date minus the period; price 0 → no fee; duplicates (vessel + SQ + product) skipped; imported as internal; the renewal task is created for active ones; the offer is linked when `rs_offer_find()` finds the SQ / offer number.

**Sales** (`rs_renewal_offer()`, `rs_renew()`, `rs_of_offer()`, `rs_renewals_due()`): + Renewal offer creates a draft through `of_save_from_post()` under a project of the vessel (an offer always belongs to a project) and stores it as `renewal_offer_id`; the offer page shows the subscription; Renew for another period moves the end date by the period, makes the renewal offer the offer of the subscription and notes it; `views/pipeline.php` has the card Subscriptions to renew (90 days ahead, 180 days back, not refused). Rights: `rs_sales_can()` — administrators, management, staff who see the offers; the Sales block is hidden from accounts without financial data.

**QHSE role** (`qh_role()` in `app/ism.php`): `ism_can()` is also true for a staff role named QHSE, HSE or Quality (the test of `fin_blocked()`), which opens ISO 27001 and, through it, marine cyber safety. Financial data stays closed for that role.

**Tests (sandbox) — every form posted**: the real list: without creating vessels 0 imported and every row named; with creation 21 imported, 1 skipped (row without vessel), statuses and dates as above; second upload 0 imported; contract saved with the new fields and linked to an existing offer by its number; KPIs and the three filters; renewal offer created as draft with title, amount and notes, second attempt refused; offer page and pipeline card show it; renewal moved the end date one year and noted it; a restricted account can neither import nor renew; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`. Not tested: the QHSE role on a real account (no such role in the sandbox — logic read through); the import against the live vessel list. 57 language keys, 11 391 per file.

**Security review**: import and renewal are server-side checked (`rs_sales_can()`), the file is parsed in memory and never stored, amounts stay behind `fin_visible()`, imported subscriptions are internal so that payment remarks are not shown to customers by accident. Proposals without cost in speed: (1) a weekly e-mail to Sales with the subscriptions and licences running out; (2) serial numbers visible to the customer only on their own vessels (already so) — consider hiding them from customer accounts altogether.

## §376 v1.168.0 (R97-092) — Subscriptions: weekly e-mail to Sales; serial numbers hidden from customers

**Request**: the two cybersecurity proposals of §375, accepted — "A weekly e-mail to Sales listing subscriptions and licences that are running out" and "Hide device serial numbers from customer accounts".

**Weekly list** (`rs_weekly()`, `rs_weekly_build()`, `rs_weekly_recipients()` in `app/rs.php`; called from `hk_tick()`): on the first visit of a calendar week (setting `rs_weekly` = ISO week) the list is built — subscriptions running out within 90 days or lapsed in the last 180 days and not refused, with the renewal offer; licences running out within 90 days — and sent as plain text by `cp_mail()` plus one notification per person with a link to the filtered list. Recipients: active staff with `can_sales`, plus `pp_managers()`; staff roles named QHSE / HSE / Quality are excluded. Nothing is sent when nothing is due. Setting `rs_weekly_on` (default on); actions `rs_weekly_now` (those who may import) and `rs_weekly_toggle` (administrators, management). The text carries no amounts.

**Serial numbers**: `views/cyber.php` and `views/rs_view.php` show the serial number only to staff; `rs_list()` searches serial and SQ only for staff, so a customer cannot confirm a serial by searching. The phone apps do not return subscriptions.
