**Tests (sandbox) — every form posted**: with the real list imported, Send now reports 4 renewals and 4 licences, two recipients, one notification each with the right link (no mail transport in the sandbox — the text was printed and read); the switch turns off and the page says so; a restricted account cannot send; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`. Not tested: delivery of the e-mail on the live server; the customer view of a subscription with a serial (code read).

**Security review**: the weekly text goes only to internal staff addresses of the portal and contains vessel, plan, dates, contract and offer numbers and, for licences, the serial number needed to renew — no amounts, no customer contact data. Proposal (no cost in speed): if the serial should not travel by e-mail either, it can be replaced by the contract number only.

## §377 v1.169.0 (R97-093) — Marine cyber safety: Equipment with its serial number; people who left out of the personnel lists

**Requests**: "In the Marine cyber safety the products should be the equipment we produce since there will be a confusion. So when a serial number is produced then it can be integrated to the products of cyber safety, maybe also to keep the same terminology (equipment)"; and "Persons not active in the company or left etc should not be present to the lists of personnel".

**1. Equipment** — wording: the 16 texts of the marine area say Equipment (el Εξοπλισμός, it Apparecchiature), the register key stays `mprod` and the numbers CBS-…. Link: `ism_records.equipment_id` (`install/migrate_cp_r97_093_marine_equipment.sql`, state `r98e`, behind `ism_has_eq()`); the record form has Serial number (Equipment register) with `ism_eq_options()` — produced or sold by us, not retired, not yet linked; one record per serial (`ism_eq_apply()`, refused before anything is written); the vessel of the serial is taken when the record has none. `ism_eq_add()` (action `ism_eq_add`, for those with marine access) creates the record from a serial through `ism_save_from_post()` — ten UR E27 documents, vessel, and project / sub-project of the production order that issued the serial. `ism_eq_card()` on `views/equipment_view.php`; `ism_eq_unlinked()` on the overview lists serials of production orders without a record; the overview and the vessel / sub-project card show the serial.

**2. Personnel lists** (`hr_gone_accounts()`, `hr_gone_ids()`, `hr_here()`, `hr_gone_close()` in `app/hr3.php`): an account whose HR card is not active is taken out of `ism_users()` (owners and teams of ISO 27001, marine and QHSE), `mt_staff()` (mentions), `hr_staff()` and `soc_staff()`. Recording the end of employment already closed the staff account; unticking Active on the card now does the same (`hr_person_save`). HR settings › People shows the accounts still open with Close the staff accounts (HR, owner, administrator). The portal never closes an administrator account. Nothing is closed by the update itself — a wrong tick in HR must not lock people out; the list makes the decision visible.

**Tests (sandbox) — every form posted**: serial added from the equipment page → CBS-001 with ten documents, vessel and fields; second click returns the same record; form select, clearing and re-choosing the serial; a second record on the same serial refused with nothing written; card, overview and vessel card wording; person made inactive → gone from the owner list, listed in HR settings, closed by an administrator, refused for an account without HR rights; sweeps admin 9 / hr 2 / pm 0; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`. Not tested: project / sub-project taken from a production order (no production order in the sandbox — code read); unticking Active through the card form. 18 language keys, 11 422 per file.

**Security review**: closing the accounts of leavers is control A 5.18 / A 6.5 — the new list shows the gap instead of hiding it. Proposals without cost in speed: (1) a line in the daily health report when accounts are open for people who left; (2) the same warning for administrator accounts in Settings › Users.

## §378 v1.169.1 (R97-094) — Leavers: health report line, warning in Settings › Users, general staff list

**Request**: the two cybersecurity proposals of §377, accepted.

**Daily health report** (`dh_build()` in `app/diag.php`): one line in the summary — accounts still open for people who are not active in HR, with the number of administrators and where to close them. Numbers only; no names leave the portal by e-mail.

**Settings › Users** (`views/admin_users.php`): a card at the top with the same accounts by name, role and leaving date; each opens the user list filtered on the person; link to HR settings › People for the staff accounts. Administrator accounts are closed here by hand, as before.

**General staff list** (`staff_users()` in `app/pm.php`, used in 18 places — project managers, teams, assignees): passes through `hr_here()`, so people who are not active in HR are left out there too.

**Tests (sandbox)**: two people made inactive (one staff, one administrator) → card with both names and roles, health report line "2 (administrators: 1)", `staff_users()` without them; made active again → card gone, line 0, list complete; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`.

**Security review**: nothing new is exposed — the card is on a page for administrators only, the e-mail carries counts. No further proposal in this release.

## §379 v1.170.0 (R97-095) — Production orders marked for cyber safety approval; the marine list shows only those

**Request** (with a screenshot of Marine cyber safety listing every produced serial): "in the list of UR E26/27 add only production orders that are defined to comply with the cybersecurity standards through the production order … otherwise the ITs will see so many production orders not related to Cybersafety approval".

**Database** (`install/migrate_cp_r97_095_production_cyber.sql`, state `r98f`): `production_orders.cyber_e27` (0 / 1, default 0), behind `pd_cyber_has()`.

**Production order** (`pd_save_from_post()`, `views/production_edit.php`, `views/production_order.php`): tick Cyber safety approval (IACS UR E26 / E27) with its explanation; the order page shows the mark (a link to Marine cyber safety for those with access); a change of the mark is written in the order log; when it is set, the programming team and the ISMS team get one notification.

**Marine cyber safety** (`ism_eq_unlinked()`): the list is now To add — production orders under UR E26 / E27 and takes only serials of orders with the mark; without the column it is empty. Existing orders are unmarked after the update, so the list starts empty until the orders concerned are ticked. Choosing a serial by hand on a record and the button on the page of a serial number are unchanged.

**Tests (sandbox) — every form posted**: order without the tick → its serial not listed; order with the tick → listed, tick shown on the form, mark on the order page, log line; ticking the first order afterwards → listed; serial added → record with the project of the production order (this path was not tested in §377); no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`. Not tested: the notification to the team (the only member in the sandbox is the user who ticks). 6 language keys, 11 429 per file.

**Security review**: no new page; the mark is saved with the rights of the production order. Proposal (no cost in speed): when a marked order reaches the stage Delivered while one of its serials has no cyber safety record or an unapproved one, warn the order manager and the IT Manager.

## §380 v1.171.0 (R97-096) — Sub-project process: Procurement fed from the procurement process

**Request** (screenshot of the Process card: Engineering and the production stages fed, Procurement typed by hand): "can we feed procurement also from the procurement process?"

**Calculation** (`proc_sub_pct()` in `app/prq.php`): lines of the requisitions of the sub-project in status submitted, management, approved or closed, plus lines of its purchase orders (kind po, not draft / cancelled) that have no requisition line. Per line: in the warehouse share d = (received + reserved from stock, or issued) / quantity; covered share c = (ordered + reserved) / quantity; progress = d + 0.5 × (c − d). A requisition not yet approved gives 0. Weight = quantity × estimated price (requisition) or line total (order); if any line has no value, all lines weigh 1. No lines → null, the value of the PM stands.

**Wiring**: `pd_sub_stages()` replaces the Procurement value with it (key `proc`, as `eng` does for Engineering — the earlier evidence from subcontracting of production orders is used only when there is no requisition or purchase order); `pd_sub_fed()` treats a sub-project with procurement lines as fed; `views/subproject.php` shows the hint with counts and a link to the Procurement tab; Unlock / Lock work as for the other stages. `prq_event()` and `po_event()` call `proc_feed_later()`, which writes the stage at the end of the request for the sub-project of the requisition, of the order and of the requisitions its lines come from. `hk_tick()` runs `proc_feed_all()` once (setting `proc_feed_v1`) so that existing sub-projects get their value without waiting for the next event.

**Tests (sandbox)**: draft → not fed; submitted → 0 %; approved → 0 %; one of two lines ordered (half of the value) → 25 %; half received → 38 %; received and the other reserved from stock → 100 %; a line without price → equal weights 50 %; page hint with counts; submitting a requisition through its action rewrote a stale 77 % to 0 %; Unlock hides the feed and keeps the PM value, Lock returns it; a sub-project without procurement unchanged; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`. Not tested: lines of purchase orders without a requisition (calculation read through), the one-off run on live data. 3 language keys, 11 432 per file.

**Security review**: only a percentage is derived and shown — no amounts, so accounts without financial data see nothing new. No proposal in this release.

## §381 v1.172.0 (R97-097) — An approved BOM becomes draft purchase orders

**Request**: "when a bom is imported in the procurement of the sub project then i need to be able to convert it also to PO to go to the procurement to follow the process".

**Before**: an approved BOM could become a requisition (uncovered lines) or a production kit; purchase orders were raised from approved requisition lines (To buy).

**Now** (`bom_po_plan()`, `bom_to_po()`, `bom_pos()`, `bom_po_can()` in `app/bom.php`; action `bom_to_po`; card `#bom-po` in `views/bom.php`): for an approved BOM and a user who may raise purchase orders (`po_admin()`; the IT buyer only for sub-projects open to IT), the lines still to buy — `to_buy` of `bom_lines()` minus the quantity already on live order lines with the same `bom_line_id` — are grouped by supplier: preferred supplier of the item, last supplier, supplier of the maker (`sup_for_maker()`); lines without one go to the supplier chosen on the card or stay. One draft `po_orders` row of kind po per supplier with the supplier's currency, incoterms, terms and e-mail, project, sub-project, default warehouse; lines carry `bom_line_id`, the price of `stk_price_proposal()` or the estimate typed on the BOM line. `sup_po_check()` is applied — a supplier that is Not approved gets no order and is reported. Refused when the BOM has a live requisition (so that nothing is bought twice). Events: `po_event create`, `bom_event to_po`.

**Tests (sandbox) — form posted**: BOM with an item of a preferred supplier, an item whose maker has a supplier and a free-text line: without a chosen supplier the free-text line is refused with the message; with one → order with the estimate as price; items covered by stock are not ordered; with quantities above stock → two drafts, one per supplier; repeat → "everything is on a purchase order"; the card lists the orders; the order page opens; live requisition → refused with its number; an account without procurement rights → 403; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`. Not tested: a Not approved supplier in the plan (code path read). 16 language keys, 11 448 per file.

**Note**: the BOM must be Approved — an imported BOM is a draft until it is issued and approved by Procurement, as before.

**Security review**: creation is checked on the server with the same right as a manual purchase order and respects the approved-supplier rule; nothing is sent to a supplier — the orders are drafts. No proposal in this release.

## §382 v1.173.0 (R97-098) — Risk log: internal by default, the ticked risks in the weekly report

**Request**: "Should we include the risk logs and status to the Weekly report?" — agreed: yes, "by default internal and only if selected to be external to the report".

**Database** (`install/migrate_cp_r97_098_risk_visibility.sql`, state `r98g`): `sp_risks.visibility` enum internal / external, default internal — every existing risk is internal after the update. Behind `rk_vis_has()`.

**Risk log** (`rk_save_from_post()`, `views/_risks.php`): tick Show in the weekly report on the form; pill In weekly report in the list.

**Weekly vessel report** (`rk_report_rows()`, `weekly_data()`, `wk_sections()` section 4): table Risk log per project with the ticked risks that are open, accepted or occurred, and those mitigated or closed in the week; columns number, title + mitigation (mark "new" when identified in the week), sub-project, score, owner, due date, status; row tone red for occurred or score from 15, amber from 8, green when mitigated; one line with the counts. PDF and Word use the same sections. The rule is the same for staff and owners: a risk that is not ticked is not in the report at all, because the copy a member of staff sends or files is produced under the staff account.

**Executive summary** (`app/xs.php`): for an owner the tile Open risks counts only the ticked risks; the facts handed to the assistant for the summary text list only the ticked risks (the released text is shown to the owner).

**Tests (sandbox) — form posted**: a ticked and an unticked risk saved; pill in the log; PDF of the week shows the ticked one with [new], mitigation and the count line, and not the unticked one; set to mitigated → still listed for the week; unticked on edit → gone from the report; Word file produced; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`. Not tested: the report opened by a customer account (the same query; the sandbox customer session had expired).

**Security review — observation**: the weekly report a member of staff downloads, files or e-mails is the internal version: it contains the weekly notes marked Internal and internal ticket comments. Risks are safe by the rule above, but the notes are not. Proposal (no cost in speed): a choice Owner's copy when a member of staff sends or files the weekly report, which builds it exactly as the owner sees it.

## §383 v1.174.0 (R97-099) — Weekly vessel report: Owner's copy when staff download, send or file it

**Request**: the proposal of §382, accepted — "add an 'Owner's copy' choice when staff send or file the weekly report".

**Problem**: `weekly_data()` decides what is internal by the account that builds the report. A member of staff therefore always got the internal version — internal weekly notes, internal ticket comments, internal open issues, our drafts — also in the PDF attached to an e-mail and in the revision filed with visibility external, which the customer sees under Documents.

**Change**: `weekly_data(..., bool $ownerCopy)` builds with `$staff = false` when asked; `wk_as_owner()` is switched on while `wk_sections()` runs so that `iss_vis_sql()` hides internal issues too; the header reads Owner's copy.
- Download (`views/report_weekly.php`, `?copy=owner`; buttons on `views/weekly_vessel.php`): Owner's copy PDF / Word next to Internal PDF / Word. A file built by staff without the choice is named …-INT.
- E-mail (`em.php` attachment key `wk:…:pdf:o`): four attachments offered — Owner's copy PDF (ticked), Owner's copy Word, Internal PDF, Internal Word.
- Filing (`doc_issue`, kind weekly): select next to the button — Owner's copy (default; filed external under the report number) or Internal copy (filed internal under number-INT; listed for staff under Internal copies filed). `ar_issued_html()` takes extra form fields.
The page on screen stays the internal version for staff.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: with an internal and an external weekly note and open issue: internal PDF contains all four and the scope line Internal; Owner's copy PDF and Word contain only the two external ones and the line Owner's copy; file names with and without -INT; filing Owner's copy → revision R0 external; filing Internal copy → R0 internal under -INT, listed on the page; no new lines in `storage/php-errors.log`. Not tested: the attachment built at the moment an e-mail is sent (same function with the flag — needs the mail connection).

**Not changed**: revisions of weekly reports filed before this release are the internal version and remain visible to customers — see the proposal.

**Security review — proposals** (no cost in speed): (1) list the weekly-report revisions filed before v1.174.0 so that the ones containing internal notes can be withdrawn or re-filed as Owner's copy; (2) the same Owner's copy choice for the monthly sub-project report and the vessel technical history, which are built the same way.

## §384 v1.175.0 (R97-100) — Owner's copy for the monthly report and the vessel history; reports filed earlier

**Requests**: "Do the same also for the monthly report if not the same and the vessel history report" and "apply also the cybersecurity proposals of yours" (§383: the list of reports filed before Owner's copies; the same choice for the other two reports).

**Finding**: both reports had the same exposure as the weekly one. The monthly report takes its notes and comments through `vis_sql()` and its open issues through `iss_vis_sql()`; the vessel history decides with `is_staff()` (drafts, timesheet hours, internal issues). Filed by staff as external documents, they carried the internal content.

**Change**: `staff_view()` (`app/pm.php`) = staff and not building an Owner's copy; `vis_sql()`, `vessel_history()`, `vh_report_tables()` and the scope label of the history use it; `iss_vis_sql()` already followed `wk_as_owner()`. `views/report_sp.php` and `views/report_vessel.php` take `copy=owner` (staff) and switch the mode for the request; internal files get -INT in their name. `doc_issue` for kinds monthly and vessel builds the Owner's copy unless Internal copy is chosen (then number-INT, visibility internal). Buttons and the select on `views/subproject.php` (Reports tab) and `views/vessel_view.php`.

**Reports filed earlier** (`ar_old_reports()`, `ar_old_reports_hide()`, action `ar_old_hide`, card on `views/admin_health.php`; administrators and management): issued files with visibility external, document number containing -WR-, -PR- or -VH-, created before the moment this list was first opened (setting `owner_copy_from`). Per revision: open the files, Make internal (visibility → internal; staff keep it, the customer no longer sees it); Make all internal. Each use writes a line to the PHP log (who, how many).

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: monthly Word and print view, vessel history PDF: internal version has the internal note / issue and the label Internal, the Owner's copy only the external ones and the label Owner's copy; file names; filing Owner's and Internal copies of both with the right visibility; the filed Owner's monthly file re-read; card lists the earlier revisions by kind; Make internal for one and for all; refused for an account without the right; no PHP errors. Not tested: the reports opened by a customer account (unchanged path).

**Security review**: the three customer-facing reports now have one rule and one switch. No further proposal; open point for the user — go through the list in System health.

## §385 v1.176.0 (R97-101) — Inventory import: template, ERP export with a title line

**Why**: the inventory import (R79-001) expected the header on the first line and offered no template. The ERP warehouse export starts with a title line, so the upload answered "columns not found".

**Template** (`stk_import_template()` in `app/inv.php`, `inventory_import&template=1`, procurement administrators only): workbook `GAS-inventory-import-template.xlsx`, sheet Inventory (Code, Description, Description EN, Category, Unit, Qty, Last price, Location, Maker, Factory code, Barcode, Min stock, Supplier, one example line) and sheet Read me. The headers are the English names the column map already knows.

**Header search** (`stk_import_parse()`): the header is the first of the first 15 lines that maps both code and description; the lines above it are skipped, warnings keep the line numbers of the file, the preview shows "Header found on line n" (`header_line`) when it is not line 1.

**Other**: pack units written as 10ΤΜΧ are packs (`stk_unit_norm()`, `stk_pack_from_unit()`); a file with more than `STK_IMPORT_MAX` = 30 000 lines is refused; `stk_import_apply()` allows 900 s and continues when the browser connection drops (everything in one transaction, as before).

**The company's item list is not in the repository**: it was prepared as a workbook for upload (header on line 1, codes trimmed, non-warehouse lines moved to a second sheet) and handed to the user.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: template download (administrator 200, staff without procurement rights 403, HR redirected); upload of an ERP export with title line (header found on line 2) and of the prepared workbook; Apply in mode full with about 14 000 lines: 16 s, items, opening stock, prices and categories as in the file; the same file applied again: nothing new, no stock or price doubled (6 s); inventory list, search, valuation and item card with that volume under 0.3 s; a file that is not a workbook and the template itself uploaded; no new lines in the PHP error log. Not tested: the duration on the live hosting and behind the WAF.

**Security review**: the import stays limited to procurement administrators, CSRF-protected, nothing is written before Apply. New: the line limit. Proposal: refuse workbooks whose sheet is larger than a set size when unpacked, in `xlsx_rows()`, for all imports.

## §386 v1.176.1 (R97-102) — System budget: parts locked at procurement are shown as locked; errors brought into view

**Report**: "I cannot delete parts from the list in the System budget". Deleting works (tick 🗑, Save lines) — except while the budget is at procurement (`sbp_state() === 'requested'`, R97-049), where `sb_save_parts_from_post()` refuses the save. The form still looked editable and returned to `#parts`, so the red message at the top of the page was never seen.

**Parts card** (`views/system_budget.php`): `$pLock`, `$edP`, `$roP` — while locked: notice `sbp.locked_parts` with the Take back form (`sbp_recall`), fields read-only, no new lines, no delete ticks, no Save lines, no BOM import / inventory proposals / add from inventory. Server rules unchanged.

**All pages** (`assets/portal.js`): with a `#section` in the address and a `.flash--bad` on the page, the message is scrolled into view after load.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: parts card normal (ticks, Save, three add forms); at procurement (notice, Take back, nothing to post); forced post of a delete while locked refused, line kept; Take back posted, card editable again, delete posted and line gone; in a browser: tick and Save deletes the line; a refused save shows the message in view; other budgets and a revision view open; PHP error log clean. Not confirmed: that the user's budget was at procurement — the screenshot (lines waiting for a cost) points to it.

**Security review**: no new input; the lock was and stays enforced on the server. No proposal.

## §387 v1.177.0 (R97-103) — System budget: 1024 part lines; packed forms

**Limits**: `SB_MAX_PARTS` 124 → 1024 (`app/sb.php`); BOM file for a budget 400 → `SB_MAX_PARTS` lines (`sb_bom_stage()`, text of the template). New `SB_AI_PARTS` = 250 (part lines listed to the assistant in `sb_ai_description()` and the consistency check, with a "… and n more" line) and `SB_AI_ROWS` = 125 (staged rows matched per press). No database change: `sb_parts` has no limit of its own, `sb_revisions.snapshot` is MEDIUMTEXT (1024 lines ≈ 0.4 MB).

**Why a new transport**: PHP accepts `max_input_vars` fields per request (1000 by default). A parts table of 1024 lines posts about 8 000 fields; PHP would silently drop everything after the limit.
- `data-pack` on a form (`assets/portal.js`, `formdata` event): with 200 or more text fields, all of them except `_csrf`, `action`, `id` are replaced by one field `_packed` = JSON list of [name, value]. Files are not touched. Works for user submits and for `form.submit()` (confirm dialogs).
- `post_unpack()` (`app/helpers.php`, called in `index.php` after `csrf_check()`): puts the list back into `$_POST`. Rules: at most 8 MB and 40 000 pairs; names `base[k][k][k]` with letters, digits, `_ . -` only, at most three levels; `_csrf` never taken from the list; a real field always wins over a packed one.
- Guard for every POST (`index.php`): a request that is not packed and carries `max_input_vars` or more values is refused with `err.form_cut` — it was cut by PHP and must not be half saved.
- Forms marked: Parts of a System Budget, BOM staging (`system_budget_bom`), procurement pricing (`system_budget_proc`).

**Save of part lines** (`sb_save_parts_from_post()`): a line whose values and position did not change is not written.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted, in a browser unless noted**: budget with 1023 lines: page 1.4 MB, opens in about 1.4 s; Save lines packed (0.5 MB), all lines kept; second save writes nothing; edit of line 501 and of the last line (quotes, ampersand, Greek), delete of line 3, new line — all correct, order numbers unique; at 1024 no new line offered and add-from-inventory hidden; curl: unpacked form with 1 400 fields refused and nothing saved; malformed `_packed` refused; hand-made `_packed` trying to replace `action`, `id`, `_csrf`, a four-level name and a path-like name — ignored, only the valid fields applied; Excel export 1 024 lines; new revision stored and opened; send to procurement, pricing form with 1 024 lines saved packed; BOM file of 1 100 lines: 1 024 staged, applied until the budget was full; PHP error log clean; page sweep unchanged. Not tested: conversion to an offer with more than 124 part lines listed one by one (the offer editor is not packed), BOM matching time against the full inventory on the live server, the WAF with a 0.5 MB form.

**Security review**: the packed field passes the same CSRF check and the same permission checks as a normal form; it cannot set the CSRF token, cannot override fields that were really posted, accepts only plain names and is size-limited. The new guard closes a silent data-loss case on every large form of the portal. Proposal: mark the offer editor and the engineering BOM editor with `data-pack` as well.

## §388 v1.177.1 (R97-104) — Daily health report of 18/11/2026: Projects list, false migration alarm, backups

**Report read** (v1.175.0, 08:02): 1 failed check, 2 warnings, `projects` 190 slow views (up to 6.2 s, 3 400 – 4 000 statements), one PHP warning text.

1. **Projects list** — statement profile on the sandbox (general log): about 15 statements per sub-project, from `bg_pill()` → `bg_calc()`, `pd_sub_fed()` → `eng_stage_pcts()` and `proc_sub_pct()` (the last one added by R97-096). New `sub_ids_with(key, sqls)` in `app/helpers.php`: on GET requests only, one statement per table gives the ids of the sub-projects that have rows; `bg_pill()` (no row in `sp_budgets`), `eng_stage_pcts()` (no row in `eng_budgets`, approved `eng_requests`, `sp_budgets`) and `proc_sub_pct()` (no `pr_requests` / `po_orders`) return at once for the others. On POST requests nothing changes (a write in the same request must be seen). Sandbox: 246 → 85 statements for the administrator, page body identical before and after for administrator and staff.
2. **"Database steps: GAS-CP-R46-005"** — false alarm: `cp_migration_state()['r46']` read `SHOW COLUMNS` with `scalar()` and then asked for `['Type']` of a string. Now `one()`. The self-check of the updater was always right.
3. **Update backups 21 folders** — the daily clean-up kept three but could not delete the others (sub-folders). New `cp_rmtree()` in `app/cache.php`: only below `storage/backups`, never through a link, depth limit.
4. **`Undefined variable $docs` in `views/reports.php`** — entries of 17/11 written by v1.157.1; corrected in v1.157.2 (§ R97-078); they leave the 7-day window by themselves.

**Not changed**: `system_budget_checks` 5.4 s (one view); news feed HTTP 403 of an outside site; one statistics time-out.

**Tests (sandbox)**: statement counts and page comparison as above; migration state before / after; `cp_rmtree()` on a scratch tree with sub-folders, a hidden file and a link pointing outside (outside untouched, three newest kept); page sweep; PHP error log clean. Not measured: the live Projects list — to be read from the next health reports.

**Security review**: `cp_rmtree()` is confined to the backups folder by real path and refuses links. No new input. No proposal.

## §389 v1.178.0 (R97-105) — Procurement: Cancel works again; delete of purchase orders, RFQs and requisitions

**Cancel did nothing** — `data-prompt` (R86-002, `assets/portal.js`) asked for the reason inside the form's submit event and then called `requestSubmit()` from inside that same event; browsers ignore a submit started while the form is firing its submit event. Now the first attempt is stopped for the other listeners too (the one-submit lock no longer locks the buttons for 30 s after a dismissed box) and the second submit is started after the event (`setTimeout 0`). Affects every `data-prompt` form: purchase order Cancel, payable, delivery note, RFQ supplier.

**Delete** (`app/po.php`): `po_delete_block()` / `po_delete()`, `prq_delete_block()` / `prq_delete()`, actions `po_delete`, `prq_delete`, buttons on `views/purchase_order.php` and `views/requisition.php` (with the refusal reason as a hint when the status would allow it).
- Purchase order / RFQ: status draft or cancelled; draft → procurement administrators, cancelled → management or portal administrator. Refused with: `po_receipts`, `si_invoices` / `si_lines`, `inv_delivery_notes` / `inv_dn_lines`, `inv_prices` / `inv_licences` / `inv_moves` (ref po), `po_costs`, a PO with `rfq_id` = this RFQ. Removes RFQ suppliers and quotes, milestones, lines, events, the `sp_costs` row (`cost_id`) and the order; the Procurement stage of the sub-project is fed again.
- Requisition: status draft, rejected or cancelled; own draft → requester, otherwise procurement administrators. Refused with lines on an order, quantities reserved / ordered / received / issued, delivery-note lines.
- A reason is required. Everything in one transaction.

**Record of deleted documents** — migration r98h `install/migrate_cp_r97_105_proc_deleted.sql`, table `proc_deleted` (kind, number, title, party, project, status, total, lines, reason, JSON snapshot of header and lines, who, IP, when). Without the table nothing can be deleted. `procdel_card()` shows the list under `purchase_orders` and `requisitions` to management, administrators and procurement administrators; totals only for those who may see prices. Numbers are not reused (the counters are untouched).

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted; browser for the buttons**: old script: Cancel with a typed reason leaves the order Sent (fault reproduced); new script: box dismissed → nothing happens and the button stays usable; reason typed → Cancelled, requisition line back to "to buy"; Delete of a cancelled order (cost line gone), of a draft order, of a draft requisition; no Delete button on a sent order or an approved requisition; requisition with a line on an order: no button until the order is cancelled and deleted and the requisition cancelled; requisition with reserved stock cancelled (reservation released) then deleted; empty reason refused; forced posts from a staff account without procurement rights and from HR refused; list of deleted documents shown to the administrator, not to staff; migration run twice; PHP error log clean. Not tested: an order with receipts, invoices or logistic costs (no such test data) — the refusal is by query only; the payable / delivery-note / RFQ reason boxes in a browser.

**Security review**: deletion needs a role, a reason and leaves a permanent record with a snapshot; it is refused wherever goods, money or stock are attached, so the accounting trail cannot be removed through it. Proposal: include the count of deletions of the last 24 h in the daily health report.

## §390 v1.179.0 (R97-106) — Similar inventory items; BOM file into a purchase order; delete of BOM revisions

**Why**: imported BOMs and typed order lines created new codes for items that were already in the inventory under a slightly different code. Trial on the sandbox with the full inventory and a real switchboard BOM: of 81 lines 46 matched exactly, and for 34 of the other 35 an inventory item existed — mostly because our codes carry a maker prefix and the item has no maker part number filled in.

**Engine** (`app/inv.php`): `stk_sim_index()` loads the inventory once per request (about 14 000 items: 0.12 s, 55 MB) with stock, an index by the first four characters of the normalised code / part number / code without its maker prefix, and an index by word (words on more than 400 items ignored). `stk_similar(row, limit, exclude)` scores: 97–99 the part number is our code without the prefix (`same`), 85 one code inside the other, 50–84 a long common beginning (`code`, `part`), 40–80 two or more shared words, +10 same maker (`text`); below 45 nothing. Part numbers written inside the description are used too. Result with on hand, reserved, free. No assistant call, nothing leaves the portal.

**BOM import review** (`views/bom.php`): for rows not matched at 95 % or more, up to four similar items with stock pill, reason and Use (sets the row's item and Accept; saved by the existing `bom_import_decide()`); mark "similar items exist" next to "Create new item"; proposals for at most 400 rows per page.

**Purchase orders** (`app/po.php`, `views/purchase_order.php`): `po_import_bom()` (action `po_import_bom`, draft PO / editable RFQ, not for the IT department's orders) reads the BOM template through `bom_file_rows()` (new, same header aliases as the staging via `bom_imp_aliases()`, plus Unit price), links exact matches (`bom_row_exact()`: Golden code, maker part number, normalised part number) and writes the others as free-text lines; price from the file, else the price proposal of the item. Card "Lines without an inventory item — similar items" for every free-text line (also typed ones), `po_line_use_item()` (action `po_line_item`): only on a line without item and without requisition link.

**BOM delete** (`bom_delete_block()`, `bom_delete()`): management added to administrators and Procurement; blocked by live requisitions (`pr_requests.bom_rev_id`) or order lines (`po_lines.bom_line_id`) instead of by past events; reason required (`data-prompt`); recorded in `proc_deleted` (migration r98i adds `bom` to `doc_kind`), listed under Requisitions.

**Tests (sandbox, full inventory loaded) — forms posted; browser for the buttons**: engine trial as above; BOM file staged (1.8 s), review page 0.13 s with 34 rows of proposals; Use in the browser sets item and Accept, decisions saved; draft PO: 81 lines imported (46 linked, 35 free), proposals shown, Use links a line and keeps the typed text in the note; refused: Use on a linked line, posts from accounts without procurement rights, a file that is not a workbook, import into a sent order; BOM delete with reason in the browser, refused with a requisition made from it and without a reason; migration twice; PHP error log clean. Not tested: import into an RFQ; System Budget BOM staging does not show the proposals yet; a BOM of several hundred unmatched rows (about 6 ms per row).

**Security review**: read-only use of inventory data already visible to the same users; the two new write actions need procurement administration and an editable order; the file goes through the existing workbook reader with the 8 MB limit. Proposal: fill the maker part number of inventory items from our own code where the code is "prefix.partnumber" — it would turn most "similar" into exact matches.

## §391 v1.180.0 (R97-107) — Inventory proposals everywhere (release 1 of the single procurement path); codes by Procurement; part numbers from our codes

**Decisions of Nikos (18/11/2026)**: direct orders may be for Stock or for Use (release 2); new inventory codes by Procurement only, who must be able to convert free lines into items; the warehouse view offers both parts ordered for the project and parts received for stock and reserved (release 3); fill the maker part number from "prefix.partnumber" codes.

**One card for three documents** (`app/inv.php`): `line_doc()` (who may link / create), `line_free()` (free-text lines of a purchase order, requisition or BOM in one shape), `line_use_item()`, `line_new_item()`, `line_sim_card()`; actions `line_use_item`, `line_new_item` (kind po | pr | bom). Views: `purchase_order.php` (replaces the R97-106 block), `requisition.php`, `bom.php`.
- Use: whoever may edit the document; Procurement also on a submitted / approved requisition and an issued / approved BOM. Only lines without item, and for requisitions without ordered / received / issued quantities.
- New item: procurement administrators (`pr_admin()`, for BOMs also `bom_pr_can()`); code typed or the line's part number; an existing code is refused (`stk.code_taken`); confirmation when similar items exist.
- BOM import review: "New inventory item" disabled for others; `bom_import_apply()` turns such a decision into a free-text line.

**System Budget BOM review** (`views/system_budget_bom.php`): proposals under each row; Use fills `code[row]` and ticks Accept.

**Matching** — `stk_by_code_tail()`: the single item whose code is "prefix.<part number>" (five characters or more with a digit; none when several items share it). Used by `bom_import_stage()`, `bom_row_exact()` and `sb_match_row()` as `part_no_norm`, 90 %, reason `bom.imp_r_tail`.

**Maker part numbers from our codes** (`stk_pn_fill_plan()`, `stk_pn_fill_apply()`, `stk_pn_fill_undo()`, card on `inventory_import&pn=1`, actions `stk_pn_fill`, `stk_pn_undo`): per prefix — items to fill, and the share of items of that prefix whose existing part number equals the code after the dot; pre-ticked from 80 % with at least five known. Written only into empty fields; tails with spaces, without a digit or shorter than four characters are left out; the ids of the last fill are kept in `storage/private/pn_fill_last.json` for the undo (only where the value is still the written one). Not done blindly because on the sandbox inventory the tail equals the existing part number for 71 % of the items only — prefixes such as CBL, OEM, STM are our own descriptive codes.

**Correction to §390**: "for 34 of the other 35 lines an inventory item existed" was wrong — a similar item existed; the same item only for about ten. With the tail matching 54 of the 81 lines of that BOM match (55 after the fill); the rest are variants that are not in the inventory, for which the nearest items are shown.

**Tests (sandbox, full inventory) — forms posted; browser for the budget review**: fill preview (467 prefixes, 87 pre-ticked, 1 151 items), apply, examples checked, undo back to the starting count; requisition of a staff member without procurement rights: card with Use and no New column, forced New refused, Use links and keeps the typed text; Procurement on the same requisition: New without code and part number refused, existing code refused, new code creates and links; BOM draft line: card, Use, forced post from HR refused; purchase order: card, New with the part number as code, supplier kept as preferred; System Budget review: Use fills the code and Accept, applied, part linked; PHP error log clean; page sweep. Not tested: an engineer account in the BOM import review (the rule is in `bom_import_apply()`); Procurement converting a line on an approved BOM.

**Security review**: creating inventory codes is now limited to Procurement on every path; the two generic actions check the document's own edit rule and the line's state on the server. The fill tool writes only empty fields, is limited to procurement administrators and can be undone. No proposal.

## §392 v1.181.0 (R97-108) — One path to purchase orders (release 2)

**Purpose of an order** — migration r98j `install/migrate_cp_r97_108_po_purpose.sql`: `po_orders.purpose` ENUM project | stock | use, default project; existing orders without project and sub-project become stock. `PO_PURPOSE`, `po_purpose_has()`, `po_purpose_pill()`; `po_save_from_post()`: project → the project is required (`po.need_project`); stock / use → project and sub-project are emptied; an order that carries lines of a project requisition cannot leave Project (`po.purpose_pr`); a form that posts no purpose (the To buy basket) gets project when a project is given, else stock. Views: select in the header form of `purchase_order.php`, pill in the title, filter and pill in `purchase_orders.php` (`po_list()` filter `purpose`).

**BOM workbook into a requisition** — `prq_import_bom()` (action `prq_import_bom`, draft requisition, whoever may edit it): `bom_file_rows()` + `bom_row_exact()`; free-text rows keep the price of the file as estimate; the R97-107 card proposes inventory items.

**Requisition → draft purchase orders** — `prq_po_can()`, `prq_pos()`, `prq_po_plan()`, `prq_to_po()` (action `prq_to_po`, procurement administrators, approved requisition): open quantity = qty − reserved − ordered; lines already on a draft order are not offered again; supplier = preferred, else last, else supplier of the maker, else the chosen fallback; `sup_po_check()` per supplier; lines through the existing `po_line_from_pr()`; purpose from the requisition (stock → stock). Card `#to-po` on `views/requisition.php`.

**Sub-project Procurement tab** (`views/_procurement_tab.php`): RFQs of the sub-project listed with its orders.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: orders created with purpose stock (project posted with it is dropped), use, project; project without a project refused; no purpose posted with / without a project; pill and select on the order page; list filter for each purpose; requisition of a staff member: BOM file of 69 rows imported, submitted, approved twice; to-orders card for Procurement only (staff: no card, forced post 403); without a supplier refused with the right message; with a fallback supplier one draft order with 69 lines linked to the requisition, shown in the sub-project tab; second press: nothing left to order; purpose change of that order refused; PHP error log clean; page sweep. Not tested: several suppliers in one requisition (the sandbox items have no preferred supplier); an RFQ in the sub-project tab (no test RFQ); the purpose on an order awarded from an RFQ stays the default Project.

**Security review**: the two new actions check on the server the same rules as the pages show (who may edit a draft requisition; procurement administration for orders); the purpose cannot be used to hide a project order — orders with project requisition lines stay Project. No proposal.

## §393 v1.182.0 (R97-109) — Warehouse: received per project and vessel; delivery notes from a selection (release 3)

**Page** `views/warehouse_ready.php` (`warehouse_ready` in the `$views` list of `index.php`; `dn_can()` to see, `dn_wh()` to issue), linked from Delivery notes and from the Procurement tab of a sub-project.

**Rows** — `whr_rows()` in `app/dn.php`:
- `pr`: lines with an inventory item of approved / closed project requisitions. Ready = `pr_lines.qty_reserved` (goods received on an order for a requisition line are reserved at receipt; stock covering a line is reserved at approval), sent = `qty_issued`, to come = qty − ready − sent; origin `order` when `qty_received` > 0, else `stock`.
- `po`: lines of project purchase orders (not draft / cancelled) without a requisition line, or without an item. Received = `qty_accepted`, sent = quantities on issued delivery notes with that `po_line_id`, ready = received − sent, to come = qty − received.
- Filters vessel (through the project), project, sub-project; show ready | open | all. Up to 3 000 lines per source.

**Delivery notes** — `whr_create_from_post()` (action `whr_create`, form with `data-pack`): the ticked keys (`pr-<id>` / `po-<id>`) are read again from the database, each quantity is checked against what is ready now, lines are grouped by project, sub-project (or project only) and warehouse, and each group goes to the existing `dn_create()` — requisition lines through `prq_issue_dn()` (stock out, reservation released, line and requisition status), order lines as stock out with the delivery-note reference, free-text lines without movement. All notes of one press in one transaction.

**Cancel** — `dn_cancel()`: for requisition lines the returned quantity is reserved again (`pr_lines.qty_reserved`, `inv_stock.qty_reserved`) and a closed requisition is approved again, so the goods are ready for another note. Before, they came back as free stock and the line showed neither ready nor sent.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted; browser for the selection**: test case with a requisition covered by stock, a requisition bought on an order (partial receipt), a direct project order with an item line and a free-text line; page for ready / open / all; in the browser: four lines ticked with a partial quantity → two notes (one per sub-project), stock, reservations and requisition lines as expected, page afterwards shows sent and to come; refused: quantity above ready, nothing ticked, a line already sent; staff without procurement rights 403, HR redirected, forced posts refused; one note per project across two sub-projects (requisitions closed); filters; cancel of that note: stock and reservations back, requisitions approved again, lines ready again; links present; PHP error log clean; page sweep. Not tested: several warehouses; a few thousand lines.

**Security review**: quantities and line ownership are never taken from the browser — only the ids and the quantity to send, re-checked against the database; issuing needs the warehouse right. No proposal.

## §394 v1.182.1 (R97-110) — Budget pricing requests: priced ones hidden by default

`views/system_budgets_proc.php`: tabs `show=waiting` (default) | `done` | `all` with counts over `sbp_queue()`; the list is filtered in the view, the query is unchanged. Empty waiting tab: `sbp.q_none_waiting`.

**Tests (sandbox)**: one budget waiting and one priced — each tab, an invalid value (falls back to waiting), nothing waiting; PHP error log clean; page sweep.

**Security review**: read-only filter on a page that already checks `sbp_require()`. No proposal.

## §395 v1.183.0 (R97-111) — New item code = prefix.<maker part number>

**Rule stated by Nikos**: a new inventory item is always coded prefix.[maker code]; the import and the proposals must show the maker code and the code to create.

**Engine** (`app/inv.php`): `stk_maker_prefixes()` — lower(maker) → prefix learned from the inventory (at least two items of the maker carry the prefix and it is 60 % of them; the first word of a maker name also finds it when unambiguous). `stk_code_propose(row, sim)` — prefix by maker; else the prefix of a similar item by code / part number (score 70 or more); else the prefix all similar items share; code = prefix.<part number as written>; `exists` when an item already has that code. `stk_code_hint()` renders the line.

**Where**: BOM import review (`views/bom.php`) — hint for every row not matched at 95 % or more, field `newcode[row]` for Procurement, passed by action `bom_import_decide` (apply) to `bom_import_apply($bid, $codes)` → `bom_imp_create_item($r, $bid, $want)`; card of free-text lines (`line_sim_card()`) — hint and prefilled field; `line_new_item()` with an empty code uses the proposal.

**Stricter**: a typed code that exists is refused (`stk.code_taken`); with a part number but no known prefix the item is not created (`stk.newcode_none`) — before, the bare part number or BOM-<id>-<seq> was used. Rows without any part number keep the old fallback code.

**Tests (sandbox, full inventory)**: proposals for the unmatched lines of a real switchboard BOM with makers filled (by maker) and not filled (by similar items; none for a maker without any item); review page with 35 fields; apply with an empty field (proposal used), a typed code, a typed code for a maker without prefix, an existing code (refused); requisition card: prefilled field, empty field without prefix refused with the hint, existing code refused, proposal used; PHP error log clean; page sweep. Not tested: an engineer account (sees the hint without the field).

**Security review**: no new input path — the code field goes through the same validation as before (allowed characters, uniqueness) and only Procurement can create. No proposal.

## §396 v1.183.1 (R97-112) — Purchase order: VAT after sending; document totals worked out again

**Report**: "the VAT, when other than 0 %, is not calculated in the PO document". Not reproduced: with a rate in the header of a draft order `po_recalc()` stores subtotal, VAT and total, and `po_pdf()` prints the VAT line (checked in the sandbox, also with the rate changed on an existing draft in a Greek-locale browser). The gap found: once an order is sent `po_editable()` is false, the header form is gone and the rate cannot be set — an order sent at 0 % stays without VAT.

**Changes**: `po_vat_can()`, `po_vat_set()` (action `po_vat`): Procurement sets the rate (0–30) on a sent / confirmed / partial / received purchase order; totals recalculated; event `edit` "VAT a % → b %". Inline form in the read-only header of `views/purchase_order.php`. The PDF route and `po_em_attachment()` call `po_recalc()` first (not for closed / cancelled orders), so stored totals that are out of date cannot reach the document.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: order sent at 0 %, rate set to 24 → page, database, log and PDF (Subtotal 150,00 · VAT 24% 36,00 · Total 186,00); stored totals made wrong by hand → corrected when the PDF is opened; rate 45 refused; staff without procurement rights 403; PHP error log clean; page sweep. Not confirmed: that this was the case on the user's order — number of the order asked.

**Security review**: the action is limited to procurement administrators and to open orders, validates the number and leaves a log line. No proposal.

## §397 v1.183.2 (R97-113) — Work permits: Accounting may create and update

`pmt_can_edit()` (`app/pmt.php`): besides administrators, HR and the project's PM, a staff account with the Accounting flag (`col_flag('is_accounting')`) edits permits of every project. The pages and the actions `pmt_save` / `pmt_delete` already use this one rule. Viewing is unchanged (all staff).

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: staff account that is not the PM of the project: no button, form 403, save refused; same account with the Accounting flag: button, form, permit created and updated; flag removed again: update refused; PHP error log clean; page sweep.

**Security review**: one more role on an existing rule; the QHSE role, which has no financial rights (R97-086), does not get it through this flag. No proposal.

## §398 v1.184.0 (R97-114) — Work permits: several employees under one permit

**Asked**: tick the employees a permit is for and list their names, because each permit concerns specific people only.

**Database** (`install/migrate_cp_r97_114_permit_people.sql`, state key `r98k`): table `work_permit_people (permit_id, person_id)`, primary key on both, index on `person_id`; the single employee of the existing permits is copied in (`INSERT IGNORE … WHERE person_id IS NOT NULL`). The step can run twice.

**Code** (`app/pmt.php`): `pmt_people_has()`, `pmt_people($ids)` (one query for a page of permits), `pmt_with_people()`, `pmt_people_text()`. `pmt_load()` and `pmt_list()` return `people`; filter `person` and search `q` also look in the new table. `pmt_save_from_post()` reads `people[]` when `people_sent` is posted: ids are checked against `hr_people`, at most 300, the old rows are replaced; `work_permits.person_id` keeps the first name (alphabetical) for older screens; an empty Holder takes the names (three, then "+n"). `pmt_delete()` removes the rows. `pmt_remind()` adds the names to the warning text when there are more than one. Before the database step has run, the form shows the former single-employee list.

**Screens**: `views/permit_edit.php` — tick list (`.tick-list` in `assets/portal.css`), filter by name that never hides a ticked person, counter; an employee who has left but is still on the permit stays listed and marked. `views/permits.php` — names under the holder.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: new permit with three ticks and an unknown id (unknown id dropped, holder filled from the names); update to two ticks with a title; list and search by an employee's name; nobody ticked and no holder → refused with the message; free holder only; delete (rows removed); as project manager; in the browser: filter, counter, save. Migration run twice. PHP error log clean; page sweeps as before (admin 9, PM 0, HR 2 flagged — the usual ones).

**Not tested**: the expiry warning e-mail with names on the live server; lists of several hundred employees (the list scrolls; the filter was tried with three).

**Security review**: the ticked ids are cast to integers and accepted only if they exist in the HR registry; the count is limited; only names are read from the registry for this list — no personal or payroll field reaches the page; the edit rule is unchanged and is checked on save. Proposal (no effect on speed): write the change of the people of a permit into the audit trail (who added or removed whom).

## §399 v1.184.1 (R97-115) — Existing inventory code not found on a purchase order

**Reported**: on a purchase order a code that exists in the inventory did not come up; the portal asked to create a new code.

**Cause**: the picker of the purchase order calls `?p=prq_items&supplier=<supplier of the order>&q=…`; `prq_item_search()` passed the supplier to `stk_items()` together with the text, which limits the result to the items of that supplier (preferred / last supplier, a price from him, or a maker he represents — R86-002). Meant to order the list on focus, it also hid every other item when a text was typed. Reproduced in the sandbox: supplier representing maker A, item of maker B → 0 results before, found after.

**Change**
- `prq_item_search()` (`app/prq.php`): with a text, first the items whose code or maker part number equals it (not blocked), then the supplier's matches, then the matches of the whole inventory, without doubles.
- `views/purchase_order.php`, `views/requisition.php`, `views/bom.php`: on `change` of the search box with no item picked, one request; when exactly one result has that code (else that part number), `choose()` is called. A partial code picks nothing.
- `bom_row_exact()` (`app/bom.php`): after the existing rules, the part-number column is tried as our code, and the code column as maker part number (exact, then by code tail).

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: search with the old and the new code (0 → 1 result; the supplier's own items still found); browser: full code typed, field left without clicking, line added and saved with the item; lower-case part number picks the item; partial code picks nothing; `bom_row_exact()` with the code in either column, with punctuation, unknown code → none. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: the live inventory (3 860 items) — in particular an item marked Obsolete is still not offered by the picker, by design.

**Security review**: the endpoint keeps its access rule (staff with requisition or offer-builder rights), the text is bound as a parameter, the result stays limited (20 + at most 5 exact). One more indexed query per search (code is unique-indexed); no proposal.

## §400 v1.184.2 (R97-116) — Price proposed when an item is picked on a purchase order

**Reported**: for parts that have a price, the last price from the inventory is not proposed.

**Found**
1. On the purchase order form `choose()` emptied the price field; `stk_price_proposal()` was applied only in `po_line_save_from_post()` when the field was left empty. The proposal was therefore invisible until after saving.
2. `stk_price_proposal()` reads `inv_prices` only. An item with `inv_items.last_price` set and no history line returned no price (the offer builder already fell back to the card price; orders and requisitions did not).

**Change**
- `stk_price_proposal()` (`app/inv.php`): with no history line and a card price above zero → that price, origin `stk.rule_card` with its date.
- `views/prq_items.php`: `?p=prq_items&price_of=<item>&supplier=<id>` returns `{price, origin, cur, age}`; `{}` unless `stk_price_visible()`.
- `views/purchase_order.php`: on `choose()` of an order (not an RFQ) one request; the field is filled if still empty and the same item is still picked; origin under the field (`#lf-price-h`, key `po.price_prop`).
- `po_line_save_from_post()`: a posted price equal to the proposal keeps the origin of the proposal; another figure is "typed" as before.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: item with card price only (100 → proposed, saved with origin "Last price on the item card"); item with history (49.9, origin with date); same item with the figure changed (saved as typed); item without any price (field empty, nothing under it); endpoint as administrator and as a project manager. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: the live inventory and the pricing rules set there (mean / lowest / same supplier); an account without the right to see prices was not available in the sandbox — the rule is the existing `stk_price_visible()`.

**Security review**: the new answer gives one price only to accounts that already see buying prices in the same picker; item id and supplier id are cast to integers; no price is added for other roles. No proposal.

## §401 v1.184.3 (R97-117) — Payables: payment proof attached to the e-mail by itself

**Asked**: the e-mail of a payable payment must take the attachment automatically.

**Found**: `views/payable.php` built the remittance card with `'atts' => []`; the proof stored by `si_pay_from_post()` (`si_payments.file_id`) was never offered. `em_card_html()` ticks only the first attachment.

**Change**
- `views/payable.php`: `$payAtts` — one entry `f:<file id>` per payment that has a proof, newest first, label `si.pay_att` (date · amount); passed to the card with `'atts_all' => true`.
- `em_card_html()` (`app/em.php`): option `atts_all` ticks every attachment (other cards unchanged).
- Payment form: hint under "Payment document" (`si.pay_doc_hint`, or `si.pay_doc_nop` when the invoice has no project); action `si_pay`: red message `si.pay_doc_lost` when a file was chosen and not kept.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: invoice with project, two payments each with a PDF → both listed and ticked on the card; `em_attachment('f:<id>')` returns the file; invoice without project → hint shown, payment recorded, file not kept. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: sending through Microsoft 365 on the live server (the sandbox has no mail connection); the red message was posted but its text was not read back on the page.

**Open point**: an invoice without project cannot keep a payment proof (`cp_store_file()` needs a project). Proposal: a storage place for documents of Accounting that do not belong to a project — needs a decision on who may see it.

**Security review**: only files already stored as payment proofs of this invoice are offered; the attachment key is resolved on the server at send time, staff only, as for the other cards. The proof is an internal file that now leaves to the supplier's address chosen by the sender — it is ticked by default but visible in the card before sending. No further proposal.

## §402 v1.185.0 (R97-118) — Payables: statement Excel / PDF / e-mail, supplier proforma; inventory items out of the valuation

**Asked**: (1) the Excel button of Payables puts erroneous data on the screen; (2) choose between a proforma to prepay, with the sales invoice assigned when it arrives, or a sales invoice directly; (3) the statement as PDF like the other reports, with e-mail to the supplier; (4) some inventory items not in the valuation, chosen per item.

**Database** (`install/migrate_cp_r97_118_si_proforma.sql`, state key `r98l`): `si_invoices.is_proforma` (0 invoice · 1 proforma waiting for the invoice · 2 proforma with the invoice assigned), `proforma_no`, `final_at`, `final_name`; `inv_items.no_valuation`.

**1 Excel** — `views/payables.php` built the workbook in the middle of the page, after the layout had been sent, so the browser showed the bytes. The files are now produced before `layout_head()`. The opening row had one cell too few; fixed in `si_statement_sheets()`, which also adds a totals row.

**2 Proforma** (`app/si.php`): `si_pf_has()`, `si_is_pf()`, `si_prepaid()`, `si_pf_pill()`. Field Document (`is_proforma`) on a new or still editable invoice; button "Register proforma (prepayment)" (`payable&id=0&proforma=1`). For a prepaid document `si_lines_from_po()`, `si_po_open_lines()` and `si_match()` work on ordered minus invoiced instead of accepted minus invoiced. `si_final_from_post()` (action `si_final`): number (unique per supplier), date, optional file; the old number goes to `proforma_no`, `is_proforma` becomes 2, the sub-project cost row is refreshed, event `final`. List filter `status=proforma`; statement kind `proforma`.

**3 Statement** — `si_statement_doc()`, `si_statement_pdf()` (letterhead of the default branch, A4), `si_em_attachment()` with key `ss:<supplier>:<from|->:<to>:pdf|xlsx` resolved in `em_attachment()`; e-mail kind `sup_statement` (`app/et.php`, texts `et.k_/d_s_/d_b_sup_statement`) so the wording can be changed in Settings › E-mail templates.

**4 Valuation** (`app/inv.php`): `stk_nv_has()`, `stk_nv()`; tick on the item form (`nv_sent` + `no_valuation`); `stk_valuation()` leaves the value of such items out of the totals and of the per-category table and returns `excluded` / `value_excluded`; list total, list filter `nv=1`, Excel value column.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: order sent, nothing received → proforma registered with the ordered line, checked (match OK), paid with a proof, empty number refused, invoice assigned with a file, second assignment refused, events and sub-project cost reference updated; list filter; statement page, Excel (opens, 9 columns, totals), PDF (rendered and looked at), with and without a from-date; e-mail card with both attachments. Item ticked → valuation 1 200 → 0 with the line "1 item left out", list filter and mark; unticked → back. Error log clean; sweeps as usual.

**Not tested**: sending the statement through Microsoft 365; a proforma in a payment request above the Management threshold; partial receipts after a proforma was checked (the received goods are then already counted as invoiced — intended); the health report or other reports that might quote an inventory value were not searched beyond the inventory pages.

**Security review**: the statement files are served only to accounts with payables rights (`si_can()` inside `si_statement_doc()`, also at e-mail time); dates and ids are validated; PDF sent with `nosniff` and `no-store`. Assigning an invoice needs payables rights and is logged with user and time. The valuation tick is limited to Procurement administrators. Proposal (no effect on speed): require a second person (Accounting or Management) when an invoice is assigned to a proforma that is already paid, as is done for payments above the threshold.

## §403 v1.185.1 (R97-119) — Idle time-out: the open page goes to the sign-in page

**Reported**: the portal stayed open for 45 minutes without signing out.

**Found**: the server does end the session after `sec_idle_minutes` (default 30; Settings › Login history) — `session_boot()` checks it on every request and the heartbeat `?p=ping` does not extend it. No background request keeps a session alive (the weather badge calls an outside service; `ping`, `bg`, `csp_report` aside). But the heartbeat in `assets/portal.js` only stopped itself on the answer 401, so the page stayed on the screen, readable, until the next click. If the live value of the idle time is above 45 minutes, the session was simply still valid — check the value in Settings › Login history.

**Change**
- `assets/portal.js` (presence heartbeat): on 401 → `location.replace('index.php?p=login')` (the sign-in page shows `login.idle`). One extra heartbeat is sent when the idle time of the page runs out (`data-idle` + 5 s) and one whenever the tab becomes visible again.
- `views/_layout.php`: `<body data-idle="<seconds>">` for signed-in pages.

**Tests (sandbox)**: browser — active session, tab looked at again → stays; session file aged by 40 minutes on the server → the open page went to the sign-in page with the message; no script error. PHP error log clean.

**Not tested**: the real 30-minute wait; a phone browser that suspends timers in the background (the check then runs when the tab is shown again).

**Security review**: closes an exposure — content of an expired session no longer stays on an unattended screen. Cost: one small request per page per idle period and one per tab activation. Proposals (no effect on speed): (1) set the idle time for administrators and Accounting lower than for the rest; (2) a warning one minute before the time-out with "Stay signed in".

## §404 v1.185.2 (R97-120) — Quality: no financial data; Milestones and Safety log

**Asked**: Quality should not see financial data and should have access to Project Milestones and Safety Log.

**Found**: `fin_blocked()` (R97-086) recognised Quality only by the staff-role name `qhse|hse|ehs|quality`, while QHSE access (`QH_RX`) also accepts `qa|qc`; access by discipline or through the QHSE team was not covered at all. The Milestones / Safety log page (`project_ms`) could be read by staff but edited only by administrators, Management and project managers, and was reached only through a project.

**Change**
- `app/pm.php`: `FIN_QRX` (adds `qa`, `qc`, Greek forms); `fin_blocked()` also true through `fin_blocked_more()`: discipline name matching the same words, or membership of `qh_team()` unless administrator / Management. Everything already hanging on `fin_blocked()` follows (router `FIN_PAGES`, `fin_visible()`, `stk_price_visible()`, `sb_can()`, `col_can()`, accounting flags, `pi_is_sales()`).
- `app/xs.php`: `xs_quality()`; `xs_can_edit()` true for Quality; `xs_projects()`.
- `views/project_ms.php`: without a project → chooser of the running projects (milestones, not yet achieved, safety entries, last entry).
- `views/_layout.php`: QHSE menu item "Milestones & safety log" for accounts with QHSE access.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: one staff account in four set-ups — role Engineering (offers / pipeline open, no menu item); role "QA/QC Engineer"; discipline "Quality Control"; member of the QHSE team → offers, pipeline, payables redirected to the dashboard, menu item present, safety entry and milestone saved. Administrator named in the team keeps the pages. Test role, discipline, team and entries removed afterwards. Error log clean; sweeps as usual.

**Not tested**: the live roles and disciplines (their names decide who is Quality); every single amount on every page was not walked again — the rule is the one of R97-086, now reached by more accounts.

**Security review**: this narrows access. A position whose name contains QA / QC as a separate word now loses financial data even if it is not meant to be Quality — check the list of staff roles. Proposal (no effect on speed): replace the name rule by a tick "No financial data" on the staff role and the discipline, so the choice is explicit and shown in Administration.

## §405 v1.186.0 (R97-121) — HR › Job descriptions

**Asked**: a feature in HR to create job descriptions; optimise the texts of the fourteen attached documents and integrate them; AI assistance to create new ones; the job description of the discipline on each vacancy; linked to each employee by discipline; PDF; approval by Discipline Managers, General Manager and CEO.

**Database** (`install/migrate_cp_r97_121_job_descriptions.sql`, state key `r98m`): `hr_jds` (text, state, revision, the three approvals), `hr_jd_revs` (approved issues as JSON), `hr_jd_acks` (read by employee per revision), `hr_people.jd_id`, `rc_vacancies.jd_id`.

**Code**
- `app/jd.php`: access `jd_manage()` (HR, administrators, Management), `jd_can_view()`, `jd_list()`; writing `jd_save_from_post()`, `jd_delete()`; approval `jd_step_users()`, `jd_steps()`, `jd_submit()`, `jd_approve()`, `jd_finish()`, `jd_reject()`, `jd_revise()`, `jd_retire()`; the issue in force `jd_content()`, `jd_current()`, `jd_revs()`; employees `jd_assign_from_post()`, `jd_person_set()`, `jd_mine()`, `jd_ack()`; vacancies `jd_vacancy_fields()`; set `jd_seed()`; assistant `jd_ai_draft()` (kind `fill`, page `hr_jd` or `rcr_vacancy`); `jd_pdf()`; `jd_handle_action()` for the `jd_*` actions.
- `app/jd_seed.php`: the fourteen descriptions in one structure. Source: the documents of November 2026; duplicates merged (two versions of the marine electrical design engineer folded into the three levels), advertisement wording, benefits and the general clauses taken out of the individual texts (the clauses are printed once, from the language files), DNVGL → DNV, the Greek facilities text translated.
- Views: `hr_jds.php`, `hr_jd.php`, `my_jd.php`, `_jd_body.php`; card on `hr_person.php`; card, hidden field and link on `rcr_vacancy.php` (`rcr_actions.php` stores `jd_id`).
- Lists: `$views`, `HR_PAGES` / `HR_ACTIONS`, `EMP_PAGES` / `EMP_ACTIONS`, `PA_PAGES` / `PA_ACTIONS`; menus in `_layout.php`; `ai_all_pages()` gets `hr_jd`.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: set loaded as HR (14 drafts, second press 0); a non-HR account refused on save and submit; edit with bullets and numbers stripped; submit; edit refused in approval; CEO refused before the Discipline Manager; Discipline Manager then CEO → approved, issue R0 kept (no General Manager account in the sandbox → step skipped); employees ticked, unknown id dropped, employee card; employee page, confirmation, PDF with name; discipline manager sees only his discipline and gets 403 on another; revision R1 while R0 stays in force; send-back without and with reason; delete refused when approved, done for a fresh draft; retire and back; vacancy prefilled from the description and saved with the link; PDFs rendered and looked at; pages in the browser. Assistant: the request reached the API and the error was shown (the sandbox has no valid key).

**Not tested**: a real answer of the assistant; the General Manager step with a real account; the HR-only and employee-only menus in the browser (their page and action lists were extended, the pages were opened as administrator, HR officer and engineer).

**Security review**: the texts are role descriptions, no personal data; the assistant receives the brief and the form fields only. Approvals are tied to the account (discipline manager id, staff role names General Manager / Managing Director, CEO / Owner scope) and checked on the server at each press; an administrator cannot sign for somebody else. An employee reaches only the issue in force of his or her own description. PDFs are sent `no-store` / `nosniff`. Proposal (no effect on speed): a weekly line in the HR dashboard of employees without a job description or who have not confirmed the current revision.

## §406 v1.187.0 (R97-122) — The HR year: annual flow on the HR dashboard, warnings, weekly e-mail

**Asked**: integrate the HR-year planning (Jan targets and entitlements · Feb training plan · Apr / Jul / Oct quarterly target review, Jul summer leave groups · Nov targets closed, evaluations drafted · Dec HR reviews, company closures · 31 Jan evaluations acknowledged) in HR, show the annual flow on the dashboard and warn the HR Manager of delays by notifications and weekly e-mails.

**Database** (`install/migrate_cp_r97_122_hr_year.sql`, state key `r98n`): `hr_year_steps (year, step_key, done_at, done_by, done_name, note)` — the ticks only; everything else is read from the existing tables.

**Code** (`app/hry.php`)
- `HRY_STEPS` (twelve steps, month 13 = January of the next year); `hry_window()`, `hry_staff()` (registry, no contractors).
- `hry_measure()` per step: personal targets per employee (`hr_targets`), annual entitlement per employee (`hr_entitlements`), trainings of the year (`hr_trainings`, never automatic), targets with a log line in the quarter (`hr_target_log`), leave periods over 15 Jun – 15 Sep, targets closed, evaluations by state (`hr_evaluations`), mandatory periods over 15 Dec – 10 Jan applied (`hr_leave_periods`).
- `hry_cycle()` → state done / late (days) / due / soon / later; `hry_attention()` over the current and the previous cycle (a previous year with nothing in the portal is not reported); `hry_cycle_started()`.
- `hry_tick_from_post()` (action `hry_tick`), `hry_handle_action()` (`hry_tick`, `hry_mail`).
- `hry_recipients()`, `hry_weekly_build()`, `hry_weekly()` (settings `hry_weekly`, `hry_weekly_on`), `hry_sweep()` once a day (setting `hry_day`), registered in `nf_sweep()` as job `hr-year`.
- `hry_card_html()`: HR view on `hr_dashboard.php` (figures, names missing, ticks, mail switch), plain calendar on `my_leave.php`. Styles `.hry-*` in `assets/portal.css`.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: card on the dashboard with the red line; tick with note, undo, unknown step refused, non-HR account refused; mail switch off / on and "send now" (text built, transport absent in the sandbox → 0 of 1 sent, reported on the page); daily sweep run three times from the command line: one notification for the late steps, one per step due, no doubles on the second run, nothing on the third; e-mail text read; employee calendar on My leave; screenshots looked at. Error log clean; sweeps as usual.

**Not tested**: delivery of the e-mail through Microsoft 365; the turn of the year (January card of the previous cycle) with real data; a registry of several hundred employees (about thirty small queries per dashboard view; nothing is computed on other pages).

**Security review**: the e-mail and the notifications carry counts only; names of employees still missing are shown on the HR dashboard alone (HR and Owner). Ticks and the mail switch need HR rights, checked on the server; the step key is validated against the fixed list. Proposal (no effect on speed): send the managers of the disciplines a reminder in November listing how many of their people still have no evaluation draft — counts only.

## §407 v1.187.1 (R97-123) — People who left stay out; released from the portal; session duration in the login history

**Reported**: an employee who resigned still appears in the name days of the dashboard — check that a person who is terminated, left or resigned is unregistered everywhere. **Asked**: record the session duration of each user in the login history, to measure the use of the portal by the personnel.

**Found (leavers)**: celebrations, the lists of personnel and notifications all read `hr_people.is_active`; the end event (`hr_employment_event_from_post()`, `ev = end`) sets it to 0 and closes the staff account, so the path itself was right. Two other writers could set it back to 1 with the employment still ended: `hr_people_import_card()` wrote `left_date = <file>` and `is_active = <file has a leaving date ? 0 : 1>` for every imported row — an Excel update with the column "Left on" empty re-activated the person and wiped the leaving date; `hr_person_save_from_post()` took `is_active` from the tick box of the card. The live data was not read; the fault was reproduced in the sandbox by the state those writers leave (active, no leaving date, closed period) and the person re-appeared in the celebrations.

**Change (leavers)** — `app/hr3.php`: `hr_employment_ended()`, `hr_leavers_reconcile()` (active card + closed period + no open period + end date reached → `is_active = 0`, `left_date`, audit line, release), `hr_leaver_release()` (staff account, `sec_revoke_all()`, `plan_entries` after the last day, pending `hr_requests` after the last day, `qh_team` / `ism_team` / `ism_mteam`), `hr_leaver_holds()` and `hr_leavers_holding()` (discipline manager, manager of accounts, PM of running projects, open tasks, hiring manager, open administrator account). Called: at the end event, after `hr_people_import_apply()`, once a day (`nf_sweep()` job `hr-leavers`, setting `hr_lv_day`). `app/hr2.php`: the import keeps `left_date` and never re-activates an ended employment; the card save refuses "Active" for an ended employment (`hr.lv_still_left`). Views: card on `hr_person.php` (tab Employment), list on `hr_dashboard.php`; line in the health report (`app/diag.php`, count only).

**Change (sessions)** — migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_123_login_sessions.sql` (state key `r98o`): `login_sessions (user_id, started_at, last_at, ended_at, end_reason, ip, device)`. `app/auth.php`: `ls_start()` in `finish_login()`, `ls_touch()` in `current_user()` (at most once a minute, not for `ping` / `bg` / `csp_report` / `relock`), `ls_end()` in `sec_end_session()` and at sign-out (a time-out ends the session at its last activity), `ls_usage()`, `ls_recent()`, `ls_dur()`. `views/admin_log.php`: card "Portal usage" (7 / 30 / 90 / 365 days, per person, sessions of one person).

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: test employee with account, planning, a pending request, QHSE team, discipline manager → end event (resignation): card Left, account closed, sessions revoked, future planning and request removed, team list cleaned, card and dashboard list "Manager of disciplines: 1"; fault state restored by hand → shown in celebrations → daily correction → gone, second run nothing; card saved with Active ticked → stays Left with the message. Sessions: sign-in opens a row; heartbeat does not move the time, a page does; sign-out, idle time-out (ends at last activity), new sign-in; report as administrator, 403 for staff. Sandbox restored from a dump. Error log clean; sweeps as usual.

**Not tested**: the live registry (which of the two writers re-activated the person reported); the Excel update itself was changed but not re-run with a file; app sign-ins (the phone app does not open a row in `login_sessions`).

**Security review**: leaving now also ends every open session and app token of the person at once. Session rows hold the account, times, the address and the browser family — no content; the report is for administrators only and the staff handbook states that the time signed in is recorded. Proposals (no effect on speed): (1) delete session rows older than 24 months; (2) show each person his or her own sessions on My account.

## §408 v1.188.0 (R97-124) — Equipment given to employees: issue list accepted by the employee, handover list accepted by HR
