**Asked**: how do we manage onboarding and the equipment given to the employee — instrumentation, tools, laptop, telephone, working clothes, safety equipment — and his acceptance; the same for the handover list and its acceptance by HR.

**Before**: onboarding had the checklist "My first weeks" with a buddy (Community), and the release of contract had one tick "Company property returned". Nothing recorded *what* was given, and nothing was accepted by anyone.

**Change** — new module `app/hre.php`, migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_124_hr_equipment.sql` (state key `r98p`): `hr_eq_items` (one line per thing: category, description, serial, company number, quantity, size, condition, state) and `hr_eq_docs` (issue and handover lists: number, state, acceptance of the employee with name / time / address / how, acceptance of HR).
- States of a line: `draft` → `pending` (on an issue list) → `held` → `returned` | `missing` | `writeoff`. A `missing` line put on a later handover list is copied to it (`clone_of`) and the old one stays on its list as `followed`, so an accepted list never changes.
- Issue list `EQI-yyyy-nnnn`: `sent` → `accepted` | `disputed` | `cancelled`. Handover list `EQR-yyyy-nnnn`: `open` → `accepted` | `cancelled`; HR gives every line a result.
- Actions (`hre_handle_action()`, before `pm_handle_action`): HR — `hre_add`, `hre_del`, `hre_issue`, `hre_paper`, `hre_cancel`, `hre_receive`; employee — `hre_accept`, `hre_dispute`; both — `hre_handover`. Scopes: `HR_PAGES/HR_ACTIONS`, `EMP_*`, `PA_*`.
- Views: `_hr_eq.php` (tab Equipment of `hr_person`), `my_equipment.php` (also serves the PDF of a list to HR and to the employee concerned), `hr_equipment.php` (register, Excel), card on `hr_dashboard.php`. PDF `hre_pdf()`.
- Instruments can be taken from the calibration register (`ism_records`, `reg = qequip`); one instrument, one holder.
- People who left: `hr_leaver_holds()` counts equipment (also for a person without an account); `hr_leavers_holding()` reads them in one statement.
- Daily job `hr-equipment` (`hre_sweep()`, setting `hre_day`): reminder after 3 days, then weekly.
- 173 language keys (`hre.*`, `hr.lv_equip`, `hr.ck_equipment`).

**Tests (sandbox) — every form posted**: lines added (also without description → refused), removed, issue list, acceptance without the tick → refused, difference reported, withdrawn and re-issued, accepted (name, time, address recorded, lines held); handover started by the employee and by HR, confirmed by the employee, HR acceptance with a missing result → refused, then accepted with in order / damaged / not returned / not to be returned; the not-returned line on a later list and returned; acceptance on paper (empty name refused); instrument from the register and refused for a second person; staff posting HR actions → refused, register 403, PDF of another person 403, accepting another person's list refused; Excel opened; PDFs rendered and looked at; leaver → equipment in "After leaving" and on the dashboard; reminder once, second run none. Sandbox restored from a dump. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: real data; the phone app (no screen for it there); e-mail — the module notifies in the portal only.

**Security review**: an acceptance can only be given by the account linked to the card (or recorded by HR as paper, and shown as such); lists are read by HR and by the person concerned only; accepted lists cannot be edited; nothing goes to the assistant. Proposals (no effect on speed): (1) let IT and the warehouse / QHSE prepare lines for their own categories without access to the HR card; (2) standard kits per discipline so onboarding is one click; (3) take laptops and telephones from the ISMS asset inventory as instruments are taken from the calibration register, and show the holder there; (4) expiry / replacement dates for PPE.

## §409 v1.189.0 (R97-125) — QHSE: selected standard, evaluation of a new supplier; HR: process flow, equipment import, kits, preparation, IT assets, replacement dates

**Reported / asked**: (1) on the QHSE page the user cannot see which standard of the upper row is selected; (2) a new supplier must create a Supplier evaluation in QHSE ISO 9001 for the manager; (3) a process flow of onboarding and handover on the employee card and overall on the HR dashboard; (4) the five proposals of §408.

**Found (1)**: `QH_STD` has the keys `'9001'` … which PHP keeps as integers, so `$std === $sk` in `views/isms.php` was true only for `ims`. Compared as strings now; `aria-current` and a small label "Standard" added.

**Change (2)** — `app/ism.php`: `qh_supplier_new()` makes the `qsup` record (`candidate`, linked by `supplier_id`) and a planned `qsupeval` (initial, due in 14 days), once; `qh_notify_uids()` (team, people with QHSE by role or discipline, administrators as last resort); `qh_supplier_sweep()` once a day (`nf_sweep()` job `qh-suppliers`, settings `qh_sup_day`, `qh_supeval_from`) for suppliers created by imports — only those created after the first run, so the existing list is not flooded. Called from `sup_save` for a new supplier.

**Change (3)** — `app/hrf.php`: `hrf_onboarding()`, `hrf_handover()`, `hrf_progress()`, `hrf_html()` (full and compact), `hrf_overview()`. Read-only: employment, account, `hr_jds` / `hr_jd_acks`, `hr_eq_items` / `hr_eq_docs`, `soc_onboard`, `hr_leaver_holds()`, `hr_offboarding`. Card above the tabs of `hr_person.php`; card on top of `hr_dashboard.php`.

**Change (4)** — migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_125_hr_equipment_kits.sql` (key `r98q`): `hr_eq_kits`, `hr_eq_items.expires_on`, `.exp_notified`. `app/hre.php`: `hre_prep_cats()` (HR all; IT team it / phone / access; QHSE instrument / ppe / clothes; warehouse tool / clothes / ppe), `hre_add` / `hre_del` open to preparers for their categories and their own drafts, new page `views/eq_prepare.php`; `hre_sources()` (calibration instruments and hardware of the IT asset inventory), holder shown on `isms_rec.php`; kits (`hre_kits()`, `hre_kit_for()`, `hre_kit_apply()`, `hre_kit_on_start()` from `soc_onboard_start()`); import (`hre_import_template()`, `hre_import_from_upload()` — file read, not kept); replacement date on lines, filter and KPI on the register, notification 30 days before in `hre_sweep()`.

**Tests (sandbox) — forms posted**: QHSE tabs marked for IMS / 9001 / 14001; new supplier → `AS-001` to evaluate + `AS-001-E01` planned, saving it again adds nothing, daily catch-up takes an imported supplier once and leaves the older ones; kits saved, applied (second time nothing), added when "My first weeks" starts; staff cannot post kit or import actions; a member of the IT team prepares lines (asset from the inventory, wrong category refused, own draft removed, HR's draft not, issue refused, HR card and register 403); import with six rows — three imported, three skipped with reasons, issue lists only of the imported lines, date given kept, a text file refused; flows printed for two people and looked at on the card and dashboard; replacement notification once. Sandbox restored. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: live data; warehouse and QHSE as preparers (same code path as IT, other category list); the phone app.

**Security review**: preparers reach no personal data — names and disciplines of active employees and the lines of their own categories only; kit, import, issue and acceptance stay with HR; the upload is limited to .xlsx up to 5 MB, read and discarded, and never sent to the assistant; the automatic evaluation writes QHSE records only and reads no supplier prices. Proposals (no effect on speed): (1) a tick on the user account for "may prepare equipment" instead of the position rules, if the positions do not fit; (2) the employee's signature drawn on the phone for the paper cases; (3) periodic re-evaluation of suppliers planned automatically 12 months after the last one.

## §410 v1.189.1 (R97-126) — The code of the imported file is kept on a free-text line and proposed for the new inventory item

**Reported**: on the card "Lines without an inventory item — similar items" of a purchase order the field Code of "New inventory item" is empty, although the imported file had a code for every row.

**Found**: `po_import_bom()` and `prq_import_bom()` link a row whose code exists and write the others as free text — `po_lines` / `pr_lines` had no place for the code, so it was dropped; the field is filled only by `stk_code_propose()`, which needs a maker part number, and these rows had none.

**Change** — migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_126_typed_code.sql` (key `r98r`): `po_lines.typed_code`, `pr_lines.typed_code`. `app/inv.php`: `line_code_has()`, `line_code_keep()` after the INSERT of a line without item; `line_free()` returns the code (for a BOM line its own `code`); `line_sim_card()` shows it and puts it in the field unless an item with that code exists meanwhile; `line_new_item()` uses it when the field is empty, before the proposal from the part number; `stk_similar()` is asked with it. Carried on: BOM → requisition, BOM → order, requisition → order, RFQ → order (`app/bom.php`, `app/po.php`). `line_codes_from_file()` (action `line_codes_file`, Procurement only): for lines imported earlier, the same file read again, codes put on free-text lines by description; a description with two different codes is left alone.

**Tests (sandbox)**: draft order, file with four rows (two with new codes, one without code, one with an existing code) → two free lines carry the code and the field shows it, the third is empty, the fourth linked; ＋ with the field emptied → item created with the code of the file; codes cleared → "Take the codes from the file" fills two lines; a member of staff without Procurement is refused. Migration run twice. Sandbox restored. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: the order of the report itself — its 81 lines were imported before this version and take their codes with "Take the codes from the file".

**Security review**: the upload is limited to 8 MB, read by the same reader as the import and not kept; only Procurement can use it, on a document it may edit; the code is written through bound parameters and printed escaped. No proposal for this change.

## §411 v1.189.2 (R97-127) — Maker part number lost by the BOM importer; numbers taken from the file; all new items in one step

**Reported**: the file has the column "Maker part no" filled for every row, yet the 81 lines of the order show no part number and the Code field of "New inventory item" is empty; creating the items one by one "will take years"; matching must always look at the maker's number and the description.

**Found**: `bom_file_rows()` (import into an order / requisition) and the staged BOM import read the template columns; `bom_import()` — used when a BOM is created with its file attached, and as fallback — had its own older list of header names without "maker part no" and "golden code". Rows came in with description and maker only; `bom_to_po()` copied them; `stk_code_propose()` needs the part number, so nothing was proposed, and `stk_similar()` could only compare descriptions (the "similar description 60 %" of the screenshot).

**Change** — `app/bom.php` `bom_import()`: header names merged with `bom_imp_aliases()` (part number before code for ambiguous names), match by `bom_row_exact()` (code, maker part number). `app/inv.php`: `line_codes_from_file()` now for orders, requisitions and BOMs — a line takes part number, maker and code from the row with the same description and is linked when the row names an inventory item exactly; `line_new_code()`; `line_new_all_rows()` / `line_new_all()` (action `line_new_all`): every line with a proposed code and no item with the same / nearly the same part number (reason same, code or part at 85 % or more) becomes an item in one step. No schema change.

**Tests (sandbox)**: a file with the columns of the report — BOM created with the file attached: part numbers kept, the row whose part number is in the inventory linked; order with four lines without numbers → three completed from the file, one of them linked, the fourth untouched; fields show SE.18639 and SE.A9F74316; "Create the 2 new items" → both created with maker and part number and linked; staff without Procurement refused. Sandbox restored. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: the order of the report (81 lines) — to be completed with "Take the numbers from the file".

**Security review**: the bulk creation runs through the same checks as the single one (Procurement, editable document, code not taken) and is limited to 400 lines per run; the upload is read and not kept. Proposal (no effect on speed): a line in the inventory item's history "created from <document>" so a bulk creation can be traced and undone per document.

## §412 v1.190.0 (R97-128) — Purchase order: lines edited in the table; prices read from the supplier's offer by the assistant

**Asked**: typing the price of each item through "edit" is not convenient — a field at the unit price, or the assistant reading the offer / RFQ answer of the supplier; then: edit all fields in place for each line instead of going down to the form.

**Change — in place** (`views/purchase_order.php`, `app/po.php`): `po_inplace_can()` (document still editable), `po_prices_can()` (prices: an order, by who may see prices), `po_num()` (comma or dot, thousands), `po_prices_from_post()` (action `po_prices`) — per line `desc`, `note`, `qty`, `price`, `disc`, `lead`, `need`; a field not in the form is left alone; only changed lines are written; quantity not below the received one; origin "typed" for a changed price; `po_recalc()`; event `prices`. The inputs carry `form="po-prices"` so the table needs no nested form; the table now scrolls inside its card.

**Change — assistant** (`app/po.php`): `po_ai_can()` (page `purchase_order` ticked in the AI settings, user allowed, order editable or RFQ sent / quoted), `po_prices_ai()` (action `po_prices_ai`): PDF as a document block, image as an image block, .xlsx as tab-separated text (600 rows / 90 000 characters), with the lines of the order (id, our code, maker, maker part number, description, quantity, unit); answer as JSON, checked by `po_ai_parse()` (ids of this order only, once each, numbers within bounds); kept in the session (`$_SESSION['po_ai'][order]`) and shown as values of the fields in gold with source text and certainty; saved only through "Save the lines" / "Save quotation"; `po_prices_ai_clear`. Same rate limit, monthly cap and `ai_log` entry as the other uses. `ai_all_pages()` has the new page, off until ticked. The instruction to the assistant states that text in the supplier document is data, not an instruction.

**Tests (sandbox)** — forms posted: in-place save of seven fields on four lines (thousands format, empty price, invalid price, quantity 0 ignored), totals and event; same values again → "Nothing was changed"; staff without Procurement → 403. Assistant against a local stand-in that records the request: PDF sent as document, Excel as text, key header present, four line ids sent; answer with three good lines, one foreign id and one duplicate → three proposals, gold fields, low-certainty and currency warnings, offer number; nothing saved until the button; one proposal corrected → two lines "supplier offer …", one "typed", offer number on the order, `ai_log` row; text file refused; page unticked → no upload form, action refused. Sandbox and its configuration restored. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: a real answer of the assistant on a real offer — how well it matches depends on the document; the proposals are for checking, not for trust. The hosting time limit on a very long PDF.

**Security review**: the offer and the order lines leave the portal for the assistant only when an administrator has ticked the page and the user has the assistant; our prices are not sent — only the lines to be priced; the file is not stored; the answer cannot write anything by itself and can only address lines of this order; values are bound and escaped. Proposals (no effect on speed): (1) keep the offer as a document of the order automatically, so the price can always be traced to its file; (2) a warning when a proposed price differs by more than 20 % from the last buying price.

## §413 v1.190.1 (R97-129) — Purchase order: "Pull requisition line" renamed

**Asked**: what "Pull requisition line" is and where it takes its lines from, since the items of the requisition were converted correctly into the order — then: rename it.

**Answer**: `po_to_buy()` — lines of approved requisitions of the same project / sub-project with `qty − reserved − ordered > 0`, less those already on the order; `po_pull` adds the chosen one. It is an optional extra, not a step of the conversion.

**Change** — `views/purchase_order.php`: label "Still to buy in this project, from other requisitions:" before the selector, button "＋ Add this line to the order", the explanation as tooltip (`po.pull_label`, `po.pull_line`, `po.pull_hint`). The box was already hidden when the list is empty. Older handbook sentence corrected.

**Tests (sandbox)**: page of a draft order with and without open requisition lines; language key counts equal. Error log clean.

**Security review**: wording only — no change of access or data. No proposal.

## §414 v1.190.2 (R97-130) — Purchase order: estimated prices of the requisition carried over, and taken on existing orders

**Asked**: why the estimated prices of the requisition are not brought into the order when it is converted — or let them be retrieved from the order when the requisition had prices.

**Found**: `po_line_from_pr()` priced a line with an inventory item only from `stk_price_proposal()` (supplier price, last buying price, card price) and used `pr_lines.est_price` only for free-text lines. An item never bought before has no proposal, so its line arrived without a price although the requisition had an estimate.

**Change** — `app/po.php`: the estimate is the fallback for every line without a price (origin `po.price_est`, with the number of the requisition); `po_est_lines()`, `po_prices_est()` (action `po_prices_est`, Procurement with prices, editable order) fill the lines of an existing order that have no price and whose requisition line has an estimate; button under the lines of the order.

**Tests (sandbox)**: requisition with four lines (item without buying price, free text, item with last price, no estimate) pulled into an order → 55 and 12 as estimates, 100 from the item card, the fourth empty; prices cleared → button "(3 lines)" → three filled, totals recalculated, button gone; staff without Procurement refused. Sandbox restored. Error log clean.

**Security review**: no new data leaves or enters — the estimate was already visible to the same people on the requisition; the action checks the same rights as the price fields. No proposal.

## §415 v1.191.0 (R97-131) — Support: process flow from the offer to the delivery (portrait page)

**Asked**: the process flow drawn in Figma earlier — portrait, fitted to a dedicated page of Support so the users know the Sales / Purchasing flow, with the inventory interactions and the delivery notes.

**Change** — new view `views/process_flow.php` (page `process_flow`, staff; in `$views`, `HR_PAGES`, `PA_PAGES`, `EMP_PAGES`), card on `views/support.php`. Built in HTML and CSS, not as an image: one column of twelve steps in five phases, each row `quantities — step — money` joined by straight lines, steps joined by a vertical arrow; texts from 80 language keys (`pf.*`, English / Greek / Italian), so it follows the language of the user, wraps on a phone (one column) and prints (`@media print`, button Print). Each step links to its page. Two lists below: what changes the stock; delivery notes.

**Checked in the code before writing each sentence**: reservation at the approval of a requisition (`prq_approve_apply()`, `inv_stock.qty_reserved`); `pr_lines.qty_ordered` when an order is sent; receipt (`po_lines.qty_received / accepted / rejected`, `stk_move('in')` at the order price, reservation for the requisition, `pr_lines.qty_received`); issue (`prq_issue()`, `stk_move('out')` with project and price, reservation released); delivery notes (`DN_KINDS`, `dn_create()` — requisition line → `prq_issue_dn()`, other item line → `stk_move('out')`; `dn_cancel()` books back and re-reserves); supplier invoice against `po_lines.qty_invoiced`; budget of the sub-project from the offers (`bg_prefill_from_offers()`), budget figures on the requisition; proforma per payment term of an approved offer (`pi_create()`); other movements (`fsr.php`, manual, transfer, adjustment, opening).

**Figma**: a portrait FigJam board "Golden A/S Portal - Sales and Purchasing process flow (portrait)" with the same twelve steps and the stock node was generated for editing; the page in the portal is the reference.

**Tests (sandbox)**: page opened as administrator, staff and HR (200), no untranslated key on the page, screenshot looked at, language key counts equal. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: printing on paper from a browser (print styles written, not printed); the Greek and Italian texts were written with the page, not reviewed by a native reader of the trade terms.

**Security review**: the page holds no data of the company — no amounts, names or documents — and reads nothing from the database; staff only. No proposal.

## §416 v1.192.0 (R97-132) — Purchase orders: status flow per order; production orders renumbered -PRD-

**Asked**: (1) in Purchasing, the status of each order as a flow, so Procurement and everybody know when it is received, whether it is paid and so on — propose a visual; (2) production orders have the same coding as purchase orders, which is confusing — only two are open, so it can be fixed.

**Change (1)** — `app/po.php`: `po_flow_data()` reads for a set of orders, in four statements, the line quantities (ordered / accepted / invoiced), the last receipt, the furthest logistics milestone reached and the supplier invoices (gross, paid, proforma, earliest unpaid due date); `po_flow()` turns them into eight steps with a state and a note — percentages only. Drawn by `hrf_html()` (new state `bad`, red): full under the title of `purchase_order.php`, compact in a new column of `purchase_orders.php` with a legend. RFQ: four steps. Nothing is stored.
- On the way: `done` when everything is received; `run` with the last milestone; `bad` when the expected date has passed and the order is open.
- Paid: `done` when the invoices are paid in full and everything is invoiced; `run` when partly paid or a proforma is paid in advance; `bad` when an unpaid invoice is past its due date.
- A cancelled order: reached steps stay, the rest do not apply, the last one is red.

**Change (2)** — `app/pd.php` `pd_next_no()`: `<branch>-PRD-YYYY-NNN`. Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_132_production_order_numbers.sql` (state key `r98s`): `-PO-` → `-PRD-` on numbers of exactly the automatic shape; a number typed by hand is left alone. The column is still called `po_no`.

**Tests (sandbox)**: seven test orders (draft; sent; confirmed, shipped and late; partly received with a paid proforma; received with an overdue invoice; closed and paid; cancelled) — list and order pages opened, screenshots looked at. Migration on three test numbers: two renamed, a custom one left, second run changes nothing; next number continues. Sandbox restored. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: live data; orders with credit notes or several partial invoices were reasoned, not created.

**Security review**: the flow shows states and percentages, no amounts, to whoever may already open the order; the links to Payables appear only for those who have Payables. The renumbering changes one text column. Proposal (no effect on speed): a filter on the list — "late deliveries" and "payment overdue" — fed by the same data.

## §417 v1.193.0 (R97-133) — Purchase orders: pipeline board

**Asked**: a dashboard pipeline for the purchase orders, so Procurement sees the orders and their statuses easily and summarised.

**Change** — `app/po.php`: `PO_PIPE`, `po_pipeline()` — the orders that are not closed or cancelled, each in the stage of the first unfinished step of `po_flow()` (draft · sent · transit · partial · toinvoice · topay · toclose), with flags `late`, `overdue`, `waiting` (sent more than 7 days ago, not confirmed) and `prepaid`; totals of the three attention counts. `views/purchase_orders.php`: tab `pipe` — board in the markup of the sales pipeline (`pp-board`), column head with count and total (prices only for who sees them), cards with the flow in a new smaller size (`hrf_html(…, 'mini')`), filter by supplier and search. Read-only; the data comes from the same four statements as the flow.

**Tests (sandbox)**: eight test orders across the stages (one late, one overdue, one unconfirmed for nine days, one paid in advance) — each in the expected column, flags and counts right, totals per column; screenshot looked at. Sandbox restored. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: live data; a very long list (the board shows every open order — with several hundred it will be long).

**Security review**: nothing new is exposed — the same orders and the same price rule as the list. Proposal (no effect on speed): the three attention counts on the main dashboard of Procurement and in the weekly e-mail to the buyer.

## §418 v1.193.1 (R97-134) — Payables: files of an invoice without a project are kept with the invoice

**Reported**: after paying a supplier proforma — a little more than the payment request, the amount having been corrected by hand — the page says "The payment is recorded, but the file was not kept (the invoice has no project, or the file type is not allowed)". What does it mean?

**Answer**: nothing to do with the amount. `si_pay_from_post()` stored the proof with `cp_store_file()`, which files a document in a project (`sp_files.project_id` is mandatory); the invoice of the report has no project, so the upload was skipped and R97-117 said so. The same was true, silently, for the invoice PDF itself (`si_save`) and the invoice assigned to a proforma (`si_final`).

**Change** — migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_134_si_docs.sql` (key `r98t`): `si_docs (invoice_id, payment_id, kind, title, stored_name, original_name, mime_type, size_bytes, uploaded_by, uploaded_name)`. `app/si.php`: `si_doc_store()` (seven extensions, 15 MB, content checked against the extension for PDF and images, random name under `uploads/si/yyyy/mm`), `si_docs_of()`, `si_doc_stream()` (through the invoice, `si_can()` only), `si_doc_em_attachment()` (key `sd:`), `si_pay_doc_from_post()` (action `si_pay_doc` — attach a proof to a payment afterwards; project invoices go to the project as before). Used as fallback in `si_save`, `si_final`, `si_pay`. `views/payable.php`: 📎 links, "Attach the proof" on a payment without one, proofs among the attachments of the remittance e-mail.

**About the amount**: the payment request keeps the amount it was approved for (`si_requests.amount`, `si_request_items.amount` are written when it is made); a payment is accepted up to the open amount of the invoice. When the invoice is corrected afterwards, the payment follows the invoice and the request still shows the approved figure. Not changed here — proposal below.

**Tests (sandbox)** — forms posted: invoice without project, payment with a PDF → kept, shown, downloaded byte for byte; a text file named .pdf → payment recorded, file refused with the reason; proof attached afterwards; staff without Payables 403; the document asked through another invoice 404; attachment keys `sd:` on the e-mail card. Direct address of the stored file: denied on the live site (`storage/.htaccess`, checked: 403). Sandbox restored. Error log clean.

**Not tested**: the invoice of the report itself — its payment stands; attach the proof with the new button.

**Security review**: these files are read only through Payables, which is stricter than the project files — a supplier invoice or a proof filed in a project can be opened by any member of staff who has the link (`cp_stream_file()` checks the customer side only). Proposals (no effect on speed): (1) keep all payables documents in this store, also for project invoices, or mark them so that only who sees financial data can open them; (2) when an invoice is corrected after its payment request was approved, show the difference on the request and ask for a new approval above a tolerance you set (for example 1 % or 20 EUR).

## §419 v1.193.2 (R97-135) — Payables: invoice, order, project and sub-project stay together

**What was wrong.** An invoice or proforma registered from a purchase order took the order's project and sub-project. On the page of a saved invoice the order is shown locked, and a locked field is not sent by the browser — so every later save of the header (amount, date, note) removed the order and the project. The invoice then read "No purchase order — checked by hand", its cost left the sub-project and attached files had no project.

**What changed.**

1. The order of a saved invoice is kept on every edit.
2. Project and sub-project come from the order. If the order has none, they are taken from the requisition(s) its lines came from, when they all agree.
3. Without an order, the header has the fields **Project** and **Sub-project**.
4. On an invoice that can no longer be edited (checked, requested, approved, paid), Accounting uses **Purchase order, project and sub-project › Save the link**. Only orders of the same supplier are offered. The order can be changed while no line of the invoice is matched to an order line. The sub-project cost follows the link.
5. Payables › Payment requests: each invoice of a request shows its order and project › sub-project; "no project — link it" opens the invoice at the link form.
6. One-time repair on the first opening of Payables: invoices whose lines still point to one order get the order back; invoices of an order get the order's project. Each repaired invoice has the log line "Linked to order / project — System".

**Files.** Files already kept with the invoice (Accounting only) stay there after a link; files uploaded after the link go to the project as before.

**Cybersecurity.** The link action is open to Accounting / management only (same right as registering an invoice); the order must be a sent purchase order of the invoice's supplier; project and sub-project are checked against each other on the server. No new file access path, no migration, no extra query on other pages.

## §420 v1.194.0 (R97-136) — Purchase orders: vessel shown, filter by vessel

- **Where the vessel shows:** Purchasing › Purchase orders, column Project (under project and sub-project, ⚓ name); cards of the Pipeline board; tab RFQ; tab To buy; header of the order page (link to the vessel).
- **Filter:** field **Vessel** on the filter bar of Purchase orders, RFQ and Pipeline. The list offers only vessels that have orders. Clicking the vessel in a row filters by it. The search box also matches the vessel name.
- **Source:** the vessel of the project (project card). Orders without a project show no vessel — set the project on the order, or the vessel on the project.
- **Cybersecurity:** read-only change; the filter value is an integer bound as a query parameter; visibility of orders is unchanged (IT-only buyers still see only IT purchases). One extra join on the order query, no extra queries per row.

## §421 v1.195.0 (R97-137) — BOM and requisition: BOM price or last buying price

**Why.** The Engineering BOM import did not read the column *Unit price*; estimates came only from the buying history of the item.

**Now.**

1. Import (New BOM / Import on a draft revision): the column *Unit price* (also: price, unit cost, cost, net price, τιμή, τιμή μονάδας) is kept on each line as its **BOM price**. If the file brought prices, the revision starts on *BOM price*.
2. Above the lines of a BOM and of a requisition: **Estimated prices from: Last buying price / BOM price**. The click recalculates lines and total and is logged ("Price source").
3. Rules per line: *BOM price* → the BOM price when the line has one, else the buying-price rule. *Last buying price* → the pricing rule of Settings › Procurement on the item's history, else the last price of the item card, else the BOM price, else what was typed.
4. **Take the BOM prices from the file** (same bar): upload the Excel of a BOM / requisition made before this version. Match by description, else maker part number / code; the same key with two different prices is left alone. Only the BOM price is written; the estimate switches to BOM price.
5. BOM → requisition carries the BOM prices and the choice. Requisition → purchase order and BOM → purchase order: with *BOM price* chosen the order line takes it (origin "BOM price"); otherwise the supplier price rule as before, with the BOM price as fallback.
6. Who may choose: Procurement; the engineer of the BOM; the requester while the requisition is a draft. Not on cancelled / closed requisitions. Prices are shown only to users who see prices.

**Database.** `install/migrate_cp_r97_137_bom_price.sql` — `bom_price` on `bom_lines`, `bom_import_rows`, `pr_lines`; `price_src` on `bom_revisions`, `pr_requests` (default `last`). State key `r98u`.

**Cybersecurity.** Upload limited to .xlsx up to 8 MB, read with the existing reader and not stored; actions check the right on the document server-side; values are bound parameters; the BOM file content is not sent to the assistant by this function.

## §422 v1.195.1 (R97-138) — Production: the engineer enters the amount of a subcontract

- **Before:** Amount / currency of a subcontracting line only for positions with "sees financial data".
- **Now:** on the page of a production order, anyone who may edit that order (production user, order manager, project manager, administrator) has the fields and sees the amounts of that order's subcontracts.
- **Unchanged:** Production › Subcontracting (list over all orders), its total and the PDFs show amounts only with the financial right; users marked "no financial data" are excluded everywhere.
- **Cybersecurity:** the rule is checked on the server when saving (`ps_amount_can`), not only by hiding the field; a user without it keeps the stored amount unchanged.

## §423 v1.195.2 (R97-139) — Purchase order flow: paid in advance on a proforma

- Colours of the flow: green = done, yellow = in progress, grey = not started, red = late / overdue.
- **Before:** Paid was green only when the order was also fully invoiced with a sales invoice, so an order prepaid on a proforma showed Paid in yellow.
- **Now:** Paid is green when everything registered is paid and the registered documents (invoice or proforma) cover all lines of the order; note "paid in advance" while the sales invoice is missing.
- Invoiced stays yellow, "proforma — sales invoice awaited", until the sales invoice is assigned on the proforma.
- No database change. Cybersecurity: display rule only.

## §424 v1.195.3 (R97-140) — Filters: type to pick from the list

- Rule of the searchable drop-down (`assets/portal.js`): more than 8 entries anywhere (unchanged); **4 or more entries inside a filter bar**; always when the list is marked `data-search`; never when marked `data-nosearch`.
- Purchase orders and Pipeline: the Vessel and Supplier filters are marked always searchable.
- The native list stays in the form, so values, keyboard use and validation are unchanged. Cybersecurity: browser-side display only, no inline script, CSP unchanged.

## §425 v1.195.4 (R97-141) — Purchase orders and requisitions: remembered filters

- The two lists join the remembered list filters of the portal (R29-008, `app/flt.php`): `purchase_orders` (status, purpose, supplier, vessel, search — one memory per tab) and `requisitions` (search, status, project, mine).
- Opening the list without any filter in the address (menu, back link, tab) restores the last selection; "Apply" stores the new one; **Clear** forgets it.
- Tabs Purchase orders / Pipeline / RFQs carry supplier, vessel, purpose and search to each other; the status is per tab.
- Cybersecurity: the selection is kept in the user's own server session only (not in the browser, not in the database) and ends with the session; values pass through the same validation as typed filters.

## §426 v1.196.0 (R97-142) — Purchase orders: restore a cancelled order

- **Who / where:** Procurement and management; button **↺ Restore** on the page of a cancelled order or RFQ; a reason is mandatory.
- **State after restore:** Confirmed if it had been confirmed, else Sent if it had been sent, else Draft (`po_restore_state`).
- **What comes back:** all lines open; for Sent / Confirmed the requisition lines are booked as ordered again and the sub-project cost commitment returns.
- **Refused when:** a requisition line of the order was ordered elsewhere or cancelled meanwhile (message lists the lines); the supplier is no longer approved.
- **Not covered:** an order whose balance was cancelled after a partial receipt (status Received).
- **Supplier:** not informed automatically.
- **Cybersecurity:** same right as Cancel, checked on the server; reason and user are written to the order log; no database change.

## §427 v1.196.1 (R97-143) — Purchase order PDF: delivery per line

- Column **Delivery** on the purchase order PDF (`po_pdf`), between Qty and Unit price: promised date → lead time "N days" → needed date → empty.
- Printed only when at least one line carries one of the three; the description column narrows from 78 to 56 mm.
- RFQ document unchanged. No database change. Cybersecurity: the document shows only data already on the order page; no new access.

## §428 v1.196.2 (R97-144) — Purchase orders: planned delivery of the timeline shown in the project

- **Expected delivery of an order** = the latest of: delivery date of the header, planned date of the timeline step *Delivered*, expected date of each open line (promised date, else sent / confirmed + lead time).
- Saving a planned date on *Delivered* fills the delivery date of the order when it is empty.
- Sub-project › Procurement › Purchase orders: column Delivery shows the expected date (was: header date only).
- One-time completion of existing orders on the first opening of the list (setting `po_exp_fix`).
- Handbook wording of v1.196.1 corrected: the line fields are "Lead time" and "Needed by".
- Cybersecurity: no new access or input; no database change.

## §429 v1.197.0 (R97-145) — Goods receipt: split a line between project and stock

- Purchase order › Goods receipt: next to the destination of each line, field **Quantity to the project (blank = all)**. Used only with destination *Project (delivery note)*.
- Booking: the whole accepted quantity enters the warehouse; the typed quantity leaves on the delivery note of the receipt (project / sub-project of the order); the difference stays as free stock. 0 typed = all to stock.
- Requisition line behind the order line: received = whole quantity, issued = project part; only the project part is reserved, so the stock part is free.
- Free-text lines (no inventory item): the typed quantity goes on the delivery note; nothing is stocked.
- Refused when the quantity is above the accepted quantity of the line.
- Database: `install/migrate_cp_r97_145_receipt_split.sql` — `po_receipt_lines.dest_qty`. State key `r98v`.
- Cybersecurity: same right as booking a receipt (warehouse / procurement), quantities validated on the server, no new access.

## §430 v1.198.0 (R97-146) — Purchase orders: delivery lots

**Purpose.** Partial deliveries: which lines arrive when, and where each consignment is.

1. **Card "Delivery lots"** on the order page (orders that are sent; not on drafts / RFQs). Columns: Lot, Planned, Shipped, Arrived, Delivered, Carrier / tracking, Note, Lines (received / total), Status. Each row is saved with 💾; ✕ deletes the lot (its lines stay on the order, without a lot).
2. **Propose from the line dates:** one lot per date — promised date of the line, else sent / confirmed date + lead time. Lines without a date stay without a lot. Replaces the existing lots; refused once a lot has shipped / arrived / delivered dates or a tracking number.
3. **By hand:** row "new lot" (planned date mandatory), then "Lines in the lots" → choose the lot per line → "Save the lots of the lines". Up to 40 lots per order.
4. **Status of a lot:** Delivered (delivered date typed, or all its lines received — date of the last goods receipt), Late (planned date passed), Arrived, Shipped, Planned.
5. **Timeline:** the planned date of "Delivered" = the latest planned lot (set automatically at every change); the expected date of the order follows it, plus lines in no lot.
6. **Lists:** Delivery cell with one line per lot in Purchase orders, Sub-project › Procurement; Pipeline cards show "◐ n lots · d delivered · next <date>".

**Database.** `install/migrate_cp_r97_146_po_lots.sql` — table `po_lots`, column `po_lines.lot_id`. State key `r98w`.

**Cybersecurity.** Lots are changed only with the Procurement right (checked on the server for every action and every lot / line against its order); read with the order; all changes in the order log; no file upload, no new external access.

## §431 v1.198.1 (R97-147) — Performance: batched figures of sub-projects and projects

**Finding (health report 19/11/2026).** Dashboard up to 4,0 s / ~4 300 statements when the KPI cockpit was renewed (every 10 minutes, on one user's page); projects list 700–1 660 statements; HR dashboard 430–530.

**Cause.** `kpi_projects()` → `kpi_project()` → `kpi_sub()` / `bg_calc()` asked 13 statements per sub-project and 4 per project; other loops did the same (budget attention list, cash-flow forecast, project roll-up); the projects list asked ~12 per project; `hr_people()` was not kept for the request.

**Change.**

| Area | Before | Now |
|---|---|---|
| Sub-project figures | 13 statements per sub-project | `bg_prime()` — about 12 statements for all (chunks of 400) |
| Project KPI (revisions, risks, project, sub-projects) | 4 per project | 4 in total |
| KPI trends | 1 per project and KPI | `kpi_trend_prime()` — 1 per 300 projects (falls back when the database has no window functions) |
| Projects list | offers, branch, footprint ×4, tickets card, permits card per project | batches; cards asked only when the project has open tickets / permits |
| People online | 1 per person | 1 |
| HR pages | people list read up to 15× | kept for the request (GET only); periods and type switches in one statement from the third person |
| Inventory list | last movement per item without a fitting index | index `ix_invmv_item_kind_at` |

The single functions read the batch first and fall back to their own statement, so every other caller behaves as before.

**Check.** Page output compared before / after (identical) on a test copy with 153 projects, 463 sub-projects, timesheets, budgets, costs, risks, snapshots: Dashboard 6 448 → 219 statements, KPIs 8 022 → 112, KPI report 6 315 → 87, Cash flow 2 605 → 61, projects list 526 → 215.

**Not changed.** Single simple statements that took 100–300 ms (`SELECT k, v FROM cp_settings`, `SHOW TABLES`, one-row updates): waiting time of the shared database, mostly the first statement of a request.

**Database.** `install/migrate_cp_r97_147_perf_index.sql`. State key `r98x`.

**Cybersecurity.** Read paths only; the batches use integer-cast ids and the same scope rules (the list of projects is still selected by the user's scope before anything is read); no new access; nothing kept between requests or users.

## §432 v1.198.2 (R97-148) — Folded logs; delivery lots on a draft order

- **Logs folded:** the log card of 16 document pages is a folded section (`<details class="card card--details">`): purchase order, requisition, BOM, payable, payment request, production order, system budget, ISO / IT record, assessment flow, job order, offer, ticket, timesheet, activity report, recruitment application, task. Field service and inspection reports already worked this way. Open with a click on the title.
- **Lots on a draft:** the card "Delivery lots" is shown on a draft purchase order too; the proposal counts lead times from today while the order is not sent.
- **Why the lots were missing:** they were shown only on sent orders (R97-146).
- Cybersecurity: display only; rights unchanged; no database change.

- The first line of a continuation page is in the body colour and size (the page header left the gold colour active).

## §434 v1.198.4 (R97-150) — Customer app icon is the Golden A/S logo

- `customer/mobile-customer/icons/icon-192.png`, `icon-512.png`, `icon-1024.png` are the same logo icon as the Service and PM apps (white Golden A/S logo on navy, gold bar); the gold G mark is retired.

Source: `docs-R68/capacitor-customer/package.json`, an archived prototype pinned to `@capacitor/android` / `@capacitor/ios` 6.1.2 (advisory: remote content can be loaded at the app origin through the internal HTTP proxy path). The store builds never used it — they are built from `mobile-native/` on Capacitor 8.6, where `npm audit` reports 0 vulnerabilities. The archive file is renamed `package.R68-archive.json.txt` so it is no longer read as a manifest.

## §440 v1.201.0 (R97-156) — Golden HR 1.1.0: Today, recruiting, job descriptions, HR year, expiring documents, equipment

**Server** — `customer/api/v1/lib/portal_hr_more.php` (`PortalHRMore`), loaded in `bootstrap.php`; the modules `mailer`, `pmt`, `rcr`, `jd`, `hry`, `hre` are loaded on first use only (the other apps do not load them). Gate unchanged: `PortalHR::allowed()` on every request (HR scope, Owner, CEO).

| Route | What |
|---|---|
| `GET /hr/app/today` (also `today` in `GET /me`) | counts only |
| `GET /hr/app/recruiting` | open vacancies with counts per stage, candidates in process (max 300), planned interviews, evaluation criteria, labels |
| `GET /hr/app/application/{id}` | candidate card: contact, cover note, HR notes, evaluations, interviews |
| `GET /hr/app/cv/{id}` | the CV streamed (`rcr_cv_stream`), `no-store`, logged as `cv_view` in `rc_log`, 60 / minute |
| `POST /hr/app/application/{id}/status` | shortlisted · interview · offer · rejected (reason required); `from` guards against a change made in the portal (409); step-up; `rcr_application_decide()` |
| `POST /hr/app/application/{id}/evaluation` | `rcr_eval_save()` |
| `POST /hr/app/interview/{id}/status` | done · no_show · cancelled; `rcr_interview_status()` |
| `GET /hr/app/jds`, `POST /hr/app/jd/{id}/decide` | job descriptions in review with steps and content; `jd_approve()` / `jd_reject()`; step-up |
| `GET /hr/app/year`, `POST /hr/app/year/tick` | `hry_cycle()`, `hry_tick_from_post()` |
| `GET /hr/app/expiring?days=30..120` | contracts, training certificates, work permits, equipment |
| `GET /hr/app/equipment`, `GET /hr/app/equipment/doc/{id}` | `hre_waiting()`, list with lines |
| `POST /hr/app/equipment/doc/{id}/receive`, `/paper` | `hre_receive_from_post()`, `hre_accept($id, $paperName)`; step-up |

Not sent to the app: screening answers, IP / user agent of a candidate, CV path, salary or payroll data. «Hired» is portal-only (it creates the employee card).

**Client** — `customer/mobile-hr` 1.1.0: `js/more.js` (`HRX`: today, recruiting, candidate, cv, jds, jd, year, expiring, equipment, eqdoc), `js/pdfview.js` + `vendor/pdf.min.js`, `vendor/pdf.worker.min.js` (pdf.js 4.4.168, Apache 2.0, served from this origin — the app's CSP `script-src 'self'` / `worker-src 'self'` is unchanged, no CDN). Tabs: Today · Inbox · Recruiting · Messages · More; screens without a tab (People, Calendar, Balances…) get the back button. The live lists are kept in memory only (45 s) and are dropped when the app locks; nothing new is written to IndexedDB except the Today counts inside `me`. Texts: EN, EL, IT (128 keys); UK / BG fall back to EN.

No database change. No push notifications yet: the portal stores the device token but has no sender (needs an Apple push key).

Cybersecurity: every write goes through the portal's own functions (rights, logs, retention clock unchanged); decisions on candidates, job descriptions and equipment use the authenticator step-up; rate limits per user; CV views logged; candidate data not persisted on the device. Proposal: make the authenticator (or a passkey) mandatory for accounts that use Golden HR, because without one the step-up is skipped.

- No database change. Cybersecurity: style sheet only; nothing to report.

## §442 v1.202.0 (R97-158) — Recruiting: general applications (CV without a vacancy), assign to a vacancy

- `customer/app/rcr.php`: `RCR_GENERAL_CODE` (`VAC-GENERAL`), `rcr_general_vacancy()` (the internal vacancy, created on first use: status open, `publish_web = 0`, first screening set only because the column requires one), `rcr_is_general()` (code starts with `GEN-`), `rcr_pub_general()` (website, one multipart step), `rcr_assign_vacancy()`. The internal vacancy is excluded from the website feed and from `rcr_pub_apply()` even if someone ticks «web» on it.
- `customer/rcr_feed.php`: `POST ?r=general` (multipart: name, email, phone, department_interest, cover_note, lang, consent_gdpr, cv) — site key and the write rate limit as the other writes; 201 with `application_id` GEN-…, 409 when the address already has a general application, 415 / 422 for the file, 503 when no screening set exists yet.
- The CV is checked by content (PDF, DOC, DOCX; size limit `rc_cv_max_mb`) and stored in `storage/rc` under the GEN number, as for vacancy applications. The row: `passed = 1`, `score` NULL, status `cv_received`, consent version and time.
- Views: `rcr_dashboard.php` (button «General applications (n)», label «General», no screening pill), `rcr_application.php` (card «Assign to a vacancy», screening card replaced by a note), action `rcr_assign` in `rcr_actions.php` (HR only; refuses when the person already applied to the target vacancy; logged as `assigned`).
- Retention unchanged: `rcr_retention_run()` treats GEN rows like every application.
- Golden HR 1.1.1: `general` in the candidate rows, filter chip «General», label on the card.
- Language keys `rc.general*`, `rc.assign_*` (12) in en, el, it, bg, uk — 12 438 keys each.
- Website (`goldenas-website`): `careers.html` section `#general` «Send us your CV», `hr/careers-b.js` `submitGeneral()`, proxy `api/hr.php` `r=general` (honeypot, checks, site key stays on the server).
- No database change. Cybersecurity: the public route accepts a file only with the site key (held by the website server), is rate-limited per address, checks the real file type and size, stores outside the web root and never echoes data about other candidates; duplicate check answers 409 only for the same address on the same list (as vacancy applications do).
