## §444 v1.203.0 (R97-160) — HR newsletters: schools and recruiting partners

- **Database step r98y** — `install/migrate_cp_r97_160_nl_dept.sql`: `nl_issues.dept` (`sales` default, `hr`). Without it the HR page answers "no access" and everything else works as before (`nl_dept_has()`).
- `customer/app/nl.php`: `NL_HR_GROUPS` (`school`, `rp_agency`, `rp_public`, `rp_assoc`, `rp_training`), `nl_dept()`, `nl_hr_can()` (= `hr_can()`), `nl_can_dept()`, `nl_can_issue()`, `nl_list_url()`, `nl_hr_partners()`, `nl_hr_partner_add_from_post()`; `nl_groups($dept)`, `nl_audience($aud, $site, $dept)` (an HR issue ignores saved lists and website subscribers), `nl_issues($dept)`, `nl_create(…, $dept)`, `nl_copy()` keeps the department.
- Rights: `nl_handle_action()` lets HR in, then checks every issue against its department (`nl_can_issue`); lists, subscribers, opt-outs and sending settings stay with sales / management (`nl_can()`); `nl_may_send()` uses the issue's department. Approval (`nl_appr_*`), mailbox (`nl_from`), batch, daily ceiling, tracking and unsubscribe are unchanged and shared.
- `customer/app/ct.php` `co_kinds()`: five new organisation types (`companies.kind` is free text — no database change); labels `co.kind_*`.
- Views: `hr_newsletters.php` (new; KPIs, create, issues with results, register of schools and partners with quick add), `newsletter.php` (HR issue: HR types as audience, no lists / website topics, breadcrumb to HR), `newsletters.php` (shows sales issues only). Menu: HR › Newsletters (also in the HR-only menu). Page `hr_newsletters` registered in `index.php`.
- Language keys: 5 `co.kind_*`, 30 `nl.hr_*` in en, el, it, bg, uk — 12 473 keys each.
- Cybersecurity: no new sending path — an HR issue cannot be sent without the existing approval (author never approves his own issue); HR cannot read sales issues, customer lists or website subscribers; the quick-add form writes through prepared statements and validates the e-mail; the HR audience is limited to the HR organisation types plus addresses typed by hand (visible to the approver, who sees the recipient count).

## §445 My approvals — register of every approval process per user; talent pool: CV added by HR (GAS-CP-R97-161, v1.204.0)

- New `customer/app/apr.php` (required in `index.php` after `nl.php`): `apr_kinds()`, `apr_waiting()` (what waits for the signed-in user), `apr_mine()` (own requests: pending with whom, or decided in the last `APR_DAYS` = 30 days), `apr_waiting_count()`. Read-only; every provider is wrapped in `apr_try()` (module present + rights of that module, errors swallowed per process).
- Processes and who is asked: leave (`hr_requests` — manager step, then HR), timesheets (`ts_to_approve()`), training applications (`hr_training_queue()`), purchase requisitions (`pr_requests` submitted → `pr_admin()`, mgmt → `mgmt_can()`), supplier payment requests (`si_my_pending()`), engineering extra hours (`eng_can_decide()`), BOM revisions issued (`bom_pr_can()`), job descriptions (step `mine` of `jd_steps()`), newsletters (`nl_approver()`, `appr_state = pending`), equipment lists (`hr_eq_docs`: return open / issue disputed → HR, issue sent → the employee), HR assistant flows (`hr_flows.assignee_id`, HR for submitted / hr), portal change requests (`cr_is_dev()`).
- Page `?p=my_approvals[&tab=wait|mine&k=<kind>]` (`views/my_approvals.php`): four KPIs, tabs Waiting for me / My requests, filter per process, age pill (gold from 3 days, red from 7). Menu: top group for all staff (also in the HR-only, PA-only and employee menus); badge `my_approvals` in `nav_badges_compute()`.
- Not in the register: customer approvals of documents, offers, job orders and service reports (pages Approvals / Offers, customer side), budget pricing requests, supplier evaluations.
- Recruiting: `rcr_manual_add()` + action `rcr_manual_add` (HR only, CSRF) and the form on Recruiting › Talent pool. Creates a general application `GEN-YYYY-NNNN` (`source = hr`, `consent_version = hr-manual`, `keep_until` = pool retention, `pool_consent_at`) with the CV stored like a website CV (type checked on content, size limit `rc_cv_max_mb`, random file name), adds the talent pool entry (`source = hr`) and logs `manual_cv`. The pool list shows the CV number of entries that have an application with a CV.
- Language keys: 49 `apr.*`, 19 `rc.man_*` in en, el, it, bg, uk — 12 541 keys each. No database change.
- Cybersecurity: the register adds no decision path and shows only lines the module's own rights function lets the user decide (or his own requests); HR assistant flows are listed by number and type only, confidential flows never under My requests; SQL is parameterised, status lists are constants. The manual CV form requires the HR right, the consent confirmation and a real PDF / Word file. Proposal: a weekly e-mail digest of approvals waiting longer than 7 days, and an escalation to the deputy when the approver is on leave.

## §446 Projects — filter by open issues and the list of open issues per project (GAS-CP-R97-162, v1.205.0)

- `project_tree_filtered()` (`customer/app/pm.php`): filter `iss` = `any` | `open` | `in_progress` | `overdue` | `high` → `EXISTS` on `sp_issues` with the viewer's visibility (`iss_vis_sql('ix')`).
- `iss_open_by_project(array $ids)` (`customer/app/iss.php`): the open issues of all projects of the page in one query, grouped per project.
- `views/projects.php`: field Open issues in the filter bar (kept by paging and export links through `$keep`); block Open issues per project (fold `issues-<id>`, 15 lines, the rest by note) between Offers and Tasks; opens by itself when the filter is set.
- Language keys: 10 `pr.f_iss_*` / `pr.iss_*` in en, el, it, bg, uk — 12 551 keys each. No database change.
- Cybersecurity: the filter value is checked against a fixed list and never reaches SQL as text; project ids are cast to integers; a customer sees only issues marked external, in the filter as in the list. Performance: one extra query per page of projects.

## §447 Production › Subcontracting — counter counted lines of deleted orders (GAS-CP-R97-163, v1.205.1)

- Cause: `pd_delete()` removed the order, its units, files and log but left its rows in `production_subs`; `ps_counts()` counted the table alone while `ps_list()` joins orders and projects — tab pill and KPIs showed 1, the list 0.
- Fix (`customer/app/pd.php`): `ps_counts()` counts over the same joins and project scope as the list; `pd_delete()` deletes the order's subcontract lines; `ps_orphans_purge()` removes lines left by earlier deletions (runs with the next order deletion). No database change, no new language keys.
- Cybersecurity: the counter now respects the viewer's project scope like the list (it counted all projects before).

## §448 Production — process flow indicator on the list and the order (GAS-CP-R97-164, v1.206.0)

- `customer/app/pd.php`: `pd_flow_data(array $ids)` — evidence of all orders of the page in three queries (units per status with average %, documents per phase, subcontract lines per status and the late ones); `pd_flow(array $o, ?array $d)` — nine steps `design, production, sub, fat, delivered, hat, commissioning, sat, closed` in the format of `hrf_html()` (same drawing as `po_flow()`).
- States: done = the order status is past the step; run = the order is at the step, or units already reached it; bad = at the step with `planned_end` passed, or subcontract line late / rejected; na = no subcontract lines, or order cancelled.
- Views: `production.php` (column Progress, compact), `production_order.php` (full flow above the milestones, id `pd-flow`).
- Language keys: 8 `pdf.n_*` in en, el, it, bg, uk — 12 559 keys each. No database change.
- Cybersecurity: read-only, staff only (the views already require it), ids cast to integers, no amounts in the indicator. Performance: three queries per list page, independent of the number of orders.

## §449 Sub-project › Process — production flow of its production orders (GAS-CP-R97-165, v1.206.1)

- `views/subproject.php`: block `#sp-pd-flow` below the stage editor — for every production order of the sub-project that is not cancelled: number, title, status, % and `hrf_html(pd_flow())` in full; evidence through `pd_flow_data()` (three queries for all orders). Staff only (inside the staff branch of Process).
- Language key `pd.flow_title` in en, el, it, bg, uk — 12 560 keys each. No database change.
- Cybersecurity: read-only, parameterised query, no amounts.

## §450 Community — YouTube videos in posts (GAS-CP-R97-166, v1.207.0)

- Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_166_soc_video.sql` (check `r98z`): `soc_posts.kind` gains `video`, new column `soc_posts.video_id VARCHAR(20)`.
- `customer/app/soc.php`: `soc_video_has()` (column present), `soc_yt_id()` (accepts only hosts youtube.com / youtu.be / youtube-nocookie.com with or without www., m., music.; paths `/watch?v=`, `/shorts/`, `/live/`, `/embed/`, `/v/`; the id must match `[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}`), `soc_video_html()`; `soc_post_create()` takes `video_url` (required for kind `video`, optional otherwise). Kind `video` may be posted by everybody, like a photo.
- `views/community.php`: kind Video, field YouTube video link, feed tab Video, player placeholder; the page script (with nonce) builds the `iframe` to `https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/<id>` only on the click of Play, with `sandbox` and `referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"` (YouTube refuses an embed without referrer; the portal's own policy stays `same-origin`).
- `index.php`: `frame-src` of the Content-Security-Policy adds `https://www.youtube-nocookie.com` on the page `community` only.
- Language keys: 7 `soc.*` in en, el, it, bg, uk — 12 567 keys each.
- Cybersecurity: the link is never stored or printed — only the validated 11-character id; no thumbnail or script is fetched from Google before the click, so opening the feed tells YouTube nothing; the frame is sandboxed and can be shown on one page only. No effect on page speed (no extra request until Play).

## §451 First sign-in — own password; apps: protect this device (GAS-CP-R97-167, v1.208.0)

- Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_167_must_change_pw.sql` (check `r99a`): `customer_users.must_change_pw TINYINT(1) DEFAULT 0`, `customer_users.pw_changed_at DATETIME`, `login_log.outcome` gains `pw_changed`. Existing accounts stay 0.
- `customer/app/auth.php`: `pwc_has()`, `pwc_must()`, `pwc_error()` (10–200 characters, a letter and a digit, not the local part of the e-mail when 4+ characters, not the current password), `pwc_store()` (hash, flag 0, `pw_changed_at`), `pwc_flag()`. `require_login()` sends a flagged account to `password_change` for every address except that page and the actions `password_change` / `logout`.
- `customer/index.php`: POST gate before the action switch; page gate (`$page = 'password_change'`); action `password_change`; `account_save` uses `pwc_store()`. View `views/password_change.php` (stand-alone, like the authenticator step).
- Administration › Users (`views/admin_users.php`, `au_update`, `cu_save` in `pm_actions.php`): checkbox `must_change_pw` per account (ticked on the create form), status under the password field. `ct.php`: accounts made or given a password by the contacts import are flagged.
- API `POST /auth/login` (`api/v1/index.php`): a flagged account gets `403 {password_change: true}` and no token; the same call with `new_password` sets it (`422` with the reason when refused) and signs in. Clients: Service 0.7.1, PM 1.0.12, HR 1.1.2, Customer 1.0.9 — fields New password / Repeat on the sign-in screen.
- Apps, once per device after sign-in (`UI.securityAsk()`, keys `gas.secasked`, `gpm.secasked`, `ghr.secasked`): Service and PM offer Face ID / fingerprint on open and show three safety habits; HR (biometrics already mandatory) shows the note.
- Language keys: portal 21 (`pwc.*`, `ll.o_pw_changed`) — 12 588 each; apps 15 (`pwc.*`, `sec.*`) in en, el, it, uk, bg.
- Cybersecurity: a default password can no longer stay in use — no session content and no API token before the own password exists; rule check on the server for portal and apps; the sign-in rate limit and lock-out apply unchanged; the event is logged. Proposed next: check new passwords against the list of leaked passwords (k-anonymity range query, no password leaves the server), and an expiry reminder for accounts whose password is older than 12 months.

## §452 Administration › Users — the same starting password for selected accounts (GAS-CP-R97-168, v1.209.0)

- Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_168_pw_reset_outcome.sql` (check `r99b`): `login_log.outcome` gains `pw_reset`.
- `views/admin_users.php`: tick box per row (`input.js-bulk`, not on the own row) and one for the page (`#au-bulk-all`); gold bar `#au-bulk` above the list with the count, the starting password twice, the button and Clear selection. The selection lives in `sessionStorage` (`gas.au.bulk`, this browser tab only), so it survives paging and filters; it is emptied after a successful run (`bulk_done=1`). The page script carries the nonce. The bar is shown only when the columns of R97-167 exist.
- `app/pm_actions.php`, action `cu_bulk_pw` (management only, request token as every action): ids as integers, the own account dropped, at most 300; password 10–200 characters with a letter and a digit, typed twice; per account `password_hash()` with its own salt, `must_change_pw = 1`, lock-out cleared, `sec_revoke_all()` (browser sessions and app refresh tokens), `login_log(..., 'pw_reset')`. The authenticator secret is not touched.
- `views/admin_log.php`: outcome filter gains `pw_reset`.
- Language keys: 14 (`pwc.bulk_*`, `ll.o_pw_reset`) in en, el, it, bg, uk — 12 602 each.
- Cybersecurity: the action cannot be used by anybody below management and never on the own account; the password is never stored, logged or shown in clear and is not kept in the browser (only the ids of the selection are); the accounts cannot work until they have chosen an own password (R97-167), and their open sessions end at once. Remaining exposure: a starting password known to several people is valid for each account until its first sign-in, and passkey sign-in in the apps is not stopped by the flag. Proposed next (no effect on speed): a validity period for a starting password (the account is blocked when it was not replaced within N days); refuse a starting password that is on the leaked-password list; an option to generate a different starting password per account and hand them over as a list.

## §453 Demonstration accounts — invented data in the apps, portal closed (GAS-CP-R97-169, v1.210.0)

- Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_169_demo_account.sql` (check `r99c`): `customer_users.is_demo TINYINT(1) DEFAULT 0`. Set per staff account in Administration › Users (`au_update`; the change raises `session_epoch`, so open sessions end).
- Portal: `attempt_login()` and `require_login()` refuse a demonstration account outside the API (`demo.web`).
- API (`api/v1/index.php`): for a demonstration account in the staff apps `GET /me` and the sign-in answer carry `demo: true`; every other route answers 403 `demo` — except in Golden HR, where `PortalHRDemo::handle()` (`api/v1/lib/portal_hr_demo.php`) answers all routes of the app from an invented company in the shapes of `PortalHR` / `PortalHRMore` (inbox, history, people, calendar, balances, messages, today, recruiting with a generated sample CV, job descriptions, HR year, expiring documents, equipment, sync). Decisions are kept per account in `storage/demo/hr_<id>.json` and reset at sign-in; no database read or write, no e-mail.
- Service 0.7.2 and Golden PM 1.0.13: on `user.demo` the app drops the real session and switches to its built-in demonstration data set (`gas.demo` / `gpm.demo`), stays so across restarts and returns to normal at sign-out. The built-in sets no longer carry real names (user, yards, vessel).
- The customer app is not affected by the flag.
- Language keys: 4 `demo.*` in en, el, it, bg, uk.
- Cybersecurity: before this change the accounts given to the app stores read real personnel, project and customer data. Now the data is unreachable for them on the server (not only hidden in the app), and the portal is closed to them. Proposed: an expiry date on demonstration accounts, and a mark in the sign-in log for demonstration sign-ins.

## §454 Golden PM 1.0.14 — demonstration data without step-up code (GAS-CP-R97-170, v1.210.1)

- `customer/mobile-pm/js/api.js`: the profile of the built-in demonstration data set has `totp_enabled: false`, so the decision sheets show no authenticator field (the store reviewer has no authenticator for the demonstration account). Real accounts are served by the portal and keep the step-up.
- Also in the repository: `mobile-native/well-known/apple-app-site-association` with the four bundle ids and the workflow `wellknown.yml` that uploads it to `public_html/.well-known/` with the deploy account. Without the id of an app iOS refuses passkeys and the Face ID check of its web view (Golden HR showed "not available on this device").
- Cybersecurity: no change for real accounts; the demonstration account still reads nothing from the portal.

## §455 Customer app 1.0.10 — photo on a ticket stopped the app on iOS (GAS-CP-R97-171, v1.210.2)

- Report: on iOS, New ticket › photo taken › confirmed → the app stopped and the ticket was never created.
- `customer/mobile-customer/lib/native.js`: `pickPhoto()` / `pickFile()` use the file input of the web view through `pickWith()` (input kept in the document until it answers, `cancel` handled); the Capacitor Camera plug-in is no longer called (the Service app has always used the system picker). `compress()` decodes the picture already reduced with `createImageBitmap(…, {resizeWidth: 1600})` and falls back to the canvas path — a 48-megapixel photo no longer needs its full bitmap in the memory of the web view.
- `customer/mobile-customer/screens/tickets.js`: draft of the new ticket in `localStorage` (`gas.cu.tkdraft`, text fields only, 6 hours), restored when the form opens, removed on send and on sign-out (`logoutLocal()`); 20 MB limit also on files of a new ticket.
- Tested in a browser with the demo data of the app: 12-megapixel photo attached, page reloaded, draft restored, ticket created. Not reproduced on an iPhone.
- Cybersecurity: the draft holds only what the user typed, on the phone, and is removed at sign-out. Finding: the sign-in screen of the demo mode of the customer app (`?mock=1`) prints demo credentials that are readable in the public app code — the real review account must not use the same password.


## §456 Customer app 1.0.11 — demonstration accounts for customer users (GAS-CP-R97-172, v1.210.3)

- The demonstration flag of §453 (`customer_users.is_demo`) now also applies to customer accounts: the tick box 🎭 is shown on every row of Administration › Users.
- `api/v1/index.php`: for a demonstration account the customer app gets `demo: true` at sign-in and at `GET /me` and HTTP 403 on every other route, exactly as Service and PM — no report, offer, ticket, document or vessel of a real customer leaves the portal.
- `customer/mobile-customer/lib/api.js`: on `user.demo` the app closes the server session, switches to its built-in data set (`gcu.demo`, `gcu.mock`), stays signed in over a reload and returns to the real server at sign-out (`logoutLocal()`). The authenticator step of the data set is off in the demonstration. `sw.js` cache `gas-customer-1.0.11`.
- The built-in data set no longer prints sign-in details on the sign-in screen; its fixture password was replaced.
- Tested in a browser against a local emulation of the API: sign-in → invented fleet, Home / Sign / Offers / Tickets / More filled, reload keeps the session, sign-out resets, no data request reaches the server.
- Cybersecurity: a review or presentation account can no longer read a real customer's documents. The web portal refuses demonstration accounts. No effect on performance (one flag already read with the user row).

## §457 App icons with the app name (GAS-CP-R97-173, v1.210.4)

- `customer/mobile/icons`, `customer/mobile-pm/icons`, `customer/mobile-customer/icons`: `icon-1024/512/192(/180).png` redrawn — same navy field, Golden A/S logo and gold band, plus a gold tab at the lower right with SERVICE, PM or CUSTOMER (navy lettering), matching the HR tab of Golden HR.
- Client versions raised so the service workers drop the cached icons: Service 0.7.3, PM 1.0.15, Customer 1.0.12.
- Native apps: the icon is part of the binary — new builds by `build-ios.yml` / `build-android.yml`; the Play store listing icon (512 px) is changed in the Play Console.
- Cybersecurity: images only, no code path changed.

## §458 Service app 0.7.4 — icons in the bottom menu (GAS-CP-R97-174, v1.210.5)

- `customer/mobile/index.html`: the empty `<i>` of each tab replaced by an inline SVG (24 px view box, drawn with `currentColor`); `css/app.css`: `.tabs a svg`, gold stroke on the open tab. No script, no external file (CSP unchanged).
- `?v=` of the page assets brought to the client version (Service 0.7.4, PM 1.0.15) — they had stayed one version behind in §457, so a browser could keep the older script.
- Cybersecurity: markup and style only.

## §459 Newsletters — import an issue from a block sheet (GAS-CP-R97-175, v1.211.0)

- `customer/app/nl.php`: `nl_md_parse()` (block sheet → head and blocks; no storage), `nl_import_from_post()` (checks, then `nl_create()` + `nl_image_store()` per picture + one `UPDATE`; a failure while storing deletes the half-made draft), `nl_md_sample()` / `nl_md_sample_send()`; action `nl_import` in `nl_handle_action()` (rights `nl_can_dept()` of the department posted).
- `customer/views/newsletters.php`, `customer/views/hr_newsletters.php`: card `#import` (folded) with the block sheet, a folder input (`picdir[]`, `webkitdirectory`) and a files input (`pics[]`); `?sample=1` downloads the example sheet.
- Block sheet: head between two `---` lines (`title`, `subject`, `preheader`, `lang` en / el / it). Body: `# …` heading (`h`) · paragraphs (`p`; wrapped lines joined, list lines kept, `**bold**`) · `![description](file)` picture (`img`; `[![description](file)](https://…)` adds a link) · a line that is only `[label](https://…)` button (`btn`) · `---` divider (`hr`) · `> …` lines one highlight (`card`: `### title`, text, optional picture and button). `<!-- … -->` lines are skipped. A picture is matched by file name, case ignored; the folder part of the path in the sheet is ignored.
- Limits: sheet 200 KB, UTF-8, `.md` / `.markdown` / `.txt`; 40 blocks (`NL_MAX_BLOCKS`); 20 pictures (`NL_IMPORT_MAX_PICS`, also the usual `max_file_uploads` of PHP — a folder with more files than that is cut by the server, so keep only the pictures of the issue in the folder); pictures as in the editor (JPEG / PNG / GIF by content, 2 MB).
- No database step. Language keys `nl.imp_*` (16) in the five files. Help: manuals golden / pm / staff.
- Cybersecurity: staff of the department only, POST + CSRF; the sheet is read as plain text and stored as block text — it is escaped when the message is rendered, so markup in the sheet is printed, not executed; links pass `nl_url_ok()` (http / https only); pictures are checked by content and stored outside the web root under a random name (the uploaded file name is used only to match, never as a path); size and count limits before anything is stored; the result is a draft under the unchanged approval and sending rules; nothing is fetched from an address given in the sheet (no outbound request). Performance: one page request, no effect on other pages.
- Proposal (open): reject a picture whose pixel dimensions are unreasonably large (for example above 6 000 px on a side) in `nl_image_store()`, for the editor and the import alike.

## §460 Newsletters — issues published on the website (GAS-CP-R97-176, v1.212.0; website R18)

- Database: `install/migrate_cp_r97_176_nl_web.sql` — `nl_issues.web_publish`, `web_slug`, `web_at`; check `r99d`.
- `customer/app/nl.php`: `nl_web_has()`, `nl_slug()`, `nl_slug_free()`, `nl_web_toggle()` (action `nl_web`, `nl_can_send()`), `nl_pub_news()`; `nl_save_from_post()` stores the tick and the slug of a sales issue of Golden A/S; `nl_queue()` stamps `web_at` when sending starts; `nl_hash()` adds the slug when the tick is set (issues without it keep their fingerprint, so approvals given before stay valid); `nl_md_parse()` / `nl_import_from_post()` read `web` and `slug` from the head of a block sheet.
- `customer/web_feed.php`: `GET ?r=news` (list: slug, title, summary, date, lang, first picture) and `GET ?r=news&a=<slug>` (the same plus blocks) — site key as for every route; the KYMA key gets an empty list. Published = `web_publish = 1`, `web_at` set, slug set, status sending / sent, department sales, site golden.
- `customer/views/newsletter.php`: field «Website» under the preview line; button 🌐 Publish on / Remove from the website on a sending / sent issue.
- Website (repository goldenas-website): `api/news.php` asks the feed with the site key and keeps the answer 10 minutes (the last good answer is served if the portal does not reply); `news.js` draws the list on `news.html`, the article at `news.html?a=<slug>` and the latest-news blocks of the home page and the Newsletter page; hand-built articles are listed in the page itself and win over a portal issue with the same slug.
- Import card (`newsletters.php`, `hr_newsletters.php`): the eight-step procedure «How to import a newsletter prepared by the AI assistant» (`nl.imp_how`, `nl.imp_s1` … `nl.imp_s8`); on the HR page, and before the database step, step 7 leaves out the sentence about the website.
- Language keys `nl.web*` (8) and `nl.imp_how` / `nl.imp_s*` (9) in the five files. Help: manuals golden / pm / staff.
- Cybersecurity: the feed returns only content already sent to readers (subject, preview line, blocks, picture addresses by random token) — no recipients, lists, statistics or drafts; slug validated on both sides (`[a-z0-9-]`, 80); site key compared in constant time and rate limit unchanged; the website never passes a browser's request to the portal unvalidated and draws the article with text nodes only (no markup from the feed is executed), links limited to http / https, pictures limited to the portal's picture address; publishing is covered by the approval fingerprint; taking an issue off the website is immediate in the portal and reaches the website within the cache time. Performance: the website calls the feed at most once per 10 minutes per list / article; portal pages unaffected.
- Limits: an article drawn from blocks is one column (no rows of pictures, no diagrams); it is drawn in the browser, so search engines see it later than a hand-built page and it is not in the sitemap; the website has no Italian text for it beyond the fixed labels.

## §461 Contacts import — category-only update (GAS-CP-R97-177, v1.212.1)

- `ct_import_categories()` in `app/ct.php`, reached from the Contacts card of Settings › Import with the tick box `cat_only` (action `ct_import`). Header row: any column whose title contains "mail" plus a column "Category"; a "Person"/"Name" column is used only for the messages.
- Per row: contact found by e-mail → `UPDATE contacts SET category` when the category differs; nothing else is written, no contact or organisation is created. A repeated e-mail counts once. Empty category = no change. A category `ct_norm_category()` does not know = no change, listed. E-mail not in the portal = listed, not created.
- Result: `contacts_upd`, `contacts_same`, the list of changes ("Name: old → new") and the list of rows left out. Preview runs inside a rolled-back transaction.
- A real run writes `storage/private/ct_category_<date>_<time>.json` (contact id, previous category, new category, user id) for a reversal; the folder is not served by the web server.
- New aliases in `ct_norm_category()`: "Ownner Representative", "Owner Rep.", "Technical Staff", "Technical Department".
- Language keys `ct.im_cat_only`, `ct.im_cat_change`, `ct.im_cat_unknown`, `ct.im_cat_notfound` in en, el, it, bg, uk (12 643 keys each).
- Cybersecurity: staff only, CSRF as the other imports, parameterised queries; the contact list is uploaded in the portal and is not stored in the repository.

## §462 Contacts import, category only — reversal record written (GAS-CP-R97-178, v1.212.2)

- `ct_import_categories()` used the constant `CP_ROOT`, which only the API defines; in the web portal the real run committed the update and then threw "Undefined constant" before the reversal file and the result page. Path now `dirname(__DIR__) . '/storage/private'`.
- The first real run (21/11/2026, list of 1 091 rows, about 1 055 contacts) therefore has no reversal record.
- Lesson: the local test defined CP_ROOT itself, so it could not catch this; test web-side functions without API constants.

## §463 Mobile apps released on the App Store — links and QR codes (GAS-CP-R97-179, v1.212.3)

- All four iOS apps released on 21/11/2026: Service 0.7.3, Customer 1.0.8, PM 1.0.11, HR 1.1.0 (App Store ids 6829433629, 6829433786, 6829433663, 6837033425).
- `cp_store_apps()`: `ios_live` true for all; Golden HR has an `ios` link. `store_apps_html()`: an app without Google Play but with the App Store shows the web app in the first tile and the App Store in the second.
- `assets/apps/qr-hr-ios.png` new; `icon-service|pm|customer.png` redrawn with the name tab.
- Cybersecurity: fixed https links to apps.apple.com with `rel="noopener"`; no script.

## §464 Golden A/S Employee 1.0.0 — My HR for every employee (GAS-CP-R97-180, v1.213.0)

- New web shell `customer/mobile-employee/` (bundle `gr.goldenas.employee`, X-App `employee`), built from the Service shell: `i18n.js`, `db.js`, `api.js`, `sync.js`, `hr.js`, `upd.js` taken over; `screens.js` reduced to sign-in, lock and More; `app.js` with four routes (login, lock, hr, more) — any other address goes to My HR. No reports, tickets, hours, parts or assistant code is shipped. Own storage keys (`gem.*`, IndexedDB `gas-employee`) because the web apps share one origin.
- The read model is `GET /me` and `GET /hr` only (`sync.js` `pull()`).
- API: `API_APPS['employee']`; `api_act_as()` lets HR-only accounts in as well; `index.php` answers the employee app only on `/me`, `/auth/logout`, `/devices/push-token`, `/me/passkeys*`, `/sync`, `/hr`, `/hr/request`, `/hr/request/doc`, `/hr/doc/<id>` — everything else 403; `endpoints/sync.php` accepts from it only the kinds `hr_request`, `hr_cancel`, `hr_period_join`, `hr_training_apply`, `hr_eval_ack`, `hr_message`, `hr_seen`. No database change (`mob_devices.app_id` is free text).
- Demonstration account (§453): as Service — the app switches to its built-in data and the portal gives nothing.
- Portal: `cp_store_apps()['employee']` (web app tile with QR until the stores release it), shown first for staff on the home and sign-in pages; `assets/apps/icon-employee.png`, `qr-employee-web.png`; key `apps.d.employee` in five languages (12 644 keys).
- Native: `mobile-native/apps.json`, association file, `store/employee/` (listing, review notes, six screenshots 1320 × 2868), workflows `build-ios`, `build-android`, `asc-release` take `employee`; iOS needs the variable `IOS_PROFILE_EMPLOYEE`.
- Service app 0.7.5: `hr.js` — the leave form called `classList` on `#rreq`, an element removed in R77-007 (v1.87.0); the error stopped the wiring of Send request, Join, Cancel, document links and Apply on the Leave tab. Guarded; the same file serves the Employee app. The name of a real-looking person in the demonstration data was replaced.
- Tested in a browser on the demonstration data: sign-in, four My HR tabs, a leave request sent (Requests 3 → 4), More, sign-out, unknown address → My HR, no script error. Not tested against the live API with a real account.
- Cybersecurity: least data — a stolen Employee token cannot read tickets, reports, projects or prices; the token is bound to the app id (`Token issued for another application`).

## §465 In / Out — attendance with time and position (GAS-CP-R97-182, v1.214.0)

- Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_182_inout.sql` (check `r99d`): `io_sites` (name, lat, lng, radius, network addresses), `io_entries` (one row per punch: UTC time, offset of the phone, local day and time, position, accuracy, nearest site, distance, source app, address, flags, status, decision), `io_overtime` (decision per person and day).
- `app/io.php`: `io_punch()` — flags `outside` (distance beyond the radius plus the accuracy, at most 100 m), `late` / `early` against the person's work calendar (`wc_user_calendar`, `wc_day`) with `io_tolerance_min`, `weakgps`, `nogps`, `noin`, `double`, `offday`; exceptions make the status `pending`, otherwise `auto`. A punch is judged at its own time (`io_state($uid, $at)`), so one sent later from the outbox gets the right flags; the same `uuid` is stored once. `io_day()` pairs In → Out (rejected punches left out), takes the break off a day of five hours or more, computes overtime and compares with `timesheet_lines` and `fsr_lines` (WT). `io_stats()`, `io_board()`, `io_overtime_days()`.
- Rights: decide — HR, management, administrators, project managers (all); a manager for the own people (`hr_manager_of`). Sites and rules — HR, management, administrators. Statistics — `hr_can()` or `mgmt_can()` only (HR, owner / CEO, General Manager); the tab does not exist for anyone else and `io_stats()` refuses.
- Portal punch (`io_punch_portal`): only when `io_client_ip()` (X-Sucuri-ClientIP, else the forwarded address) matches an address of a site; the position stored is the site's.
- Page `views/inout.php` (menu Planning; also in the HR-only and employee-only menus), actions `app/io_actions.php`. 107 language keys × 5 languages (12 751 keys each).
- API: `GET /inout`, `POST /inout/punch`, sync kind `io_punch`; refused for the customer app; allowed for the Employee app. The source app and the address are set by the server, not by the phone.
- Apps: `js/io.js` in Employee 1.1.0 (tab In / Out) and Service 0.8.0 (More › In / Out) — one `getCurrentPosition` per punch (high accuracy, 15 s), nearest site shown, offline outbox, demonstration data. Employee `.htaccess`: `geolocation=(self)`.
- Native: `NSLocationWhenInUseUsageDescription` (build-ios), `ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION` / `ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION` (build-android, not for the customer app). The store apps show the permission prompt only after a new build.
- Tested: 25 checks of the rules against a MariaDB test database (time zones, flags, uuid, overtime, cross-check, rights, network address); every tab of the page rendered on test data; both apps in a browser with and without a position. Not tested on the live portal or on a phone.
- Cybersecurity: position is personal data — one fix per punch, no background tracking; statistics limited to three roles; map links are plain https links with `rel="noopener noreferrer"`; all queries parameterised, CSRF on every action; the portal punch cannot be made from an unregistered network. Open: the origin server should accept requests only from the firewall, otherwise a forwarded address could be forged; a written notice to staff and a retention period for positions should be agreed with the DPO.
- Not yet: the same screen in Golden PM and Golden HR; approved leave taken off the working days in the statistics; an automatic close of a day without Out.

## §466 In / Out — online acceptance, written to the HR log (GAS-CP-R97-183, v1.215.0)

- Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_183_inout_consent.sql` (check `r99e`): `io_consents` (user, version of the notice, language shown, source, address, device, time in UTC; one row per user and version).
- `app/io.php`: `IO_CONSENT_VER` ('1'), `io_consent()`, `io_consent_ok()`, `io_consent_accept()` — stores the row and calls `hr_audit($pid, 'consent', …)` so the acceptance appears in HR card › Changes (new kind `hr.ck_consent`); `io_punch()` refuses without it. `io_consents_all()` for the list under Sites & rules. Raising `IO_CONSENT_VER` asks everybody again.
- Portal: My In / Out shows the notice with a tick box (action `io_consent`) instead of the In / Out button until accepted.
- API: `GET /inout` carries `consent {accepted, version, at, title, text, accept}` in the language of the app; `POST /inout/consent`; `POST /inout/punch` answers 409 `{consent: true}` without it; sync kinds `io_consent` and `io_punch` (the acceptance made without signal is sent first).
- Apps: Employee 1.1.1 and Service 0.8.1 show the notice before the first punch (`js/io.js`); the demonstration data starts not accepted, so the flow can be shown.
- 15 language keys × 5 languages (12 766 keys each). The notice text is `io.c_text`.
- Tested: 29 checks on the test database (no punch and nothing stored before acceptance, one row per person, HR log entry, API block), the portal page with and without acceptance, both apps in a browser.
- Cybersecurity / data protection: the acceptance is evidence that the notice was shown and accepted; it records address and device. Whether acceptance is the right legal basis for employees is for the DPO / lawyer — the notice can be reworded in the language files without code changes.

## §467 Working hours — several calendars per branch, per department and per person (GAS-CP-R97-184, v1.216.0)

- Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_184_calendar_assign.sql` (check `r99f`): `work_calendars.holidays_from`, `.branch_id`, `.note`; `hr_departments.work_calendar`.
- `wc_user_calendar_src($uid)` (and `wc_user_calendar`): the person's own calendar (`customer_users.work_calendar`), else the department's (`hr_people.user_id` → `hr_departments.work_calendar`), else the branch's (`customer_users.branch_id` → `branches.work_calendar`, a field that existed but was not read), else GR. Returns the source for display.
- `wc_holiday()` reads the holidays of `wc_holiday_code($code)` — the calendar named in `holidays_from` (one level; the country is taken from it).
- `wc_calendar_save()`: holidays from, hours copied from an existing calendar, branch, note. `wc_assign_save()` (action `wc_assign_save`, administrators): calendar per department, option to clear the personal calendar of its people, calendar per person. `wc_departments()`, `wc_people()`.
- `views/admin_calendars.php`: new fields on + Add calendar; notice on the holidays card of a calendar that shares holidays; card "Who follows which calendar". `views/admin_users.php`: the calendar select has the option "department / branch" and no longer writes GR to an account that had none when its row is saved.
- `app/acc.php`: report hours use the inherited calendar.
- 23 language keys × 5 languages (12 789 keys each).
- Tested on the test database: 17 checks — default, branch, department over branch, own over department, "apply to all", holiday of the parent calendar, and In / Out late / early for an office calendar and a site calendar at the same clock times; the page rendered with and without the new-calendar form.
- Behaviour change: an account without a calendar whose branch has one now follows the branch (before: GR).
- Cybersecurity: administrators only, CSRF, parameterised queries; no new data leaves the portal.

## §468 In / Out — tables created (GAS-CP-R97-185, v1.216.1)

- `cp_migration_state()` had two entries with the key `r99d` (newsletters `web_slug`, and In / Out `io_entries`). In a PHP array the later one wins, so the check of R97-182 was always true and `migrate_cp_r97_182_inout.sql` was never run: `io_entries`, `io_sites`, `io_overtime` did not exist, `io_has()` was false, the menu entry was hidden and `GET /inout` answered `enabled: false`. (`io_consents`, key `r99e`, was created.)
- The check of R97-182 is now `r99g`. The self-check line uses the same name.
- Lesson: take the migration key from the highest key present in `version.php` at commit time (another session also adds keys), and after a deploy read the "migrations" step of the updater report for the file name.

## §469 In / Out in Golden PM and Golden HR (GAS-CP-R97-186, v1.217.0)

- `customer/mobile-pm/js/io.js` and `customer/mobile-hr/js/io.js` are the same module as in the Service and Employee apps (route `#/io`, card under More, 39 keys `io.*` in five languages, styles `.io-*`). Client versions: Golden PM 1.0.16, Golden HR 1.2.0.
- API: the Golden HR whitelist in `api/v1/index.php` now includes `/inout`, `/inout/punch`, `/inout/consent`. Golden PM had no path whitelist. The source of an entry is the app id (`pm`, `hr`).
- Demonstration accounts: Golden PM answers from the data set built into the app; Golden HR is answered by `PortalHRDemo::io()` / `ioPunch()` (two invented sites, entries kept in the demonstration file only, at most 40, coordinates clamped). Nothing of a demonstration account reaches `io_entries` or the HR log.
- Golden A/S Customer has no In / Out: `/inout*` answers 403 "Staff only" for customer accounts (unchanged).
- Security review: no new table, no new right; the new routes for the HR app go through the same consent check, rate limit and staff-only check as the other apps; values of the demonstration punch are cast and bounded before they are stored or returned.

## §470 Golden A/S Employee on Google Play — link and QR code (GAS-CP-R97-187, v1.217.1)

- `cp_store_apps()` in `customer/app/helpers.php`: `employee` now has `play` (package `gr.goldenas.employee`) and `ios` (App Store id 6837805575) with `ios_live => false`. The card therefore shows the Google Play tile and the App Store tile as coming soon. When Apple releases the app, set `ios_live => true`.
- New images `assets/apps/qr-employee-play.png` and `assets/apps/qr-employee-ios.png` (296 px, same colours as the others). `qr-employee-web.png` stays for the web app link.
- Security review: static links and images only; no new input, route or right.

## §471 Manual — the In / Out procedure (GAS-CP-R97-188, v1.217.2)

- New section `inout` in `views/_manual_golden.php` (employee steps and manager steps), `views/_manual_pm.php` (same, under the approvals) and `views/_manual_staff.php` (employee steps only).
- The text follows the code in `app/io.php`: remarks and statuses from `io_punch()`, worked time / break / overtime from `io_day()`, the cross-check from `io_booked()` (timesheet lines of the date, all hour types; field service report lines of type WT of the reports the person created for the date) and the statistics from `io_stats()`.
- No program change. Security review: text only.

## §472 In / Out — the process as a flow, and the figures in the evaluation (GAS-CP-R97-189, v1.218.0)

- New tab `flow` on `?p=inout` for every member of staff: `views/_inout_flow.php` draws nine steps as an inline SVG (centre line, two symmetric branches at steps 3 and 6, orthogonal connectors; box texts are wrapped HTML in `foreignObject` so every language fits) and lists the steps in words. The minutes in the text come from `io_tol()`, `io_ot_min()` and `io_diff_min()`.
- `app/io.php`: the per-person calculation of `io_stats()` moved to `io_stats_calc()` (same figures). New `io_year_figures($uid, $year)` (working days counted from the person's first entry of the year to today) and `io_year_text()`.
- Evaluation: `views/_hr_eval.php` shows the line under the computed criterion `attendance_auto`; `views/my_leave.php` shows the employee's own line under "How we evaluate you". The score of `hr_eval_attendance_auto()` is unchanged; the figures are not passed to the AI draft context.
- 43 language keys `io.t_flow`, `io.fl_*`, `io.ev_txt` in en, el, it, bg, uk (12 832 keys each).
- Security review: no new input, route or write. The year figures are visible only where the evaluation of that person is already visible (evaluator, HR, the person), which matches the notice the staff accepted. The style block carries the CSP nonce; no script.
- Open: approved leave is not yet deducted from the working days of the consistency figure.

## §473 Recycle bin, delete one / all, supporting documents on system budgets (GAS-CP-R97-190, v1.219.0)

- Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_190_file_trash.sql` (check key `r99h`): tables `file_trash` and `sb_files`.
- `app/ft.php`: `ft_trash_row()` copies a record to `file_trash` (row as JSON, foreign-key references found through `information_schema`, absolute path, label, context, reason) and deletes the record; the bytes stay. `ft_restore()` re-inserts the record under the same id and links the references again. `ft_purge_row()` unlinks the bytes only inside `cp_storage()` or `customer/storage` (`ft_inside()`), and clears the row copy. `ft_daily()` (called from the daily job in `app/cache.php`) purges entries older than `FT_DAYS` = 30.
- Rights in one place, `ft_info($tbl, $row)`: administrators always; otherwise the uploader, the project manager (`projects.pm_user_id`), for `offer_att` of a draft offer also `offers.created_by`; budget documents: uploader, author of the budget, management. Protected (administrator + reason of at least five characters): kinds `issued`, `offer`, `fsr_doc`, `fsr_sign`, `isr_doc`, `ga`; `offer_att` of an offer that is not `draft`; `fsr_photo` / `isr_file` of a report that is not `draft`; `si_docs.kind = invoice`. Customers never.
- Buttons `ft_btn($tbl, $row)` and `ft_all_btn($tbl, $rows)` post `ft_delete` / `ft_delete_all` (CSRF field, confirm dialog); the return address is the current query string, checked by `ft_back_ok()` (must start with `index.php`, no control characters). Protected files go through `?p=file_remove`.
- Generic tables: `sp_files`, `sb_files`, `ticket_files`, `cp_request_files`, `kb_files`, `si_docs`. `ism_files` passes through `ft_trash_row()` with its own right (`ism_can_reg`). `cp_trash_file()` replaces `cp_delete_file()` where a user removes a file (offer attachment, note photo, report photo or file on the form, `file_delete`); `cp_delete_file()` stays for regenerated PDFs and for files that go with a deleted record.
- Views: `views/admin_trash.php` (Settings › System › Recycle bin), `views/file_remove.php`; buttons in `views/subproject.php` (files table, was administrators only), `views/drawings.php`, `views/offer.php`, `views/ticket.php`, `views/payable.php`, `kb_files_html()`, `cr_files_html()`.
- System budgets: `sb_files_add()`, `sb_files()`, `sb_stream_file()` (`?p=sbfile`, staff with budget access, `nosniff`, PDF sandboxed), `sb_files_to_offer()` called after `sb_convert()` with the ticked `files[]` and `files_vis`. `sb_delete()` removes the documents with the budget.
- `ar_verify()` treats files waiting in the bin as known and skips `_cr/`, `sb/`, `si/`.
- Not in the bin by decision of this release: `hr_docs`, `hr_flow_docs` (personal data, destroyed at once as before).
- 84 language keys `ft.*`, `sb.doc_*`, `sb.ev_file_*` in en, el, it, bg, uk (12 916 keys each).
- Security review: every action is a POST with the CSRF token; table names come from a fixed list and are checked against `^[a-z0-9_]+$`; no path from the request is used; purge refuses paths outside the storage; restore and purge are administrators only; uploads of budget documents check extension, size and, for pictures and PDF, the real content type; downloads send `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`.

## §474 Newsletters on the website — delay shortened (GAS-CP-R97-191, v1.219.1)

- Report: "the button to publish on the website did not work". Finding: the portal had published the issue (`web_feed.php?r=news` listed it); the website kept its previous answer — `api/news.php` asked the portal once per 10 minutes and sent `Cache-Control: max-age=300`, which the host cache also honours, so the News page could stay unchanged for about 15 minutes.
- Website (goldenas-website): `NEWS_TTL` 600 → 120 s, `max-age` 300 → 60 s. The portal feed limit (10 GET per address per minute) is not reached: one list and one article call per two minutes each.
- Portal: text `nl.web_done_on` in five languages now states the delay. No program change.
- Security review: no new input or route; the shorter cache adds at most a few requests per minute to the feed, inside its limit.

## §475 Recycle bin for personnel documents (HR only); Delete all on tickets, requests, knowledge base (GAS-CP-R97-192, v1.220.0)

- `hr_doc_delete()` (`app/hr3.php`) and `hra_doc_delete()` (`app/hra.php`) pass through `ft_trash_row()` (tables `hr_docs`, `hr_flow_docs`; bytes stay under `storage/hr`). Their own rights are unchanged.
- `app/ft.php`: `FT_HR_TABLES`, `ft_hr()` (`hr_can()` and `hr_files_can()`), `ft_scope()` filters `ft_list()`, `ft_open_count()` and `ft_empty`; `ft_entry_can()` guards `ft_restore()` and `ft_purge()` — HR entries for HR only, all others for administrators only.
- `views/admin_trash.php` opens for administrators and HR (`ft_bin_can()`); `admin_trash` added to `HR_PAGES`, the `ft_*` actions to `HR_ACTIONS`; link on `views/hr_person.php` (Documents tab).
- `ft_all_btn()` on `views/ticket.php` (all files of the ticket), `views/request.php`, `views/kb_q.php` (question and answers). Not on payables (decision).
- Evaluation: no change — the computed score of Attendance & consistency stays as it is (decision).
- Language keys `ft.tb_hr_docs`, `ft.tb_hr_flow_docs`, `ft.rule_5`, `ft.hr_link` × 5 (12 920 each).
- Security review: personnel documents are never listed to administrators; the daily purge still destroys them after 30 days; no new input.

## §476 Evaluation — In / Out consistency in the computed grade (GAS-CP-R97-193, v1.221.0)

- `app/io.php`: `io_year_figures($uid, $year, $pid)` now returns `excused` (working days inside an approved day-type `hr_requests` row without an entry, `io_excused_days()`), `expected` = working days from the first entry of the year to today − excused, `consistency` on the expected days, `scored` (expected ≥ `IO_EV_MIN_DAYS` = 20) and `pen` = `min(3, ceil((io_ev_ok() − consistency) / 10))` when below `io_ev_ok()` (setting `io_ev_ok`, default 90, 50–100, saved by `io_settings_save()`; field on Sites & rules).
- `app/hr7.php` `hr_eval_attendance_auto()`: adds `pen` to the existing deductions and returns the figures as `io`; score still `max(1, 5 − deductions)`. `hr_evaluation_save_from_post()` stores the computed score as before, so an evaluation saved earlier keeps its stored grade until it is saved again; the form always shows the live figure.
- Views `_hr_eval.php` and `my_leave.php` read `$a['io']` (one calculation). Texts: `io.ev_txt` (with the result), `io.ev_s_*`, `io.r_ev`, `io.fl_9s`, `io.fl_p9`, `io.fl_ev_p1..3`, `hr.ecd_attendance_auto` (rule added) in five languages (12 924 keys each).
- The statistics tab (`io_stats()`) is unchanged: its consistency still uses all working days of the period.
- Security review: no new input except the bounded setting; figures visible where the evaluation is visible.
- Notice: the acceptance text already states that In / Out is taken into account in the evaluation (consistency and accuracy); accuracy is shown, not scored.

## §477 Wellness (GAS-CP-R97-194, v1.222.0)

- Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_194_wellness.sql` (check key `r99i`): `wl_prefs` (user, JSON), `wl_mood` (day, mood — no person, no time), `wl_mood_seen` (user, day — only "has answered", purged after one day by `wl_daily()`).
- `app/wl.php`: `wl_prefs()` / `wl_clean()` / `wl_save()` (fixed key list, bounded values), `wl_today()` (working day and hours from the person's calendar, approved day-type leave, In / Out state of the day for people with the In / Out consent), `wl_state()` (cached 60 s in the session), `wl_mood_save()` (one transaction: seen row first, so a second answer fails), `wl_mood_trend()` (HR / management; weeks with fewer than `WL_MOOD_MIN` = 5 answers have no average), `wl_head_html()` (button), `wl_foot_html()` (data as `application/json`, stylesheet, script with nonce), `wl_handle_action()` (`wl_save` form, `wl_set` and `wl_mood` answered as JSON).
- `assets/wellness.js` (about 14 KB, deferred, no library): panel, sounds generated with the Web Audio API (noise buffers through filters and slow oscillators — no audio files), focus timer, breathing overlay, stretches, reminder engine. Work time is counted in `localStorage` (`gas.wl.<user>`), one tab counts at a time; state refreshed from `?p=wl_state` every five minutes. `assets/wellness.css`.
- Rules of the reminders: break after `break_min` minutes of activity (reset by five idle minutes), eyes every 20 min, water every 90 min, posture every 60 min, end of day once at end + 20 min, In reminder from start − 5 min to start + 90 min when there is no In, Out reminder from the end when the last entry is an In, «How was today?» from end − 90 min. Nothing about hours, In / Out or the day is shown when the day is not a working day or the person is on approved leave.
- Page `?p=wellness` (staff; in `HR_PAGES`, `EMP_PAGES`, `PA_PAGES`, actions in the matching lists).
- 100 language keys `wl.*` × 5 (13 024 each). Handbooks golden / pm / staff: new section Wellness.
- Not done: push to phones. The apps register a push token, but the server has no sender, no notification keys (Firebase service account, Apple APNs key) and no clock job; see the summary for the steps.
- Security review: posts carry the CSRF token; settings limited to known keys and ranges; the page data is a JSON block (no inline script), script tag with nonce; texts are written as text nodes; the anonymous answer cannot be joined to a person in the database; no personal data leaves the browser for the counters. Performance: one cached state per minute and user (four small queries), script deferred.

## §478 Golden A/S Employee on the App Store (GAS-CP-R97-195, v1.222.1)

- Employee 1.1.0 released on 22/11/2026 (availability set to all 175 countries, then "Release This Version"); Customer 1.0.12 released the same evening. `https://apps.apple.com/app/id6837805575` answers.
- `cp_store_apps()`: `ios_live => true` for `employee`; `assets/apps/qr-employee-ios.png` already encoded that address (checked by decoding).
- Website `apps.html`: the "Coming soon" tile of the Employee card is now the App Store link.
- Security review: no program change.

## §479 Push notifications — portal side (GAS-CP-R97-196, v1.223.0)

- `app/pn.php`. Queue: `pn_enqueue()` writes to the existing `mob_push_queue` for one app of the person (`pn_pick_app()`: customer → `customer`; staff → first of pm, engineer, employee, hr that has a device with a token; `$prefer` puts `employee` first for In / Out); skipped when the person has no token, has switched `push` off (`wl_prefs`) or the same `ref_key` is waiting or was sent in the last ten minutes.
- Sources: `nf_push()` calls `pn_from_nf()` for each new or renewed notification not older than `PN_FRESH_SEC` (2 h); route by kind for customers (`of` → `#/offers`, `oa` → `#/approvals`, `fsr` / `isr` → `#/sign`, `tk` → `#/tickets`, `drw` / `doc` / `cls` → `#/documents`). `pn_io_tick()` queues `io:in` (20 minutes from the start, no In) and `io:out` (20 minutes from the end, In open) once a day per person, from `wl_today()` — not on days off or approved day-type leave, only with the In / Out consent and `io_remind` on, text in `customer_users.locale` (`pn_t()`).
- Sending: `pn_flush()` under `GET_LOCK`; Android `pn_send()` → FCM HTTP v1 (`pn_fcm_token()`: RS256 assertion from the service-account key, access token cached 50 min); iOS → `api.push.apple.com` over HTTP/2 with `pn_apns_jwt()` (ES256, DER signature converted to R‖S, cached 50 min), topic = bundle id from `PN_APPS`. A row is tried once; the reason of a failure is stored in `error`; rows older than six hours expire; a token reported dead (`UNREGISTERED`, 404, 410, `BadDeviceToken`) is cleared from `mob_devices`.
- Keys: uploaded on `?p=admin_push` (administrators), validated for shape (`type = service_account` with a usable RSA key; EC private key), stored as `storage/keys/fcm-service-account.json` and `storage/keys/apns-key.p8` (0600, folder with deny rule); short-lived tokens in `storage/keys/cache-*.json`; only Key ID, Team ID and the Firebase project id are settings. Nothing of the keys is in the database, a log or the repository.
- Clock job: `customer/tick.php` (command line only, 403 through the web) loads the modules through `index.php` (which returns at `CP_TICK` before the session), runs `nf_sweep(60, true)` and `pn_tick()`. Cron line on the settings page: every five minutes. Without it `nf_sweep_detached()` runs `pn_tick()` after a page view, at most once a minute.
- Personal switch `push` in `wl_prefs` (Wellness › Settings). 63 language keys (13 087 each). Handbooks golden (new section Push notifications), pm, staff, customer.
- Still to do: Firebase project and keys (owner), cron job (owner), Android builds with `FIREBASE_GOOGLE_SERVICES_B64`, iOS builds with the push entitlement and the registration hooks, store submissions, end-to-end test.
- Security review: actions are administrator-only POSTs with CSRF; uploads limited in size and checked; key files outside the web root rules (deny), never echoed; device token format checked before the call to Apple; push text limited to title and body of the notification; `tick.php` refuses web requests. Performance: enqueue is two small queries per notification; sending happens after the response or in the clock job.


## §480 Push notifications — app side and store builds (GAS-CP-R97-197, v1.224.0)

- Registration lives in the app pages, not in the shell: `js/push.js` (Service, PM, HR, Employee) and `lib/push.js` (Customer), identical files, listed in each `sw.js`. The store shells load the pages from the portal (`server.url`), so the old `native-bridge.js` listener never ran there; it is removed from the bridge.
- `push.js` runs only in a native shell whose user agent carries `GASPush/1` and that has the `PushNotifications` plug-in. Signed in → `requestPermissions()` once → `register()`; the `registration` event gives the token, sent with `POST /devices/push-token` (`apns` on iOS, `fcm` on Android); retried every 20 s and on return to the foreground until accepted; sent again after a new sign-in. A tap (`pushNotificationActionPerformed`) sets `location.hash` to `data.route` only when it matches `^#/[\w/?=&-]*$`.
- The mark `GASPush/1` is `appendUserAgent` in the native copy of `capacitor.config.json`, written by the build only when push is really configured: Android — `FIREBASE_GOOGLE_SERVICES_B64` present and `google-services.json` holds the application id (otherwise the build stops); iOS — the downloaded provisioning profile carries `aps-environment`.
- iOS (`build-ios.yml`, step "Push notifications"): `mobile-native/ios_push.py` writes `App/App.entitlements` (`aps-environment = production`), adds the two `AppDelegate` callbacks that hand the device token to the plug-in, and the mark; the archive step sets `CODE_SIGN_ENTITLEMENTS` and checks the signed app for the entitlement. A profile without push gives a warning and a build without push (summary line "push NO").
- `asc_bundle.py` (workflow "Apple bundle id and profile"): always enables `PUSH_NOTIFICATIONS` on the bundle id, then looks at the App Store profiles of that bundle id whatever their names; a profile that is not active or does not carry `aps-environment` is deleted and made again under the same name.
- API: `api_push_token()` in `api/v1/bootstrap.php` (both routes). Token shape checked (Apple: 64–200 hex; Google: 20–255 URL-safe characters) → 422 otherwise; the same token is removed from every other device row before it is stored.
- `?p=admin_push`: `pn_last_tick` is stored in the company zone, so it is printed with `date()` and `tz_abbr()` (not `fmt_datetime()`, which converts from the database zone).
- App versions: Service 0.8.2, PM 1.0.17, HR 1.2.1, Employee 1.1.2, Customer 1.0.13.

## §481 Notification state shown in the apps (GAS-CP-R97-198, v1.224.1)

- `push.js` sets `window.PUSH` in every client: `PUSH.label()` = "store build <version> (<build>)" from the `App` plug-in (`getInfo()`), then "notifications: <state>". States: `after sign-in`, `asking permission`, `registering`, `sending token`, `on`, `not allowed …`, `not in this build …` (no `GASPush/1` mark), `plug-in missing`, `registration refused (<reason>)` (new `registrationError` listener), `token not accepted by the portal (<status>)`. Empty in a browser.
- Printed under the version: `set.version` row of Service, PM, HR and Employee; About of Customer. Text only, escaped; no token or identifier is shown.
- App versions: Service 0.8.3, PM 1.0.18, HR 1.2.2, Employee 1.1.3, Customer 1.0.14 (screens only; the store builds of R97-197 stay valid).

## §482 Planning notices by push (GAS-CP-R97-199, v1.225.0)

- `app/pn.php`: `pn_plan_tick()` is called by `pn_tick()` (clock job). Evening notice: from `PN_PLAN_HOUR` (18) to 22:00, once a day (`cp_settings.pn_plan_sent` = the date announced, written before sending) — `pn_plan_rows($tomorrow)` gives the entries of active staff without the kinds of `PN_PLAN_SKIP` (leave, sick, parental, strike); `pn_plan_text()` writes one line per assignment (vessel or project code + sub-project code · sub-project name — yard · note · car; status kinds by their `pl.k_*` name, `other` by its note) in the language of the person; queued as kind `plan:day`, ref `plan:<date>`.
- Change notice: `pn_plan_touch($uid, $date)` is called by `pl_assign()`, `pl_delete()`, `pl_delete_work()` and `pl_clear()`; it writes `cp_settings` key `pn_pd:<uid>:<date>` = time, only for today and tomorrow and not for one's own edit. The clock job takes marks older than two minutes: today → push; tomorrow → push only after the evening notice went out (earlier marks are dropped, the evening notice carries the final state); kind `plan:chg`. A day left empty gives "No assignment is planned"; a day that became an absence gives nothing.
- Not hooked: `pl_import()` and the automatic planning of work packages (`wp.php`) — their entries are in the evening notice, late changes made by them are not announced.
- Per person: `wl_prefs.plan` (default on), option on `?p=wellness`; the general `push` switch still applies in `pn_enqueue()`.
- `POST /auth/logout` (staff and customer routes) clears `mob_devices.push_token` of the device.
- Language keys (+8, 13 095 per language): `pn.pl_t`, `pn.plc_t`, `pn.plc_none`, `pn.pl_today`, `pn.pl_tomorrow`, `pn.pl_car`, `wl.o_plan`, `wl.d_plan`.

## §483 Versions in the stores: update band and announcement (GAS-CP-R97-200, v1.226.0)

- `app/sv.php`. `SV_APPS`: API app id → App Store id, Play package, name. `sv_refresh()` (called by `pn_tick()` only under the command line, i.e. the clock job; at most every `SV_EVERY` = 3 h, `cp_settings.sv_at` written first) asks `itunes.apple.com/lookup?id=…&country=gr` (`results[0].version`) and the public Play page of the package (pattern `[[["x.y.z"]]`); HTTPS only, no redirects, 10 s. A valid, not lower version replaces the stored one in `cp_settings.sv_versions` (JSON); no answer or an unreadable page changes nothing.
- `sv_announce()` runs when a stored version rises (never on the first reading): people with a non-revoked `mob_devices` row of that app and platform seen in the last 120 days, active, not demonstration; `nf_push()` kind `app:update`, ref `app:<app>:<platform>`, link to the dashboard; `cp_mail()` with the store link unless `cp_settings.sv_mail = 0`. Texts `sv.n_t`, `sv.n_b`, `sv.m_b` in the language of the person (`\n` in the text = line break).
- `GET /api/v1/store?platform=ios|android` — before sign-in, rate-limited per address (30 / minute), answers `{version, url}` of the calling app (`X-App`). Public facts only.
- Apps: `push.js` reads the installed build from the `App` plug-in, asks `/store`, and when the installed version is lower shows the band `#gas-upd` (text in en / el / it / bg / uk; Update = link to `apps.apple.com` or `play.google.com` only; Later = `localStorage gas.upd.later`, one day). `PUSH.label()` adds "version x is in the store". Screens versions: Service 0.8.4, PM 1.0.19, HR 1.2.3, Employee 1.1.4, Customer 1.0.15.
- `?p=admin_push`: card "Versions in the stores" (table, last asked, e-mail switch `sv_mail`, `sv_now`).
- API bootstrap now loads `wl`, `pn`, `sv`: `nf_push()` called inside an API request reaches `pn_from_nf()`, and the planning hooks work from the apps.
- Language keys +13 (13 108 per language).

## §484 Community: title list, posts in several languages, editing (GAS-CP-R97-201, v1.227.0)

- Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_201_soc_lang.sql` (check `r99j`): `soc_posts.lang` (language the post was written in, NULL for older posts) and `soc_post_tr (post_id, lang, title, body)`. Without it the page works as before, with the title list but no language fields (`soc_tr_has()`).
- `app/soc.php`: `soc_lang_from_post()` (a key of `LOCALES`, else the writer's language), `soc_tr_from_post()` (`tr[<lang>][title|body]`; unknown codes, the base language and versions without a title are dropped; 200 / 8000 characters), `soc_tr_save()`, `soc_tr_of()`, `soc_post_can_edit()` (writer, HR, administrators — the same rule as deleting), `soc_post_update()` (action `soc_post_edit`: title, text, language, other languages; kind, photo, video, poll untouched), `soc_notify_post()` (recipients grouped by `customer_users.locale`; one notification per person, same `ref_key`).
- `soc_feed()` adds `tr`, `vers` (language → [title, text], in the order of `LOCALES`, older posts under the key `''`) and `show` (the reader's language when the post has it, else the base).
- `views/community.php`: each post is `<details class="card soc-post" id="post<N>">`; the summary carries the title (one `<span data-soc-lang>` per language), writer, date, pills and counters; open at load when pinned, an open poll, or being edited. Script (nonce): `#post<N>` in the address opens that post (redirects after reacting, commenting, voting and notification links), `data-soc-all` opens / closes all, `data-soc-pick` switches the language inside one post. `?p=community&edit=<id>` turns the form into the editor.
- `soc_post_edit` and `soc_post_delete` added to the action lists of the scoped roles (`HR_ACTIONS`, `PA_ACTIONS`, `EMP_ACTIONS`) — until now a scoped employee could not delete an own post.
- Language keys +8 (13 116 per language). The mobile apps do not show Community.
